日本の[PS Store/PlayStation公式GTA VI page](https://www.playstation.com/ja-jp/games/grand-theft-auto-vi/)はStandard ¥9,800、Ultimate ¥12,280、予約特典GTA+の引換期限/解約/次回請求案内を掲載する。発売日の前日まではCERO審査予定表示だったが、10/17の[Game*Spark確認](https://www.gamespark.jp/article/2026/10/17/173739.html)後、Microsoft Store Japanも[CERO-Z/18才以上・暴力/犯罪・credit-card only](https://www.xbox.com/ja-JP/games/store/vi/9NL3WWNZLZZN)を表示し、PS Storeの法的情報もCERO Zを説明する。PS listingの同一ページ内にはなお一般の「CERO審査予定」credit-card noticeが残る一方、CERO Zの最終区分・対象・年齢制限も表示される箇所があり、ページ内の案内同士が過渡期状態を残すことも確認した。Rockstar Supportは日本regionのdownload product codeが発行日（2026-11-19 launch）から170日で期限切れになると説明する。PS listingは発売予定日を日本現地暦11/19と表示しつつ、metadata上のUTC release timestampは11/18になるため、日付だけで失効/購入期限を比較するとtimezone差を見落としうる。

Avocadoのmulti-channel SIM/eSIM/agent planでは、geographic price/tax/currency、legal age/content rating, actual eligibility, accepted payment method, code/service activation deadline, displayed local time and underlying UTC event timeを各々区別する候補。Country selection/localeから法的な居住地・年齢・販売資格を推定せず、確認が必要な条件を購入前に提示する。rating/statusが更新される場合、旧表示を放置せず、見出し/offer card/legal details/age/payment gateを同じsource revisionへ同期し、source date・last checked時点と未確認の条件を表示する。購入やprofile statusにより対象者外と判明した場合は、eligible alternate route/support/refund policyへ案内し、promoを期限切れ後も有効に見せない。

これは日本の2026年10月17日時点のGTA VI retailer/regional behaviorの記録で、CERO Zから日本版の表現差があるかどうか、全地域のconsumer/payment law、Avocado age/eligibility/payment policy、別販売経路の価格を確定しない。条件・価格は公開/発売前に変わる可能性がある。

## 43. 追加調査：fake early access/QR/code/downloadを公式security guidanceで解く（2026-10-17 / Security、AI06）

Rockstar [account protection support](https://support.rockstargames.com/articles/77PLWpN7ZfeLpLVLQ4l753/best-practices-for-protecting-your-rockstar-games-account)は、公式を装うphishing mail・social engineering・passwordを求めるthird-party serviceへの注意、domain/link検査、2-Step Verification、credential/QR/PIN/verification codeを共有しないこと、非公式ソフトを入れないことを明記する。GTA VIのpre-order FAQは購入先でPlayStation/Xbox/Rockstar Store/他retailerのsupport担当を分ける。[Kasperskyの2026-10-06 threat report](https://www.kaspersky.com/blog/gta-6-fake-leaks-malware-and-scams/56496/)はunreleased GTA VIを餌に、fake preorder/early-access/beta/key、QR/email collection、crypto payment、偽PC download/launcher、検索で上位に出るbait pageなどを実物調査したと報告し、公式source/domainの確認、未知download/codeや個人情報/支払いを避けるよう案内する。[Huntressの2026年9月分析](https://www.huntress.com/blog/fake-gta6-download-malware-analysis)もGTA VIの検索順位を狙うSEO poisoning、forum/social/torrentのfake ISOを確認し、分析時点で公式demoや実際に配布されたplayable leakは見つかっていないとする。両社は自身の観測したcampaignを報告するsecurity vendorであり、全GTA VI関連URLが危険という意味ではない。対象は発売前の詐欺とRockstar accountのルールに限定する。

Avocado security/SIM/eSIM/marketplace flowで取り入れる比較案は、(1)購入・activation linkのsource/providerを表示しofficial domainをallowlist化、(2)QRを通してもpassword/2FA/recovery code/Wallet seedをAvocadoのchat/request formへ貼らせない、(3)external checkoutに渡すdata fieldsとsupport ownerを事前に表示、(4)limited-time/“only N left”/early accessの根拠を示せないclaimをurgency badgeとして出さない、(5)unknown installer/sideload/credential promptをofficial activation methodとして見せない、(6)誤クリック後のrevocation/report/account recovery導線を設ける、(7)第三者offerは価格・返金・補償・権利付与の責任主体を示す。preview/third-party appの作者・publisher identityが確認できても、artifact安全性を自動で保証したことにはしない。

この調査は既存Security workstreamのleast-privilege/secret handling/verified source境界を支える候補であり、コード変更・新しい警告画面・契約/取引・URL allowlist・Wallet実行許可は作っていない。Rockstar/Kaspersky account/security guidanceをAvocado policyとして採用・保証したものでもない。

## 44. 追加調査：detailは背景へ隠さず段階的に開く（2026-10-17 / AI06、RQ14）

LOVE Magazine開発取材を引用する[GamesRadar+の記事（10/11）](https://www.gamesradar.com/games/grand-theft-auto/rockstar-has-a-whole-team-of-gta-6-writers-dedicated-to-pedestrian-dialogue-there-are-no-shortcuts-when-it-comes-to-building-a-world-like-this/)で、art director David Hobbinsとwriter Michael Wiafeが、通りの会話は走り過ぎるときは全体のtextureとして届き、立ち止まって注意を向けると個々の会話が納得できることを意図していると述べたと紹介する。同じインタビューでは“background/foreground”が観察者の注意により変わると語ったという。Bloomberg coverageはNPC文章/animation productionが負荷となり専任LA teamを設けたと伝える。いずれもdeveloper statementを報道・magazine interview経由で読むもので、Avocado usability evidenceではない。

Avocado AI06/Sky catalogに当てはめるなら、default viewに結論/実行状態/owner/費用/必要な次actionを短く出し、source, input/output, risk, exceptions, approval, recovery detailを本人の選択でその場から掘れるようにする仮説がある。低い注意状態でも誤解しない短いsummaryを置きつつ、「背景作業」扱いで権限/料金/期限/失敗理由を隠さない。user studyでは本人がdetailへ進む割合、必要情報を見つける時間、summaryのみでの誤判断、error/repair、cognitive loadを比較する。高品質の豊富なdefault contentにはreview/curation/update ownerのcapacityも見積に入れる。

これはGTA VIのdialogue design報道から生じたlayered-information仮説であり、全部のAvatar/NPC/Agent出力を詳細化する要求、外部Agentの無承認実行、Avocado UI設計の確定を意味しない。

## 45. 追加調査：体験上の機能をplatform/device capabilityごとに記述する（2026-10-17 / AI06、MAT18）

PlayStationの[GTA VI feature page](https://www.playstation.com/en-us/games/grand-theft-auto-vi/)はPS5/PS5 Proを対象にTempest 3D Audio、fast loading、DualSense haptic feedback・adaptive trigger・controller speaker、PS5のみの対応を説明し、[PlayStation Blog](https://blog.playstation.com/2026/06/24/grand-theft-auto-vi-plays-best-on-ps5-november-19)でもPS5単独の告知として具体化している。[限定版DualSense告知](https://blog.playstation.com/2026/09/03/first-look-at-the-grand-theft-auto-vi-limited-edition-dualsense-wireless-controllers/)はゲームの触覚応答とcontroller自体の色/texture/availabilityを別の商品情報として紹介する。PlayStation公式ページは発売前で国・店舗により在庫・発売時刻が異なる旨を明記し、feature FAQ内のage-rating answerは「未確認」の古い表示が残る一方、ESRB/日本console storeには後発ratingが掲載済み（§42）。ページ状態は単一の一括revisionでない。

Avocadoへの仮説: sensory cueや性能の説明は「app/runtime capability」「接続端末/OS capability」「周辺機器」「有効化・設定」「地域/availability」を分け、どの組合せで使えるかを明記する。触覚を喜ばせる演出だけでなく、任意でmute/adjust、visual/audio fallback、status meaning、acknowledgementを与え、視覚/音声/触覚の一経路だけにcritical noticeやauthorizationを依存させない。購入可能性や装着検出を機能の利用可能性、アクセシビリティ適合の保証と取り違えない。device-specific featuresを他platform parityへ拡張せず、fixtureやuser testでは機器能力/permission unavailable、feature disabled、degraded channel、reconnectionを別状態で確認する。

PS5側の[accessibility catalogue](https://www.playstation.com/en-us/accessibility/)と[system accessibility help](https://www.playstation.com/en-gb/support/hardware/ps5-accessibility-settings/)を追加確認すると、consoleにはzoom/color/text/mono audio/screen reader/captions/controller mapping/haptic intensity/chat transcription等のsystem affordanceがある一方、captionsの対象は動画、chat transcriptionは対応gameに限定され、Accessibility Tagsは開発者がStore上で支援機能を列挙する仕組みである。[controller settings](https://www.playstation.com/en-us/support/hardware/adjust-general-controller-settings/)からvibration/trigger intensityを変えられる。従ってsystem optionがあるだけで、各game contentがcaptioned/compatibleと判断できない。Avocado候補は自前のaccessibility feature inventoryを機能ごとに公開し、OS/device settingsへ任せる項目・service側の実装項目・個別Tool未対応を分けること。

これはSonyのGTA VI PlayStation marketing/product pageに記載されたPS5 featureの観察であり、Xbox機能差、GTA VI内の設定項目/アクセシビリティ、Rockstarの機器APIやAvocadoへの統合を確定しない。rating/offer FAQの表示にはページ間および同一page内に更新時期のずれがある。

## 46. 追加調査：UGCのreport対象・privacy control・block機能の限界を分解（2026-10-17 / Security、RQ14）

Rockstar [GTA Online reporting support](https://support.rockstargames.com/articles/3h4bpT9AgPUjudbEq2mZU2/reporting-content-and-behavior-in-grand-theft-auto-online)はabusive communication、cheating、disruptive play、offensive creator content、job cover image/description/in-game text/assetsをreport項目として区別する。report reasonにはharassment、identity hate、personal-information request、self-harm threat、illegal/regulated activities、child exploitation、violent extremism、gore、nudity/sexual-content、sexual harassment等があり、reportしたfeatureも選ばせる。別の[privacy/UGC support](https://support.rockstargames.com/articles/6NKqPKffElhndfInHKOyeP/rockstar-games-privacy-settings-and-user-generated-content)はprofile, wall, single-player/profile statsをprivacy settingsで制限できる一方、Crew membership、wall comment、multiplayer data等の一部が常時visibleと説明する。またplayerをblockしてもUGC上のcomment閲覧や書き込みは止まらず、reportから別途moderationへ進む。これらはGTA Online/ Rockstar account固有の状態とUIで、GTA VIのUGC systemを示すものではない。

Avocado RQ14/Sky community candidates: content report category・target object・feature context・evidence/reason・report statusを別記録し、block, mute, hide content, prevent direct contact, report/review, appealを一つの「blocked/safe」stateにまとめない。privacy preferenceはprofile/status/comment/content visibilityごとに説明し、comment/crew/activity that remain visibleを設定前に列挙する。Report submission acknowledgmentはaction receiptであり、moderation decision/appeal accepted/removed statusではないと示す。test cases include blocked author’s existing and new comments, shared UGC page visibility, reviewer access, appeal, cache/search exposure, reporter protection and error recovery; measure time-to-report, targeting accuracy, false report, review/recovery time and privacy expectation mismatch.

Rockstar policy is platform-specific; report categories are not an exhaustive checklist for Avocado, and its settings, visibility, moderation deadlines or safe-account guarantee should not be copied. This is a research hypothesis; no moderation system, account linkage, or feature requirements were changed.



## 47. 追加調査：同じ公式hubから別商品・販売者・supportへ分岐する（2026-10-17 / SIM/eSIM、AI06）

[Rockstar公式GTA VI hub](https://www.rockstargames.com/VI/vice-city-collection)はゲームのpreorder、launch trailer、soundtrack preview、collector’s box/merchandiseを並べるが、対象商品ごとに別ページへ分岐する。[Vice City Collection merchandise listing](https://store.rockstargames.com/merchandise/gtavi-goodtime-state-vice-city-collection)は$399.99の「Collector’s Box」と物理的なcollectible 11点を説明し、ゲームを含まないと明記、18+ adult collectibles / choking hazard warning、free shipping対象外を表示する。Rockstar Storeの[company details](https://store.rockstargames.com/rockstar-company-details)は購入者所在地とcheckout時に表示されるmerchantによってTake-Two affiliateまたはXsollaがmerchant of recordとなる分岐を示し、[refund/return FAQ](https://store.rockstargames.com/refund-and-return-policy)はdigital game、physical merchandise、third-party storefrontでrefund/return/claimのrouteが違うとする。[regional shipping article](https://support.rockstargames.com/articles/4x9aYiCmLgCpnNATxhenu8/rockstar-store-regional-shipping-information)は配送可能地域、checkout時の送料、米英外の関税/税、配送期間が異なると説明する。[preorder FAQ](https://support.rockstargames.com/articles/40DHWEDBnq5jUuWJqKcH02/grand-theft-auto-vi-pre-order-purchase-support-faq)ではplatform marketplace購入はPS/Xbox support、Rockstar Store physical purchaseはXsolla support、他retailerはそのretailerへ振り分ける。

AvocadoのSIM/eSIM/Cloud Agent acquisition funnelではブランド共通のentry pageを持っても、game/software, physical device, SIM/profile, network plan, cloud usage, accessory, subscription/promoの対象商品・権利・契約seller・merchant of record・invoice/refund/technical support owner・shipping/tax・age/safety conditionをcheckout前に分けて表示する候補。商品間bundle/complimentary perkを含む場合、「collection/boxが商品に含むもの」と「別売り/別契約が必要なもの」をhero/checkout/receiptへ一貫して出す。商品を認識した後もseller transitionとdata transfer scopeを示し、公式hubの一つのbrandが全商品、payment、supportを販売/責任していると誤読させない。

これはRockstar Storeの一つの米国 merchandise listingとgeneral support pagesの現状記録で、Avocado merchant-of-record、tax/return/shipping/age policies、商品価格やbundle policyを設定しない。取引条件・sellerはregionとcheckoutにより変わる。

## 48. 追加調査：年齢rating・privacy collection・家族制御は別の情報層（2026-10-17 / AI06、Security、RQ14）

米国の[ESRB GTA VI entry](https://www.esrb.org/ratings/41627/grand-theft-auto-vi/)はMature 17+、複数のcontent descriptor、別枠の`In-Game Purchases`を示す。[ESRB ratings guide](https://www.esrb.org/ratings-guide/)はInteractive Elementsが年齢ratingの割当とは別のオンライン/購入機能表示だと定義し、random-item purchaseはより具体的な別descriptorとしている。GTA VI entryには`Users Interact`も`In-Game Purchases (Includes Random Items)`も掲載されない。これは掲載時点の米国rating recordの範囲であり、ゲーム機能や地域別classifications全ての将来状態を保証しない。また、[PlayStation family controls](https://www.playstation.com/en-us/support/account/ps5-parental-controls-spending-limits/)はgame age restriction、communication/UGC、spending limit、個別game/communication exceptionを異なるcontrolsとして案内する。端末family manager/guardianの設定はAvocadoの独自age/permission判定と同じ仕組みではない。

Rockstarの[Privacy Policy](https://www.rockstargames.com/pl/privacy?country=us)（2026-03更新と検索取得）は、そのサービス全体のdata categoriesにID、account/device/platform identifiers、purchase/gameplay history、device activity、inferences、chat/audio/visual、optional precise location等を列挙し、gameplay dataは未登録でも自動収集する場合があること、third-party platformから情報を受ける場合、data rights/retention/opt-outの案内を含む。ポリシー本文は今回のopenで取れず、Rockstar全サービスの説明であってGTA VI固有のruntime telemetry一覧や個別consent UIの観察ではない。

Avocadoへの比較候補は、(1) identity/age/eligibility、account-link providerと共有fields、automatic telemetry、user-supplied content、sensitive location/audio、purchase/usageを別data classに分ける、(2) collection source・purpose・recipient・retention/delete・optional/requiredを各classに結ぶ、(3) age suitability/content warning、communication permission、UGC visibility、spending/usage cap、guardian oversight、specific exceptionを独立設定として見せ、例外の対象・期間・承認者・失効をreadbackできること、(4) game/OS/platform ratingとAvocado自身のaccess decisionを区別する。これは既存Rockstar/PlayStation policyのコピー、Avocadoの子ども利用方針、本人確認水準、収集許可を決めない。

## 49. 追加調査：private playtest・moderation・discovery statusとengagement metricを切り分ける（2026-10-17 / RQ14）

GTA Online Mission Creatorに加え、隣接事例としてEpic公式[Fortnite publishing lifecycle](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/fortnite/publishing-page-features-in-fortnite-creative?lang=en-US)を照合した。そこでは private version、限定testerのplaytest、moderationへ送ったrelease、moderation後のListed/Unlisted/Unpublishedを別状態で扱う。ListedはDiscoverから発見でき、Unlistedはコードを知る人が入れるがDiscoverに出ず、Unpublishedはplay不能という区別がある。公開releaseは同じIsland codeに一つだけで、playtestの反復versionを選び直せる。別の[Discover説明](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/fortnite/how-discover-works-in-fortnite)はpersonalized/editorial/new/sponsored rowを挙げ、engagement、retention、similarityと人のreviewを可視化するほか、[Creator analytics](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/fortnite/managing-your-projects-in-fortnite-creative)はimpressions/clicks/CTR/session durationやthumbnail A/B test等を説明する。

Avocado RQ14への候補は「draft→private preview→test evidence→moderation/review→public/unlisted→superseded/withdrawn」をartifact revisionに結び、テスト・review・発見可能性・現在配信中を別statusで表示すること。探索用metricsはimpression/clickだけでなく、適合task選択、sandbox完了/失敗/復旧、task outcome quality、creator cohort exposure、cost per successful task、wrong-fit recommendationを測る。Fortniteのengagement/retention最適化を Avocadoへそのまま使わず、Avocadoでは短時間で仕事を終えられること・費用上限・安全成功を評価軸に含め、滞在時間を価値の代理にしない。A/B testはranking/thumbnail表示の実証に使えても、権利・security・品質を証明しない。

この比較はFortnite Creative/UEFNの現行公開資料であり、GTA VI creator tools、Rockstar partnership、Avocadoの公開pipeline/ranking/analytics/privacy設定を示さない。実装・要求・market-leading claimは変更しない。

## 50. 追加調査：小規模作者のplaytest計画とfeedback triageを支える（2026-10-17 / RQ14、AI06）

Games User Research (GUR)の現場課題を理解するため、Denisova et al.の[CHI PLAY 2024 paper](https://eprints.whiterose.ac.uk/id/eprint/216480/7/CHI_PLAY_24_Indie_Playtesting_final_.pdf)をfull textで確認した。研究はindie game developer 13名への半構造化interviewで、本人の友人/開発者communityから集めた早期の便利標本は初期発見に役立つ一方、対象作者/プレイヤーを代表するとは限らず、expertとnoviceの偏り、research methodの選択、率直で信頼できるfeedback、手法上の時間/機材負担、Discord/video/survey等の大量・不揃いなfeedbackから繰り返し課題を抽出すること、どの指摘を優先修正するかが難しいと報告する。個々の最新タイトルやGTA VIの調査ではなく、indie作者向けの定性的explorationである。

Avocado RQ14/Sky SDK候補として、test開始前に「何を知るためのtestか」をtask/participant criterion/observation metric/permissionへつなぎ、prototype build/version、tester cohort、consent data、issue/repro/evidence、decision/priority/owner、修正版と再testを一つの回収可能なprojectへ結ぶ軽量GUR workflowを検討できる。ownerが継続作者なら初心者task、初参加者ならsupport/復旧も試すなど、cohort/contextを分けたうえで、peer feedbackとQA/security/right reviewを区別する。video/audio captureやtelemetryはデフォルトで要求せず、必要なときだけ目的/保存期間/閲覧者を提示し同意を取る。Agentはfeedbackを要約/クラスタ化して候補issueを作れても、重要度やpublish-stopを自動決定しない。Task success, time-to-first-success, severe issue count, recurrence, severity/action status, post-fix regression, author time/costを測定候補にする。

このpaperのauthorsもsampleの自己選択・social media recruitment偏り・地域/言語の限界を列挙している。13人のinterviewはAvocado作者市場需要、testing ROI、特定metric thresholdを証明しない。RQ14/SDK・sandbox/APIの実装/priority・料金・研究参加者データ収集を追加・変更しない。

## 51. 追加調査：ゲーム内残高のearned/purchased・platform・migration条件を分離（2026-10-17 / RQ13、Wallet）

Rockstarの[current U.S. Terms of Service](https://www.rockstargames.com/legal?country=us)は、Virtual ItemsがRockstar service内での限定licenseであること、原則として金銭価値を持たずservice外でのsale/transfer/cash redemptionは書面の明示条件がない限り許可されないことを記す（ページ本文をweb openで取得できず、公開search excerptを確認。適用法/消費者権利・地域差を含むRockstar側の契約であり法律助言ではない）。[GTA Online profile migration FAQ](https://support.rockstargames.com/articles/7gFUOWEoLxs87G4Bqh08mu/platform-changes-and-gta-transfers-during-gta-online-profile-migration)は、購入したGTA$（Shark Cards/platform membership等）とプレイで得たGTA$の移行可否を分け、購入分は同じconsole family間のみ、earned fundsはplatform familyを越えて移行できると案内する。FAQは特定profile migrationの説明で、PC Legacy→Enhancedでは未使用balanceが失われる条件、PC購入後の両版creditなど例外条件も挙げる。[PS3/Xbox 360 shutdown FAQ](https://support.rockstargames.com/articles/7dADpZ41gSg4Ma8LHO6dF3/end-of-online-services-for-playstation-3-and-xbox-360-versions-of-grand-theft-auto-v-max-payne-3-and-l-a-noire-faq)は当該service closureでvirtual currency purchasesがplatform-specific、refund/transfer不可と明記する。いずれもGTA V/GTA Onlineであり、GTA VI currency、multi-platform balance、refund promiseを定めない。

Avocado RQ13への候補: provider-specific virtual balanceとWallet real-fund ledgerを別型・別権限・別journalに維持し、遊戯内earned/purchased/bonus grant、owner/account、platform/runtime version、expiry/transferability、eligible services、source transactionを記録する。移行時はbalance classごとの規則でreadbackし、不可移行・expire・service sunset時の条件を前もって示す。Cash-out/換金やAvocado Wallet transferはゲームproviderが別途明示許可し、法務/決済/二重記帳の個別gateを満たす場合だけ候補にし、GTA$のような第三者currencyをAvocadoで作成/売買/convertする許可と読み替えない。ゲームclientのbalance表示は取引承認や独立したfinancial ledger証拠ではない。

比較根拠としての記録のみ。RQ13 adapter、Wallet、ATM、手数料、production exchange、実ゲームbalanceは変更せず、fixture以外の移動・換金・購入は行わない。

## 52. 追加調査：featured reward・affiliate・engagement share・in-game salesは別経済（2026-10-17 / RQ14、RQ15、RQ13）

Rockstarの[GTA Online Mission Creator announcement](https://www.rockstargames.com/newswire/article/39a3412379o79o/play-thrilling-new-gta-online-experiences-built-by-the-community-with?pubDate=20260430)は、#communityseriesで応募対象にしたMissionがCommunity Series等へfeatured採用された場合の一度きりのGTA$10,000,000とgame内衣服/trophy awardを案内し、prototype sharing/peer feedbackも促す。これはGTA Onlineのgame-internal rewardで、現金payoutやGTA VI creator programの説明ではない。

Epicの[Fortnite Engagement Payout docs](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/fortnite/engagement-payout-in-fortnite-creative)は、18歳以上のeligible developerに対し、Fortnite Item Shopと関連real-money purchaseのeligible net revenueの40%をmonthly engagement poolとして配り、active playtime、retention、新規/復帰player acquisition等を使う説明を公表する。公式supportは[Support-A-Creator](https://www.epicgames.com/help/c-34406160/a19029301) affiliate code経由の購入報酬と、Island developer programのengagement payoutを別programとし、developerがreal moneyまたはV-Bucksで受け取れる選択を記載する。[In-Island Transactions](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/fortnite/in-island-transactions-in-fortnite)はcreatorが自分のitemをV-Bucksで販売し、Creator Portalにcentral transaction recordを持つ仕組みで、engagement payoutと別のeconomic pathである。verified threshold, tax/Hyperwallet, policy enrollment, payout estimates/approvalなど別条件もあり、規則は変更され得る。

Publishing lifecycle detail from Epic's current [Creator Portal workflow](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/fortnite/publishing-from-the-creator-portal-in-fortnite-creative): private versions are unmoderated and accessible by tester code; a release then gets a metadata/assets review and a separate gameplay review. A rejected new release does not replace the last approved live island. After acceptance, the creator can select automatic or manual publication, and a published item must also be Listed to enter Discover. This adds a useful rollback/failure property beyond the previous short state summary.

Avocadoへの候補は、creator compensation/benefitを (a) curator-selected one-time in-product reward、(b) publisher-purchase revenue share pool、(c) affiliate acquisition bounty、(d) creatorのdirect in-product sale、(e) marketplace/subscription payout、(f) in-game-only virtual awardへ型分けし、それぞれsource revenue、net/gross basis、who is eligible, owner/age/region, seller/payor, platform fee/tax, period/threshold, estimate-vs-verified, clawback/dispute, Wallet treatmentを明示すること。Featured status aloneはearning claimを意味せず、user-added favorite/creator followingやview metricsもpayment entitlementを付与しない。Avocado RQ15の「手数料0」ATM意味をGame fee/publisher revenue shareへ流用せず、GTA$10mやEpicの40%をAvocado price/payout promiseとして使わない。

この比較は別々のGTA OnlineとFortnite program policyであり、GTA VI author monetization, Avocado fee/payout, creator market eligibility, legal/tax/accounting terms、実支払いを確定しない。RQ13実残高・外部資金は移動しない。

## 53. 追加調査：推薦露出・広告流入・creator reward・現金化の状態表示を分ける（2026-10-17 / RQ14、Wallet）

[Roblox Creator Hubのacquisition analytics](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/acquisition)は、Home recommendation、Search、Sponsored ads、Search ads、Friends、off-platform share link等を別流入として示し、play/impression、play-through、7日retention、30日revenue等へ結びつける。Discovery documentationは推薦候補のengagement、retention、spend等を説明する一方、広告起点とorganic recommendationを同一の獲得として数えないようpaid/organicの分析を案内する。これはRoblox独自の指標とアルゴリズムで、Avocado rankingの正解や成功基準ではない。

[Creator Rewards](https://create.roblox.com/docs/creator-rewards)のDashboardはrewarded active spenders/new/reactivated audienceと推定Robux報酬を表示する。報酬はEarned Robuxとして60日holdがあり、Audience Expansion eligibilityやDevExの資格/税条件が別に適用される。[DevEx terms](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/developer-exchange)はDashboardのEarned Robux estimateが支払保証でも全残高の適格性保証でもなく、requestごとにreviewされると明記する。したがって露出、eligible event、推定報酬、hold中balance、審査中request、支払完了は異なる状態である。規則・換算条件は変更され得るため実運用時点で再確認が必要。

Avocado RQ14/RQ13への候補: 一覧表示/推薦impression、paid promotion、share referral、qualified use、creator-accrued estimate、確定/調整、hold/threshold、review、available-to-withdraw、paidを別イベント・別集計にし、本人がsource別で比較できるsummaryを提供する。Ranking cohortと広告予算は本人の明示設定の範囲に限定し、paid placementをorganic recommendationへ偽装しない。抽出期間・cohort・deduplication・refund/chargeback・data latency・estimate confidenceも表示する。Metric improvementはtask success, safety, recovery, accessibility, retention/fitと併せて読む。長時間滞在や高課金だけで優先順位を上げない。

これはRoblox creator ecosystemの現行文書からの設計比較で、GTA VI discovery/payout specification、Avocado algorithm、creator revenue share、exchange rate、Wallet account/tax policyを決定しない。現金化・送金・広告購入は実施せず、RQ13/15と既存商品/Wallet contractの変更もしない。

## 54. 追加調査：共同作者のplay/edit/publish/monetize権限を分ける（2026-10-17 / RQ14、Security）

[Roblox Groups (teams)](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/groups)はplaytest, edit, publish, analytics, monetization, secrets, API key, Data Store, role management, group revenue等を別permissionとして列挙し、特にrole assignment/manage spend/delete data等に二段階認証を求める。Creator Hub [Collaboration](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/collaboration)ではOwner/Edit/Play/No Accessを区分し、group-ownedとuser-ownedで共同編集可能範囲も異なる。publish docsはnew gameのdefault accessをcreator/editor/適切なgroup roleに限定し、準備後にpublic releaseを別途行う。

Avocado RQ14候補: creator team rolesをPlaytest, Edit, Review, Publish, Analytics, Monetization, Secret/API-key, Support, Wallet settlementに分け、危険度に応じて強い本人確認/二人目review/期限付き権限を要求する。project ownerは役割の付与/剥奪とaudit logを確認でき、access changeはその後のjob execution/releaseへ明示される。Test accessやEdit権はproduction publishやpayment authorityを含まない。Source project permission、OS Broker approval、external Tool credentials、real-fund Wallet authorityの間で権限が暗黙に継承されないようにする。

補足のRockstar [account unlink procedure](https://support.rockstargames.com/articles/tp2PvsKT8MKjraByTigca/unlinking-an-account-from-your-rockstar-games-account)はXbox/PSNのrel link制限とGTA V/RDR2 progress loss caveatを明示し、Steam/Epic/Xbox PC unlinkはsupport pathへ分岐する。これはGTA VIのlink ruleではないが、identity changeはUI上のtoggleではなくscope/eligibility/support/recoveryがある状態遷移だと示す隣接例。Avocado account-link, 2FA, secret exposure, protected actionの設計/実装をこの調査だけで変更しない。

Roblox team permission modelとRockstar account recoveryは各社独自。これらからGTA VI co-op/creator workflowやAvocado role matrixを確定せず、権限変更/外部credential/実資金操作を行わない。

## 55. 追加調査：viral budget額と会社のaggregate accountingを分ける（2026-10-17 / RQ14、事業判断）

Take-Twoの[2026 Form 10-K (SEC filing)](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/946581/000162828026037434/ttwo-20260331.htm)は、2026-03-31時点で未発売タイトル全体に関するsoftware development costs/licensesのnet aggregateを$2.1497bnと記録するが、個別タイトル別のGTA VI budgetは開示しない。会計方針はtechnological feasibility後のinternal payroll/stock compensation/incentive compensationおよびthird-party development/production/content costを資産化し、feasibility前はR&D expenseへ計上、release後はtitleごとのrevenue/straight-line methodに基づきamortizeすると説明する。Aggregate asset balanceやcompany-wide annual R&D expenseを単一game's cash spend/production budgetとして割り当てることはできない。

Recent GamesRadar coverage [relaying a Bloomberg interview](https://www.gamesradar.com/games/grand-theft-auto/rockstar-still-wont-reveal-the-cost-of-gta-6-development-but-its-definitely-not-the-previously-rumored-usd1-5-billion/) reports that Rockstar publishing head Jennifer Kolbe did not disclose a concrete number and disputed a $1.5bn rumor; Bloomberg article/body and interview transcript were not directly inspected here. This secondhand report is weaker evidence than a title-specific filing; leave every circulating number labeled rumor/estimate unless Rockstar/Take-Two provide a scoped primary disclosure. Take-Two's [FY2026 Form 10-K](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/946581/000162828026037434/ttwo-20260331.htm) calls GTA VI a planned November 19, 2026 release, and its earlier [quarterly investor release](https://ir.take2games.com/static-files/a68e5705-1d42-46f6-bd6b-95c219d7aac1) listed PS5/Xbox Series X|S for that date at publication. Release schedule remains subject to future update.

Avocado candidate: do not use viral competitor budget, projected payback, developer-revenue share or sales forecasts as observed inputs. Keep actual observed cost, committed contract, reserve, forecast and scenario assumptions separate; state scope, period, labor/infra/content/marketing inclusion, revenue timing, and source quality. A total portfolio capitalized balance or investor bookings guidance is not per-product unit economics, and should not drive Avocado price, creator payout, pilot ROI or hiring claims.

This is public-reporting/source-quality analysis only. It does not establish GTA VI budget, profit, break-even date or realized sales and does not change Avocado commercial assumptions or approval.

## 56. 追加調査：遊べるsampleから作者用sourceを開く学習導線（2026-10-17 / RQ14、SDK）

Rockstarの[GTA Online Mission Creator guide](https://www.rockstargames.com/newswire/article/39a3412379o79o/play-thrilling-new-gta-online-experiences-built-by-the-community-with?pubDate=20260430)はCreator basics用にRockstar-created five tutorial missionsを紹介し、同一のmissionを通常のJobs Menuで遊ぶほか、Mission Creatorで開いてどのように作ったか学べると説明する。さらにprototypeができたらRockstar Discordの#job-sharingでfeedbackを交換し、publish時に#communityseriesを付ければpotential considerationの対象となるが、tagはfeatured adoption guaranteeではない。Featured rewardも別途一度だけGTA$10mとin-game items。ここはGTA Online creator ecosystemに限定され、GTA VI featureや外部作者への金銭給付ではない。

Avocado RQ14候補: taskを完遂できるreference project、検証可能なfixture/sample data、agent/toolの設定例を分けて配布し、(1) sampleをrun (2) user-owned copyを作る (3) 目的/権限/fixtureを安全に変更 (4) exact revisionでtest (5) preview/share (6) explicit publishへ進むguided pathを比較する。Userはlive sampleを利用でき、editor表示時にsource/tutorial affordanceを見つけられる。success metricはtime-to-first-success, sample fork率, first edit→valid test率, failure/recovery, unsafe permission inheritance, author support load, publish completion。Rating/high-stakes fixtureは制限し、reference data/credentialsを実利用へ再利用させない。

Five tutorial missions are game-native examples and offer no evidence that Avocado authors need this flow; Community Series tag/rating/creator payout are not a product contract. RQ14 implementation/SDK/price/rights/publishing policy and runtime remain unchanged.

## 57. 追加調査：SDK exampleは版固定・実行可能・再検証可能にする（2026-10-17 / RQ14、SDK）

Alharbi & Kolovosの[ICPC 2024 controlled study](https://eprints.whiterose.ac.uk/id/eprint/218768/)は61 Java developerにJoda-Time API examplesのcomprehension/reuse taskを実施し、linear exampleはreaction timeが速い一方、correctnessとsubjective ratingに大きな差は見つからず、追加研究が必要と記す。API examplesに直線的で短い導入を使う可能性は示すが、Avocado author successやJava以外のSDKへ直接一般化しない。[REVEAL study](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.cola.2023.101201)はReact noviceのonline examplesに含まれた複数のAPI compatibility/omission errorsを解析し、検知/repair支援がexample integrationを改善するformative evaluationを報告する。ScienceDirect full-textは今回開けず、publisher abstractのみの確認とする。[Executable API documentation](https://doi.org/10.1016/S0164-1212(02)00055-9)の研究提案はproseとexpected input/outputを持つ実行可能test casesをAPI docsに加え、同じtestを実行して仕様文書の同期を確認する方式をcase studyで示す（2003年のsoftware documentation approach）。

RQ14/SDK候補: starter API examplesをSDK/runtime version, permissions/capabilities, deterministic fixture, expected output, test command, dependency versionsと一緒に配る。copy/editでversion/policy/data scopeが変わるならold validation badgeをinvalidateし、sandboxで再実行して結果とfailure/recoveryを示す。最初の導入はlinear pathにして、後からoptional concepts/alternativesを分岐表示する。Metrics: first-run pass rate, copied-example repair frequency, API misuse/security findings, task success, comprehension time/error, cross-version breakage, retry/recovery. Agent-generated code and tests remain proposals/evidence; broker permission and human approval remain independent.

Study population/framework limits and publisher-abstract-only source access mean these are design/test candidates, not an Avocado SDK requirement or proof of impact. No API/runtime/design catalog/Tool code has been changed.

## 58. 追加調査：bundle商品・trial・digital codeの受取/地域/更新条件を分ける（2026-10-17 / SIM/eSIM、Service Access、RQ14）

Rockstarの[Platforms, Editions, and Versions FAQ](https://support.rockstargames.com/articles/4QfG4FmZCf5W1gS8jy4UVT/grand-theft-auto-vi-platform-editions-and-versions)は、physical box内にdiscではなくdownload codeが入ること、PlayStation redemptionはplatform-account regionに制限されXbox codeはregion lockされないこと、日本向けPlayStation product codeは地域規制によりlaunch-date issuanceから170日で失効することを区別する。同FAQのdigital pre-order bonusはgame purchase/Vintage Vice City itemsと1-month GTA+ membershipを別componentとして掲げ、physical pre-orderにはGTA+ bonusが含まれない。standard ownerはcodeをredeem後、別途store upgradeでUltimate Editionへ移行できる。[日本向けPS Store listing](https://store.playstation.com/ja-jp/product/JP0230-PPSA29660_00-GTAVISTANDARD001)はStandard ¥9,800/Ultimate ¥12,280、購入アカウントに結びついたGTA+ 1-month redemption deadline 2027-03-31、trial後のmonthly auto-renew and cancellation conditionを表示する。listingは2026-10-17確認時の地域/時点snapshotで、税・為替・promotion changeもある。

Avocado SIM/eSIM/service-access候補: bundle summaryでphysical SIM/eSIM delivery/activation, network plan, RockstarOS/Sky/Zema entitlement, cloud Agent usage, device/client install, promotional trialをseparate line item/stateにし、include vs metered/paid distinction, seller, price/tax, eligibility/channel, region, expiry/redeem-by, account binding, trial-end renewal price/period/cancel routeを同時に示す。Payment, line activation, service entitlement, native/browser client readiness, usage-budget authorizationは別々のevent/readbackにする。One month free/physical product inclusion/upgrade eligibilityは自動で全channels/user/deviceに適用されるとは仮定しない。

Rockstar/PlayStation channel rules do not define Avocado's SIM, subscriber, carrier or local consumer-law requirements. No purchase, account redemption, service contract or price/policy change was made.

## 59. 追加調査：sponsorshipを見えない推薦へ溶かさない（2026-10-17 / RQ14、creator market）

Ershov, He & Seiler, [“How Much Influencer Marketing Is Undisclosed? Evidence from Twitter” (Marketing Science 2025)](https://pubsonline.informs.org/doi/10.1287/mksc.2024.0838)はbrands横断の100m超tweet corpusとclassificationでsponsored post disclosureを推定し、preferred specificationで96% (lower bound 82%)がdiscloseされないと報告し、online surveyで多くのconsumerがnon-disclosed sponsorを識別できなかったという。これはTwitter influencer advertisingの検出methodとsurveyであり、ゲームのUGC discoveryではない。数字をAvocado prevalence/CTR forecastへ転用できず、algorithmsが公平である証拠でもない。

Avocado RQ14候補: paid placementのpayer/source/payment basis/period, ranking reason/provenance, organic recommendation, user-selectable filters, creator's own self-promotion, affiliate link and platform-curated featureを違うbadge/statusで表示する。Creator upload時にsponsorship/rights disclosureを申告し、catalog reviewでbilling/affiliate metadataとの一致を確認する。Curation staff/Agentがad paymentをranking feedbackへ混ぜる場合はranking result summaryにshow; paid badge is immutable in exported links. Audit paid impression share, organic exposure, cohort reach, clicks/task fit, concealment/misclassification complaint, reviewer accuracy separately. Disclosure should not silently erase organic exposure nor guarantee rank.

This suggests transparency and auditability, not a legal sufficiency finding: applicable advertising law, privacy rules, ranking policy, payouts and moderation standards need their own review. No Avocado sponsorship feature or requirement is authorized by the study.

## 60. 追加調査：ゲーム内data collectionはpurposeとsharing contextを説明する（2026-10-17 / Security、RQ14、MAT18）

Bourdoucen, Nurgalieva & Lindqvist, [CHI PLAY 2023 “Privacy Is the Price”](https://doi.org/10.1145/3611064)は21 online gameのdata practice分析と、player20人のqualitative interviewを組み合わせる。著者はpersonal-data sharing willingnessがgame setting/design contextに関連すること、data collectionへの誤解/懸念があることを報告し、transparencyとplayer controlの設計を提案する。Aalto institutional repositoryのabstract/full-text listingも確認したが、今回のweb browserで論文本体のfetchは失敗。Game online telemetryの研究で、single-player GTA VIのdata handlingやAvocado surveyを示さない。

Winter et al.の[2025 SSRN preprint](https://papers.ssrn.com/sol3/papers.cfm?abstract_id=5166399)は609人のquestionnaireを報告し、playerがprivacyを大切にする一方、privacy controlsで困り、customizable consentへの期待を述べる。ただしSSRN上のworking paper/preprintでありpeer-reviewed statusを確認していない。両研究のpopulation/sample/measureは違い、合算/generalizeはしない。

Avocado candidate: optional gameplay/work telemetry, test recording, microphone/audio capture, room sensor and account identityをone all-or-nothing agreementへ束ねず、purpose, field, audience/provider, retention, cross-job reuse, deletion, export, opt-out/withdrawal, child/guardian rulesを必要なscopeごとに提示する。Test participant permission, creator debug collection, product analytics, safety/security log, billing/cost recordを別categoryにする。No capture by default; release/publish permission does not authorize player surveillance. Metrics include completion/recovery per data minimization tier, opt-in/withdrawal success, delete-time, unwanted-surprise reports and task-performance impact.

These are research-based design candidates, not a determination of consent/legal basis for any jurisdiction or proof about Rockstar/GTA VI's actual collection. No privacy policy, telemetry, data-retention, device sensor collection or RQ14 requirement was changed.

## 61. 追加調査：一種類の「集中音profile」を全員へ当てはめない（2026-10-17 / MAT18、MAT15、RQ14）

Torresin et al., [Building and Environment 2024 study of home-study places](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.buildenv.2024.111324)は451 university students questionnaireとその一部23人のfield study/interview, building checklist, sound-pressure measurementを組み合わせ、acoustical/psychosocial preferencesで5 distinct groupsを得た。study-place building location, outside noise, indoor environmental perceptionなどもprofile差に関わるとする。同著者の[residential soundscape study of 61 homes](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.buildenv.2023.110726)はhome working/relaxationに適したsoundscapeをhigh comfort/low content, perceived private/controlledと評価し、traffic/window/Speech Interference Level等がcomfortに関連する事例を示す。これは異なるsample/model/country-specific observational evidenceで、Avocado AI音響効果や特定の音響基準閾値を決めない。

さらにZhao et al., [Smart home soundscape: constructing a perceptual model with qualitative and quantitative methods (Frontiers in Psychology, 2026)](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC13278869/)は28 long-time smart-home user interviewsと12 sound source/3 scenario (rest, entertainment, work)のVR evaluationを120人に実施し、sound source perceptionをcomfort/informativeness/pleasantness/clarity/redundancy、environment perceptionをcomfort/recognisabilityとして整理した。Alarms were high-information but uncomfortable; indication sounds were clearer/pleasant; operational sounds comfortable but potentially redundant。Short, controlled VR setting, one relatively orderly interior, tech-savvy volunteer group, no sound-source combinations/long-term adaptation are explicit limits; scenario effect was small in this experiment. Voit et al., [The Attention Kitchen (ACM MUM 2021)](https://hcistudio.org/publication/voit2021the/) compared OnObject, ambient On-Environment, and On-Smartphone notifications during cooking with 24 participants; ambient On-Environment notifications were least disruptive in that task but do not prove a universal modality ranking or urgency policy.

Avocado Mini/MAT18 candidate: default to silence/no new sound output; keep ambient masking/music, operational sounds, status indications and urgent alarms distinct. For each cue, evaluate comfort, informativeness, recognisability/clarity and redundancy separately; high informativeness does not make a cue acceptable if it is aversive. Offer per-person/per-room reversible profiles instead of one “productive sound” setting; a shared room has a visible owner-controlled pause/off and should not broadcast private job details. Identify the task/context first (focused reading/call/shared lounge/sleep); measure comfort/control, task performance, speech interference, annoyance/startle, unwanted activation and preference variation by role/cohort. If sound is not wanted, a visual/private haptic cue or no cue remains available. Do not infer preferences from camera/audio signals or learn personal profile without explicit configuration.

These building/soundscape studies do not evaluate GTA VI or Avocado Mini hardware, and observational correlation is not proof an agent-generated soundscape improves performance. They supply a pilot design hypothesis only; no device, feature, threshold, rights, study protocol or acceptance gate changed.

The 2026 smart-home soundscape study also describes repeatable VR as a privacy-preserving way to compare sound perception where real homes are difficult to standardize. Avocado evaluation could begin with controlled synthetic scenes and no room recording, then use an in-situ pilot only after the owner, task and access scope are explicit; passing VR tasks would not prove physical-room or device acceptance.

## 62. 追加調査：shared roomのstatus soundはowner権限と同居人privacyを分ける（2026-10-17 / MAT18、Security、RQ14）

Zeng & Roesner, [USENIX Security 2019 in-home user study](https://www.usenix.org/conference/usenixsecurity19/presentation/zeng) developed a prototype smart-home app with location-based and supervisory access controls plus activity notifications; a month-long field study covered seven households. The authors report multi-user, multi-device physical-environment systems have access-control and intelligibility/usability challenges even in non-adversarial homes; their small study did not resolve adversarial/home-abuse scenarios. Meng, Kekulluoglu & Vaniea, [CSCW 2021 “Owning and Sharing”](https://doi.org/10.1145/3449119) interviewed 10 in-home account owners/cohabitants plus 9 visitors. Cohabitants who regularly use shared assistants may feel ownership without equal account settings; visitors/cohabitants also reported data-use/always-monitoring and operational transparency concerns, with social etiquette functioning as an informal boundary. These samples are qualitative and describe voice assistants/smart homes, not Avocado Mini adoption or household policy.

Avocado Mini/MAT18 candidate: identify whose account/room/physical output/device a cue belongs to; room-level owner authority does not automatically authorize disclosing the individual user's job name, model, budget, approver, contacts or private prompt in a shared audible/visible channel. Provide silent/private personal routes and generic room-state cues; keep each occupant's job/consent/settings separate from owner device admin. A visitor's presence, social relationship or room sound cannot prove identity/authority/consent; environmental audio cannot be used as sole gate for unlocking a workspace, reading private records or approving work. Stop/mute/off and per-room data boundaries must be legible to cohabitants/visitors, not only the account admin.

This is a multi-user privacy/access-control comparison, not a design acceptance or tenant/legal authority decision. We did not collect room audio, inspect a home, set user/guardian roles, add microphone listening or change the Mini/security permission model.

## 63. 追加調査：購入後のcancel条件をdownload/bonus/trial stateに結びつける（2026-10-17 / SIM/eSIM、Service Access）

PlayStation公式の[予約購入キャンセル案内](https://www.playstation.com/ja-jp/support/store/cancel-ps-store-pre-order/)では、発売前または購入後14日以内という時間条件だけでなく、主要コンテンツのdownload/stream開始前という条件がある。事前downloadが有効だと利用者が意識する前に条件が変わりうる。発売前利用できるbonusは本編をdownloadしていなくてもcancel時に利用停止となる。別の[subscription refund案内](https://www.playstation.com/en-us/support/store/ps-store-refund-request/)は無料trial期間を14日間の返金期間へ含める例を示し、subscription cancelは将来のpayment停止であり、現在のbilling periodが終わるまで利用可能という説明と、すでに使った場合の部分返金調整を分ける。Xbox supportはこの検索では公式cancel内容を取得できず、platform比較は未完了。

Avocado SIM/eSIM candidate: purchase receipt/state view must freeze a timestamped copy of the offer version, geography/channel, paid amount, trial clock, auto-download/activation facts, which benefit is currently available, refund/cancel eligibility and next action. Separate stop-renewal, terminate service immediately, revoke promotional entitlement, and request/refund payment; do not let “cancel” ambiguously imply all four. Prompt before enabling prefetch/automatic activation when that event changes cancellation rights; show the consequence next to the control. Keep support/routing and original-payment vs wallet refund state visible and reconcile against provider callback rather than claim a refund from the user's cancellation click. Local law and Avocado contract terms require separate definition; PS rules are product-specific examples only.

No Avocado cancellation/consumer policy, automatic activation, subscription, entitlement or payment was changed.

## 64. 追加調査：GTA VI直近のlaunch campaignはmedia/news/merch/music/preorderを別journeyにしている（2026-10-17 / RQ14、Service Access）

ユーザー提供のYouTube URL `MNPmV5nWvC4` は、Rockstarが10月15日に出した[Grand Theft Auto VI Launch Trailer](https://www.rockstargames.com/newswire/article/517572321a549a/watch-the-grand-theft-auto-vi-launch-trailer)と一致する。公式記事はJusticeの新曲をfeatureとして案内する。Newswireの一覧では10月8日に[In-Game Radio Stations](https://www.rockstargames.com/VI/music)とGTA VI collection、9月17日にThe Album announcementが並び、公式GTA VI landing pageはtrailer, radio/music, album, people/placesへ分岐する。個々のitemは日付・形式・目的が異なるため、launch trailerをgameplay/API/online announcementと読み替えず、music productやmerchをbase-game feature/preorder eligibilityと混同しない。これらは10/17時点でクロール可能な公式campaign snapshotである。

Avocado RQ14/SIM service candidate: product launch/hub page should separate product capability/status note, demo/preview, documentation/sample, creator/community story, merchandise or paid add-on, and subscription/usage offer. Each card should carry source owner, publish/last-checked date, applicable version/region, paid/eligibility status and its own CTA; an editorial release does not prove runtime capability or rights for a displayed asset. Measure route comprehension and wrong-click/support contacts along with conversion. The Rockstar campaign is a content-organization example, not evidence of GTA VI creator access or an Avocado product specification.

No Avocado site, marketing materials, service offers, assets, music rights or product claims were changed.

## 65. 追加調査：同じpreorderでもcharge timingと失敗recoveryはproviderごとに違う（2026-10-17 / SIM/eSIM、Service Access）

Microsoft公式[Microsoft Store pre-order FAQ](https://support.microsoft.com/en-us/accounts-billing/pre-orders-for-microsoft-store)は、digital pre-orderをMicrosoft account balanceで払う場合は即時請求し、他のpayment optionではreleaseの最大10日前に請求することがあると説明する。残高不足分をpayment backupで補う場合があり、release時にauthorization失敗すると、案内を送りpayment method更新機会と複数回再試行の後、未解決ならpreorderをcancelする。Billed後/発売前のcancelはrefund処理で、refund statusは別に調べる。公式[Cancel order/pre-order support](https://support.microsoft.com/en-us/accounts-billing/cancel-an-order-or-pre-order-from-microsoft-store)では、cancel buttonが出ない場合は既に請求済み等の可能性を示し、Xbox refund flowを別リンクする。またMicrosoft Store price guaranteeは同説明上physical preorderに限定され、digital itemに適用されない。これらはMicrosoft Store account/region-specific rulesで、GTA VI listing自身のtermsやAvocado contractではない。

PS/Microsoft official examples together suggest Avocado checkout/status ledger should model: order accepted → upcoming charge date/source → payment attempted/authorized/failed/retry deadline → confirmed/cancelled → entitlement issued/revoked → refund requested/approved/settled. Show exactly which event occurred, amount/source, required user action and deadline; expired card or backup funding ambiguity should not silently turn an order into “paid.” Keep line-item partial cancel/refund and bundle component entitlement mapping explicit; provider-generated cancellation/refund confirmation is evidence, while the user's click is only a request. Do not promise price protection without a written offer and matching region/channel.

These are provider behaviors to compare, not consumer-law conclusions. We did not make an order, update payment details or alter Avocado billing or cancellation policy.

## 66. 追加調査：spatial inputの「手ぶらが自然」と「tangibleなら良い」を測定で分解する（2026-10-17 / MAT18、MAT04参照）

Rauschnabel et al., [Frontiers in Virtual Reality (2023), “Touching the future of training”](https://www.frontiersin.org/journals/virtual-reality/articles/10.3389/frvir.2023.1187883/full) compared real object, controller VR, optical free-hand VR, and tangible tracked physical object in the same VR training flow. Four-arm between-subject study recruited 100 young adults (primarily students, low prior VR experience); evaluation included immediate workload/focus/realism and a knowledge test a week later. The authors report free-hand lowest in haptic realism and concentration, worse workload/subjective performance than controller; adding a tracked tangible object improved free-hand experience and showed the highest observed knowledge scores, but the one-week between-group difference was not significant. Controller remained the study's best overall VR method. This was low-complexity explosive-ordnance familiarization with controlled hardware, not a home workbench, material design task, long-session fatigue study, or evidence that any tactile tool is safer.

Zhang et al., [Cognition, Technology & Work (2025 online), “Human-centered design of VR interface features to support mental workload and spatial cognition during collaboration tasks in manufacturing”](https://link.springer.com/article/10.1007/s10111-025-00809-6) compared portable vs fixed, flat vs 3D, tangible vs non-tangible mini maps. Sample: 12 industrial practitioners in 6 pairs plus 18 students in 9 pairs; practitioners tried 5 prototypes and students 3 randomized versions. Portability affected 9/10 reported metrics, improving navigation/layout perception and lowering measured workload/frustration; 3D had selective layout/time effects; tangibility alone had no significant main effect. Teams noted that moving the map nearer/adjusting its orientation could help, while a portable mini-map sometimes blocked view or pulled attention away from the environment. This tested navigation aids in manufacturing collaboration, not spatial authoring or avocadoMini's hardware; participant background split and short prototype task limit transfer.

MAT18/MAT04 experiment candidate: compare (A) the planned physical/tangible hand interaction, (B) controller/button/desktop fallback, (C) no-free-hand hybrid with visible tactile surrogate, and (D) moveable/repositionable workspace/legend while holding task, content and software constant. Record hand-tracking errors, task completion/correction, orientation and object-placement accuracy, recovery and save/reopen, mental demand, physical demand/fatigue, frustration, focus loss, obstruction, accessibility preference and session duration. Do not treat “natural,” presence, or novelty rating as successful authoring. Let users reposition boards/menus and continue a task from another mode. Do not mandate a motion gesture where a button, keyboard, voice-free visual action, or no-action route is feasible; no voice inference is needed.

These studies support comparison variables and fallback hypotheses only. They do not change hardware envelope, hand-tracking design, user requirements, or prototype/device acceptance gates.

## 67. 追加調査：gestureは一律に避けず、支援対象userと一緒に負担の少ない語彙を作る（2026-10-17 / MAT18、Accessibility）

Bamoallem et al., [INTERACT 2025 “HF-MAI: Touchless mid-air interaction for individuals with dexterity impairment”](https://orca.cardiff.ac.uk/id/eprint/180300/) evaluated a touchless Leap Motion prototype with people with MS/dexterity impairment, MS therapists and a UX expert. Abstract reports pinch/spread/rotate variants as faster than difficult touchscreen equivalents and emphasizes co-created single/two-finger gesture vocabulary. The repository record exposes the accepted post-print and the abstract, not participant counts in the retrieved material; avoid claiming statistical generality from the abstract alone. A related, later peer-reviewed article by the same group, [Universal Access in the Information Society 2026](https://orca.cardiff.ac.uk/id/eprint/187819/), reports separate samples of ten MS-Dex participants for conventional tablet challenges and ten for gesture elicitation across 18 tablet scenarios. Participants preferred gestures reducing precise finger coordination and extensive hand movement. Authors recorded video/Leap tracking in study; Avocado research need not retain identifiable video to evaluate gestures.

Combined with §66, the design implication is not that gesture is inherently accessible or inherently fatiguing; benefit and burden vary with task/body/condition. MAT18 should co-design optional gesture vocabulary with intended accessibility users, permit adapting/reassigning gesture and reducing range/precision, compare against tactile/controller/touch/keyboard alternatives, and let users switch mode without losing task state. Measure execution success, time, error/recovery, reach/pain/fatigue reported by participant, confusion/memorization and preference. Preserve non-gesture path; never infer disability, identity, consent or preferred input from camera/mic telemetry.

These are small/context-specific samples and do not establish clinical benefit, broad population accessibility, or Mini certification. No sensor retention, accessibility claim, input mode, hardware, requirement or acceptance gate changed.

## 68. 追加調査：空間interactionの累積腕疲労は手位置・負荷・休憩条件を含めて測る（2026-10-17 / MAT18、MAT04）

Li et al., [UIST 2025 “Weight-Induced Consumed Endurance (WICE)”](https://doi.org/10.1145/3746059.3747745) propose a model for real-time/cumulative shoulder fatigue that adds carried-object weight/location and individual arm-mass inputs, then compares predictions to endurance time and Borg CR10 exertion ratings. The reported validation used a 38-person lab study, held arm-to-torso angle at 90°, compared no added load with 0.5–2.0 kg attached at elbow/wrist, mixed assignment and rest between arms/conditions. It showed model alignment to the study ground truth; this is not proof a deployed home system can diagnose or prevent injury, and 90-degree static posture, added weights and healthy adult sample do not match avocadoMini sessions. The work's main design relevance is that a seemingly light wearable or a high/extended arm position changes fatigue; comparing prototype weight at wrist vs on a fixed support can expose avoidable burden.

MAT18/04 candidate evaluation: keep primary acceptance based on task accuracy, time, error/recovery and user-reported comfort/effort/fatigue under realistic task durations. Record arm elevation/reach zone and interaction device weight as controlled study variables, include breaks and free choice to change mode; report group variation. If a biomechanics model is explored, use it offline to compare layout/weight scenarios with disclosed assumptions rather than inferring an individual's health or blocking actions. Do not add camera-based fatigue detection, collect EMG/medical data, prescribe break schedules, or make safety/clinical claims.

The 38-person controlled experiment informs ergonomic study design, not a medical or product guarantee; hardware weight, interaction modes and acceptance gates remain unchanged.

## 69. 追加調査：Rockstarの2026 Mod GuidelinesはCreator Platform外のcommercial moddingを許可しない（2026-10-17 / RQ14、RQ13、Commercial/IP gate）

Rockstar's official [Mod Guidelines](https://www.rockstargames.com/community-resources/mod-guidelines?pubDate=20260921) page is dated Sep 10, 2026 (indexed official localizations display the same date). It says multiplayer modding must use Rockstar's official licensed Creator Platform unless separately licensed by Rockstar; unless permitted via its official Creator Platform, all modding must be strictly non-commercial; the Creator Platform is the exclusive licensed home for monetizing mods. It also prohibits real-money gambling/cash-out, paid loot-box/gacha/chance mechanics, selling in-game virtual currency for real money, cryptocurrency, third-party ads/sponsored content in mods, and charging access to Rockstar-created game assets/missions/DLC. Rockstar Support's [single-player mod policy page](https://support.rockstargames.com/articles/5NVOAYjcTomO8v6SX2k76k/pc-single-player-mods) and [RP server page](https://support.rockstargames.com/articles/5I66kExWgligszgMCU3XC1/roleplay-rp-servers), both last updated Sep 15, 2026, route to the current Creator Platform terms. I opened the linked, [6-page Creator Platform License Agreement, last updated Sept 10, 2026](https://fivem.net/terms), directly; the following details come from that primary document rather than news coverage. Cfx's [FiveM Tebex setup documentation](https://docs.fivem.net/docs/server-manual/setting-up-a-tebex-store/) calls Tebex the authorized/exclusive monetization partner for FiveM/RedM and says other payment platforms violate its Platform License Agreement. The Creator PLA expressly defines Creator Services as FiveM and RedM; its Covered Games are only games Rockstar specifically authorizes for use with those services. Rockstar's public Mod Guidelines do not name GTA VI, and the PLA does not establish GTA VI Creator Platform eligibility/API/server support. It also does not specifically resolve a separate Avocado SaaS/SIM/cloud-access fee, a free bridge adapter, or RQ13 Wallet transfers.

Avocado RQ14 candidate: any FiveM/Creator Platform adapter or game-side UGC sample should remain a free, non-commercial fixture while specific game eligibility, author rights, Creator PLA, approved storefront flow and service-boundary are unresolved. Do not route mod purchase, paid game currency, chance-based reward, advertisement, or access to Rockstar IP through Avocado Wallet or a third-party checkout. If a permitted creator revenue path is later established, keep creator package revenue, Avocado service/SIM/cloud-usage charges, game currency, and Wallet's real-money ledger as separate contracts and states; do not imply Rockstar/Tebex approval of Avocado. GTA VI does not yet have confirmed creator API/server support in the reviewed public materials.

Creator PLA sections 3.1(1)–(8) prohibit: user real-money-to-server then user cash-out/transfer flows; paid chance mechanics; sale of game currency for real money; sale of Rockstar-created Virtual Items; third-party-created Server Content aggregated and sold/licensed to Server Admins for real money; third-party promotion/ads/sponsorship; and crypto. Section 3.1 says the list is non-exhaustive and separate express Rockstar terms are needed for otherwise prohibited commercial exploitation. Section 3.2 puts policy/legal compliance (consumer protection, ads, ecommerce, tax, subscriptions, personal data) on the commercial operator. Section 5.2 lets Rockstar designate required or exclusive ecommerce/payment/hosting/marketplace service providers and says those designations can change; a third-party transaction is not Rockstar's transaction. Do not infer that Avocado may offer a parallel paid UGC marketplace to FiveM server admins, or that labeling a purchase “service access” removes it from these limits.

Section 6 says Rockstar and Operator may provide (not must provide) marketplaces; Marketplace Creator grants a server-admin a limited license to incorporate content into that creator's own Custom Server and exploit it only as PLA permits. Marketplace Content cannot be resold standalone, redistributed, or used to develop/train/enhance/tune/source/promote Generative AI tools (§6.4(4)). If game-platform content is ever considered as Agent input or model training data, exclude Creator Platform Marketplace Content absent separate express rights; do not feed it to IP Studio/Patent AI/model training by default. Section 6.2 also allocates reasonable technical support for Marketplace Content to its creator and transaction/payment-processing support to Rockstar/Operator. Sections 7.1–7.3 warn Creator/Marketplace Content or servers can break after game/service updates or enforcement and a refund may not be received; section 7.4 does not require Rockstar to operate any marketplace and allows it to stop one. Avocado bridge evidence should therefore record game/build/resource/marketplace-license version, provenance, dependency and last verified time, keep authored source export separate from licensed runtime use, and provide recovery/support owner without promising platform continuity or refund.

The PLA §2.3 also requires Custom Server websites/listings/storefronts to prominently identify an operator and valid email and include a “not approved, sponsored, or endorsed by Rockstar” disclaimer. Section 2.4 forbids using Creator Services to distribute third-party-licensed musical works/samples or modify voice performances in Covered Games. This is relevant to Avocado's product cards and soundscape references: Rockstar participation, a game screenshot, radio preview, listing, Tebex checkout, or “official Creator Platform” does not imply that Rockstar endorses Avocado or separately licenses music/brand assets.

For FiveM specifically, Cfx's Tebex onboarding guide lists seller support contact, store description, payout wallet, identity verification, package setup, Cfx account association and Tebex review before go-live; it says the storefront partner handles tax/billing steps. RQ14 onboarding should model provider eligibility/identity-review/listing-review/store-live states separately from SDK install/test. These are Tebex's own FiveM seller steps and service description, not Avocado's KYC, payouts, tax, compliance promise, or GTA VI market gate.

RQ13 game-currency exchange remains a separate design/research-only gate; the guideline's real-money virtual-currency sales ban makes the exact game, balance type, direction, provider approval and transaction semantics critical. Do not enable any exchange, even if framed as user-generated content or service access, until the current signed terms explicitly authorize the exact path. No implementation, seller registration, money movement, contract, or rights approval was made.

## 70. 追加調査：FiveM client eventは非信頼入力、game UIは権限sourceにしない（2026-10-17 / Security、AI06、RQ13/14）

Cfx official [Secure Your Events](https://docs.fivem.net/docs/developers/server-security/) warns that cheats may let a client trigger events in any context, advises registering events only for the side that should receive them, and recommends validating money, state bags, inventory, player position, experience and permissions/roles against server-side values rather than client-editable values. Its [event documentation](https://docs.fivem.net/docs/scripting-manual/working-with-events/listening-for-events/) distinguishes local and networked event registration. [Resource manifest docs](https://docs.fivem.net/docs/scripting-reference/resource-manifest/resource-manifest/) distinguish client_script/server_script/shared_script and mark client-delivered files, while [full-screen NUI docs](https://docs.fivem.net/docs/scripting-manual/nui-development/full-screen-nui/) describe NUI as a resource-scoped webview with focus controls. Those docs are FiveM-specific and current; this is not a GTA VI API claim.

Cfx [Sandbox documentation](https://docs.fivem.net/docs/developers/sandbox/) adds another useful boundary: resource file writes are scoped to the same resource by default; writes outside resource folders and path traversal are blocked; `os.execute` and most child-process operations are blocked. Cross-resource filesystem, ConVar, unsafe worker/process capabilities require explicit configuration grants, with warnings that these overrides can be dangerous. Avoid asking users to grant these broad overrides just to connect an Avocado game UI; prefer a narrow server-side resource, named API/capability, scoped secret held server-side and auditable revocation.

Avocado bridge candidate: treat in-game UI/NUI and all client event payloads as untrusted requests. Game/server may propose `job_id`, task intent, user-visible selection and bounded parameters; OS/Sky/Zema broker must independently authenticate the user, validate target capability and scope, show estimate/permission/recipient/side effect, request explicit approval as required, then return a correlation-ID-bound status/result. Do not put Avocado credentials, Wallet authority, privileged server token, OS signing key or persistent recovery secret in client NUI/resource files. Server-side FiveM checks should independently enforce role, inventory, balance/position, rate limit and idempotency for gameplay side effects; a game script's successful response is not evidence of external payment/real action. Use allowlisted game actions and structured API, no arbitrary remote shell or generic network-to-local tool. Keep test/preview separate from publish, execution, send, purchase or balance transfer.

This aligns with Avocado's nontrusted planner/Broker and separated Wallet ledger requirements, but FiveM docs do not validate Avocado's security or authorize a Rockstar connector. No server, credentials, integration, permission policy or runtime was changed.


## 71. 追加調査：Creator PLAの任意Marketplaceと現行Cfx Marketplaceを混同しない（2026-10-17 / RQ14、commercial gate）

Cfx official [Finding Resources documentation](https://docs.fivem.net/docs/server-manual/finding-resources/) explicitly points server operators to an operating Cfx Marketplace and FiveM/RedM release forums for community resources. It says resources may be free or paid, have dependencies, and must be used at the buyer's own risk; Cfx disclaims responsibility/support for community-made resources and directs buyers to creator support. The official doc links directly to marketplace.cfx.re, although that marketplace site itself timed out during this research. This is direct evidence that a FiveM resource Marketplace is currently operating within the Cfx ecosystem, consistent with the Rockstar Creator PLA's discretionary Marketplace framework; the PLA does not obligate Rockstar/Operator to provide it, and scope still follows the agreement and current provider terms. Read §3.1(6) together with §6: the PLA defines a narrow Rockstar/Operator Marketplace service while separately restricting covered users from independently running a real-money third-party Server Content aggregator. The current Cfx channel does not authorize Avocado to operate one. It does not establish a GTA VI marketplace or authorization to aggregate creator works outside the defined FiveM/RedM services.

Avocado RQ14 candidate: keep platform/catalog identity explicit (Cfx Marketplace, Tebex storefront, forum release, Avocado directory), with seller identity, rights/terms, compatibility/version, dependencies, support party, moderation/withdrawal and price/transaction provider shown separately. A directory link, imported metadata or seller listing is not itself a license or permission to re-sell content. For any proposed Avocado listing or distribution, resolve whether it is merely linking, hosting metadata, delivering an artifact, or aggregating/reselling third-party content before the commercial/rights gate; do not infer that the current Cfx Marketplace's existence authorizes Avocado to operate a competing market. These are FiveM/RedM comparison points, not GTA VI integration authorization.

No marketplace, payment or creator account was opened; no seller, listing, contract, revenue share, commercial permission or product claim changed.


## 72. 追加調査：FiveM asset entitlement・version retention・Enhanced対応を別状態で追う（2026-10-17 / RQ14、SDK、Service Access）

Cfx official [Asset Escrow documentation](https://docs.fivem.net/docs/server-manual/asset-escrow/) describes encrypted/protected Lua/YFT/YDD/YDR assets, current support limits, and distribution through Tebex packages. Its operational states have useful edge cases: re-upload replaces the creator's prior version, but previous downloads remain usable by existing users; subscription expiry removes the asset from the buyer's account so it no longer starts; asset transfer to another creator account is unsupported; and NUI is not supported by escrow. The [FiveM for GTAV Enhanced migration guide](https://docs.fivem.net/docs/developers/legacy-vs-enhanced/) separately says Asset Escrow is not implemented for that version, and documents changed player-ID reuse plus a client-server networking change. Thus “FiveM-compatible” is too broad without the exact Legacy/Enhanced runtime/build and packaging method.

Avocado RQ14/SDK candidate: track artifact revision/hash, target OS/runtime/device build, dependencies, signed/published revision, user entitlement, download/installed copy, runtime compatibility, renewal/revocation and recovery/export separately. For expiry or revocation, tell user what is prevented at next launch, whether currently running tasks/data persist, what can be exported, and which fallback remains; do not confuse payment failure with immediate deletion or continued authorization. Preserve migration tests for ID recycling and client/server state, and avoid hidden non-portable escrow requirements in samples. Any restriction on users retaining installed software requires product-specific contract, safety/recovery design and applicable legal review; FiveM's model is a comparison, not a policy to copy.

Cfx docs may change, and they cover FiveM rather than GTA VI. No FiveM resource was installed; no Avocado entitlement, artifact, payment, runtime or contract changed.


## 73. 追加調査：Creator PLAはMarketplaceの買い切り・subscription license終了条件を分ける（2026-10-17 / RQ14、Service Access、IP/contract gate）

Creator PLA §6.3 specifies a limited but perpetual license for Marketplace Content acquired through a one-time fee; for subscription content, the license automatically terminates when the Server Admin ends the applicable subscription or the Marketplace Creator stops offering that subscription, whichever happens first. It is limited to incorporating content into the admin's own eligible Custom Server, and Rockstar's overriding termination/availability rights under the TOS remain. This differs in kind from Cfx Asset Escrow's operational rule that an expired subscribed asset is removed from the user's account and no longer starts (§72). It also differs from a generic marketplace product card: a price label alone cannot tell the user whether their license persists, whether a local/remote artifact remains, or whether the runtime still works after service changes.

RQ14/Avocado candidate: preserve offer version and exact license type/term, licensor and rights scope, server/device/runtime binding, renewal and termination event, retained local copy/export, continued-use rights, runtime authorization, support owner and fallback separately. Design a user-readable end-of-access state that says whether rights ended, cloud service stopped, cached artifact remains, local execution is permitted, or data export is due; do not infer these states from “subscription canceled” alone. A one-time perpetual license is not a promise of service uptime or cross-version compatibility. Creator PLA rights are bounded by Rockstar services and do not grant Avocado a right to resell, host, modify or use that content in AI.

The exact applicable agreement/version and Creator's ownership of the rights still govern each item. This comparison does not set Avocado license terms or service guarantee. No offer, subscription, license, payment, integration or policy changed.


## 74. 追加調査：サーバーruntimeのEOS warningとEOL接続停止は異なる（2026-10-17 / RQ14、SDK、support/recovery）

Cfx official [End of Support/End of Life guide](https://docs.fivem.net/docs/server-manual/end-of-support-end-of-life/) distinguishes a gray EOS warning (server remains joinable) from a red EOL warning (connection fails until server owner updates artifacts). Outdated server artifacts can continue to function while falling outside issue support; artifacts older than three months are no longer joinable through the server browser. The documented support interval is relative to subsequent releases, not an invariant absolute date. Therefore “works,” “supported,” and “discoverable/joinable” are three different statuses, even before a build is technically incompatible.

Avocado RQ14 candidate: in listings and connected Tool health views distinguish compatible/running, latest recommended, supported until date/release, warning-only, blocked at launch, and removed/deprecated; say what action belongs to provider vs user and keep a previous known-good version/rollback path when the rights and platform allow it. A status badge must reflect the exact runtime/device revision and evidence timestamp. A hard stop should preserve user work and recovery/export path where possible. Cfx release schedule and its three-month cutoff are examples for FiveM/RedM only, not Rockstar GTA VI dates or Avocado support policy. No runtime or update policy changed.

## 75. 追加調査：resource discovery・authorized checkout・seller approvalは別関門（2026-10-17 / RQ14、creator commercial gate）

Cfx official [Tebex setup guide](https://docs.fivem.net/docs/server-manual/setting-up-a-tebex-store/) calls Tebex the authorized monetization partner and exclusive monetization partner of FiveM, and says using another platform/payment provider violates the Creator PLA for that monetization path. It also requires seller email/details and identity verification, project/store setup, a server plugin/secret key, customer support contact, wallet, a package, Cfx account association and Tebex review before a store can go live. The separate [Finding Resources guide](https://docs.fivem.net/docs/server-manual/finding-resources/) directs buyers to Cfx Marketplace/forums to discover resources and directs paid-resource buyers to check whether seller checkout is via Cfx Marketplace or Tebex; Cfx says community resources are at user's own risk and support generally belongs with their creator. Marketplace directory visibility, authorized payment, seller verification, package approval, artifact entitlement and technical support thus are distinct claims/statuses. This reconciles §§69 and 71: an existing marketplace surface does not make every third-party marketplace or Avocado checkout authorized.

Avocado RQ14 candidate: separately record `listed/discoverable`, `seller verified`, `terms/rights reviewed`, `checkout provider authorized`, `transaction settled`, `entitlement granted`, `artifact installed/compatible`, `support owner known`, and `withdrawn/appeal`. Avoid implying that Avocado has conducted KYC, taken payment, guaranteed the resource, or earned Rockstar approval simply because a third-party listing is linked. Keep any integration secret server-side and scope it to one named provider. These are Cfx/FiveM procedures, not Avocado seller policy, a tax/compliance guarantee or GTA VI integration. No accounts, stores, secrets, listing or payment were configured.


## 76. 追加調査：Cfx listingの“free”は無条件利用・再配布自由を意味しない（2026-10-17 / RQ14、catalog/rights）

A current example, [Vapid Scout Classic on Cfx Marketplace](https://marketplace.cfx.re/packages/7328062-fivem-car-suv-scout-classic), displays a zero price and “FiveM” platform, but its listing terms limit use to licensed purchaser/subscriber servers with assets granted in the Cfx portal, bar asset reupload/resale/rebranding (with a distinct livery exception), and bar redistribution outside the covered servers. Its metadata also reports Game Build 3095 for some features, says subscription is optional, lists support, and directs a non-server-owner player to purchase/gift to the server owner. The page surfaces creator identity, reporting route, product details, installation, support contact, dev specs, terms and Cfx/Tebex merchant branding. This is one vendor's listing and not a standard required for every Cfx Marketplace product; the seller's claims themselves were not independently validated.

Avocado RQ14 candidate: treat `price = 0`, license scope, qualified user/server/device, portal entitlement, dependency/build requirement, redistribution permission, bundle/transfer, technical support, report/takedown and author contact as independent fields. A free sample can still be restricted to a named server/account or carry non-transfer/usage terms; conversely, a gift to a server owner changes delivery recipient but does not imply the payer gains server access or that a platform grants rights. Make clear which statement comes from platform verification and which is creator-authored metadata. Measure install success and post-install permission/compatibility comprehension, not clicks or zero-price conversion alone. No Cfx content imported into Avocado; no seller/license/price/terms changed.


## 77. 追加調査：FiveM server checkoutとTebex Creator resaleは会計・契約モデルが違う（2026-10-17 / RQ14、RQ15、Wallet）

Cfx's [FiveM Tebex setup docs](https://docs.fivem.net/docs/server-manual/setting-up-a-tebex-store/) describe a server monetization integration: a store owner creates game-server project/package, configures the integration secret server-side, passes identity/review and receives store tools. Separately, Tebex's [Creator Agreement](https://www.tebex.io/terms-creator-agreement) describes Tebex purchasing creator Digital Goods at a Trade Price and reselling them itself; that agreement says the Buyer sale contract is with Tebex, lists weekly itemized reports, allows creator support referrals, and sets special post-termination reserve/dispute terms. Tebex's [creator payments guide](https://docs.tebex.io/creators/tebex-checkout/how-payments-work) describes a different payout-stage view: payment, fees, settlement in a Tebex Wallet, withdrawal request and arrival to PayPal/bank, with payment-method variability. These three sources describe distinct roles and must not be collapsed into one generic “Tebex gets 15%/pays after N days” rule. The setup page points to currently variable policies/fees and the payout guide can change; this record deliberately does not copy monetary rates or promise timelines.

Avocado RQ14/RQ15/Wallet candidate: model each explicit commercial arrangement independently and label who is buyer/seller/merchant/reseller/agent, where buyer contract is made, who handles support/tax/refunds, what funds enter which provider wallet, itemized deductions and settlement hold, creator payable/accrual, dispute reserve, eligible withdrawal, request/approval, transfer-in-flight and confirmed receipt. Creator balance, platform ledger and Avocado's real-fund Wallet are not fungible; no “earned,” “paid,” or “withdrawable” label should skip those states. For any later integration, keep provider id/terms version, effective date, country/currency, fee table, payout method and dispute eligibility evidence attached to the specific flow and re-check at runtime. Tebex conditions do not authorize Avocado to act as reseller, seller of record, payment provider, or money transmitter and do not set RQ15 fees or RQ13 game exchange. No seller account, payments, payout, Wallet ledger or fees were changed.


## 78. 追加調査：payment test・manual grant・production entitlementは混ざらない（2026-10-17 / RQ14、RQ13、Security、verification）

Tebex official [How to Test a Package](https://docs.tebex.io/creators/tebex-control-panel/how-to-create-packages/how-to-test-a-package) distinguishes Store Test Mode from Manual Payments. Test Mode simulates a checkout without charging and triggers webhooks and Sales statistics, but not Earnings; Tebex says to use it only while a store is private because a public test-mode store could let unintended users buy for free. Manual Payments grant/deliver a package without checkout, but do not trigger webhooks or Sales/Earnings statistics; the linked [Manual Payments guide](https://docs.tebex.io/creators/tebex-control-panel/payments-overview/manual-payments) says FiveM Asset access cannot later be revoked and deleting the manual payment does not undo in-game delivery. For FiveM assets, Tebex recommends testing with a subscription-based package and a gift card/coupon covering the initial cycle, so renewal does not continue automatically. These are markedly different fixtures; a mocked/manual grant is not proof of paid purchase or revenue.

RQ14 / Security candidate: label test transaction, manual grant, preview entitlement, paid transaction, refund, revoked access and runtime delivery as distinct; store explicit test-mode flag, environment, test actor, artifact revision and teardown/cleanup outcome. Keep free test mode private with access control and a deadline. Before emitting irreversible gameplay or externally visible actions, validate the signed transaction event and current entitlement at the trusted server/broker; never trust client UI success or a manual grant record as a cash payment. Do not use a production wallet or real game balance to test. A test assertion should verify compensation/revocation if available; where the provider cannot revoke, use disposable identities/resources or provider-recommended reversible setup and never apply test mode to a public store.

This is Tebex behavior as documented for its creator packages/FiveM assets, not Avocado or GTA VI evidence. No store was opened and no test transaction, manual grant, checkout, webhook, account or asset was created.

## 79. 追加調査：署名付きwebhookも再送・取消・精算状態を持つ（2026-10-17 / Security、RQ13、Wallet）

Tebex official [Webhook Overview](https://docs.tebex.io/developers/webhooks/overview) documents endpoint validation before delivery; recommends checking sender addresses and verifying its HMAC `X-Signature` over the raw request body. It expects a 2xx response and retries other results, then marks delivery failed after several attempts and may require endpoint validation again. The event catalog distinguishes payment.completed/declined/refunded, dispute opened/won/lost/closed, recurring-payment started/renewed/ended, cancellation.requested and cancellation.aborted. For recurring payments, a cancellation request takes effect at the end of the billing period; the `ended` event is emitted then. The page exposes transaction, product, price, fees, user, status and settled_at fields, but a notification is not itself evidence that the system fulfilled its duty, that money has settled or a creator withdrawal arrived.

Avocado bridge candidate: authenticate webhook source/signature, persist event ID/provider transaction ID and exact raw-body hash, process idempotently, deduplicate retries, reconcile against provider state, expose endpoint delivery failures/revalidation, and keep `payment requested → provider completed → entitlement granted → item delivered → settled → withdrawable → payout requested → received` separate. Treat payment.refunded or dispute-lost as corrective ledger events rather than deleting history. A valid signature means the provider sent the event; it does not promote the game/client event to trusted instruction or user authorization. Tebex's event schema and policies can change; this informs a candidate test plan only, no Avocado schema/runtime/API change.
