## 80. 追加調査：payment webhookの個人情報をgame/clientへ横流ししない（2026-10-17 / Security、Privacy、RQ13/14）

The payment subject example in Tebex's [webhook documentation](https://docs.tebex.io/developers/webhooks/overview) includes buyer first/last name, email, IP address, platform username/ID, marketing-consent flag, country/postal code, product variables/username, payment method, fees and transaction state. This is an example schema, not proof that every event carries every field or that all fields are available to each project. Still, it shows why a payment integration payload should be treated as sensitive data. A Tebex login-verification endpoint separately receives username/IP/country, and a Tebex plugin integration secret is distinct from the FiveM client or resource UI.

Avocado Security/Privacy candidate: verify provider signature first, then map the event server-side into a narrow envelope (opaque provider transaction ID, product/revision/entitlement, status, amount/currency if needed, event timestamp, consent-independent action). Keep buyer contact, exact IP, location and tax/support details in the minimum component that needs them; don't echo them to NUI/client scripts, game broadcasts, analytics, creator co-authors, generic AI prompts or rooms. Marketing-consent is provider-specific and not implied by purchase, playtest, report submission or telemetry opt-in. Attach purpose/access/retention/deletion to each retained field, and avoid raw webhook bodies in routine logs. A signature authenticates sender/integrity, not purpose, consent or access to unrelated Avocado workflows.

Tebex example payloads and data practice are not Avocado's legal basis or a decision about processing roles. No payment payload, customer data, secrets, recording or sensor input was collected in this research.


## 81. 追加調査：listing停止・paid entitlement revoke・test grant cleanupは別操作（2026-10-17 / RQ14、Service Access、recovery）

Tebex's current [package creation guide](https://docs.tebex.io/creators/tebex-control-panel/how-to-create-packages/how-to-create-a-package), updated recently, says a package can be one-time, recurring or both, and offers timed “Remove After” access revocation for FiveM/RedM Assets on one-time and subscription transactions. The guide separately says disabling a package prevents new subscriptions/sales but does not affect existing subscriptions. A trial collects payment details up front but charges at trial end. This supplements the Escrow guide's subscription expiry behavior and is distinct from Tebex's [test-payment guidance](https://docs.tebex.io/creators/tebex-control-panel/how-to-create-packages/how-to-test-a-package), which says assets delivered by Manual/Test Payments cannot be revoked and recommends a coupon-funded subscription test instead. Surface/offer state, new purchase eligibility, current buyer's entitlement, duration/renewal, runtime execution, and whether this was a real or non-revocable test grant are not interchangeable.

Avocado RQ14 candidate: provide separate controls/results for hide/unlist, stop new sales, cancel renewal, end access now, scheduled expiry, refund, dispute revocation, and delete package; before each action show impacted existing users, date, data/export effects and reversibility. Make `test` and `production` hard-separated environments, tag every grant with source and revoke capability, and test revocation from the actual issuance path before calling an entitlement reversible. A product disappearing from search must not silently erase active paid access; a successful revoke must not imply customer refund or erase owed creator funds.

Tebex behavior may change and the package guide/asset escrow docs cover provider-specific terms. No Avocado listing or access state changed.


## 82. 追加調査：Cfx Marketplaceの掲載はapproved partner・審査・USD価格の状態を持つ（2026-10-17 / RQ14、creator market）

Tebex's [Cfx Marketplace seller guide](https://docs.tebex.io/creators/growth-and-success/cfx-marketplace) confirms the Cfx Marketplace is a curated, live discovery channel for FiveM assets. Only approved Tebex Partners can submit; Cfx reviews assets and may request changes, materially edited listings can be re-reviewed, bundles require every included package to have been individually approved, and Marketplace listing adds no separate listing fee/commission. Listings need Creator profile/support URL, categories/collection, subtitle/tags, media, deliverables, price and a separate USD Marketplace price. Marketplace removes some package restrictions/custom variables, regional pricing and upsells; sales are global percentage discounts only, with no fixed-amount or region/customer-specific discount. Marketplace purchases appear in Tebex statistics and use usual settlement times. “No additional listing fee/commission” does not mean a sale is cost-free: Tebex's separate [creator fee overview](https://docs.tebex.io/creators/pricing-overview/tebex-fees-and-billing-overview) describes platform and payment-gateway fees, with method/store variation and withdrawal costs. This confirms §§71/75's key distinction: directory existence is real, yet seller eligibility, approval, listing and sale execution are all separate.

Avocado RQ14 candidate: give directory providers explicit onboarding and review state (`not eligible → partner eligible → submitted → changes requested/approved → listed → edited/re-review → delisted`), show creator profile/support route, artifact/dependency and license details, and keep listing fee/transaction commission/processing/tax/settlement regional rules separately. An approved listing says a platform reviewed submission against its own criteria; it does not prove IP title, safety, compatibility or fit for the buyer. If localization is unavailable, disclose when price or promotions apply globally and don't imply channel-specific pricing. Cfx/Tebex rules are mutable and not Avocado product settings; no creator account/listing, pricing, fee or promotion changed.


## 83. 追加調査：Cfx resource manifestが列挙するgame APIはGTA5/RedMのみ（2026-10-17 / RQ14、integration capability gate）

Cfx's official [Resource Manifest reference](https://docs.fivem.net/docs/scripting-reference/resource-manifest/resource-manifest/) gives `game` values `common`, `gta5`, and `rdr3`: `common` resources cannot access game-specific APIs, `gta5` means FiveM and `rdr3` means RedM. Cfx's current setup documentation also requires an installed, updated GTAV for a FiveM client. The current public Cfx manifest schema does not list GTA VI. This establishes the boundary of the documented current API and package; it cannot establish future compatibility or prove that Rockstar will never provide another runtime. Along with the Creator PLA's Covered Game scope, the current public material reviewed does not support installing a FiveM resource or calling a GTA VI creator API today.

Avocado RQ14 integration candidate: list an adapter only with a platform/game API identifier, supported title/build/channel, client/server side, test fixture, current terms, permission grant and verified last-run evidence. Unknown title gets `not_supported_or_unverified` and browser/OS-client route; do not infer API from common scripts, similar game code, shared publisher or trailer UI. Keep any future GTA VI work behind an explicit license/API/console deployment and Rockstar/Store review gate. This updates an evidence boundary only, not the Avocado launch/platform plan.

Cfx schema can change and is not proof about future GTA VI; no resource/server/connector or console account was configured.


## 84. 追加調査：FxDKのlocal build/previewはrollback・secret packaging・player identityに境界がある（2026-10-17 / RQ14、SDK、Security）

Cfx's official [FxDK docs](https://docs.fivem.net/docs/fxdk/) describe a single-user local development environment with editor, game/server consoles and resource monitor, automatic resource restart on edits, a visual World Editor and “one-click” server builds. The [Project Building guide](https://docs.fivem.net/docs/fxdk/project-building/) versions previous server artifacts/resources for rollback by default, but permits disabling that behavior; a disabled old resource folder is permanently deleted. It says Windows artifacts are currently built directly, while Linux deployment needs manual artifact handling. The build's `variables.cfg` can include Steam WebAPI and Tebex secrets and is regenerated at build; the public-server license key is intentionally not entered in FxDK UI for security reasons and is instead manually placed in a non-overwritten `server.cfg`. FxDK has a password-style convar field, but its resource docs warn that actual secrets must not be configured as informational or replicated values. [Resource docs](https://docs.fivem.net/docs/fxdk/resources/) warn that player identifiers are unavailable in FxDK, so locally previewed workflows cannot validate production identity. They also allow declared `fxdk_watch_command` and `fxdk_build_command` to execute tools such as Yarn or dotnet, while project builds may copy the entire resource (including source) unless `.fxdkignore` applies.

Avocado RQ14 candidate: use local preview for task iteration, not multi-author or production acceptance; show exactly which differences the fixture lacks (identity/permissions/network/services) and run separate identity/entitlement/security tests. Provide immutable successful builds, explicit rollback, target OS/runtime build matrix and package contents report. Never bundle service, signing, payment, WebAPI or recovery secrets into build artifacts; test with dummy credentials and scan the full output. Treat creator-supplied build/watch scripts as privileged code to inspect and execute only in isolated resource-limited build environment, with declared inputs/outputs and approval; don't make arbitrary shell execution a generic Tool API. These conclusions are Avocado candidates; FxDK is FiveM-specific, single-user and not a GTA VI toolchain.

No FxDK project/resource/secret was created or run. No Avocado build/development mode changed.


## 85. 追加調査：GTA VIの公式音楽導線とファン動画の非商用範囲を混同しない（2026-10-17 / RQ14、IP/rights、creator publishing）

Rockstar's current [GTA VI music page](https://www.rockstargames.com/VI/music) has hand-picked station previews, a standalone Justice score single, selected album tracks, and separate pre-save/physical purchase links; it says the album has 34 original tracks and lists six radio previews plus nine album singles. This is a useful reference for separating discovery/preview from licensed playback and merchandise checkout. It does not grant Avocado rights to host or repackage tracks, use game audio in generated creator assets, or imply that game radio can be exported.

Rockstar Support's [copyrighted-material posting policy](https://support.rockstargames.com/articles/7bNaeoMFTV0iUDGhStTXvz/policy-on-posting-copyrighted-rockstar-games-material), last updated Jan. 10, 2025, describes a general tolerance for occasional fan use that is non-commercial and non-spoiling. It explicitly defines non-commercial to exclude making money from footage or using it to promote a product/service; it also says this is not a comprehensive grant and does not cover advertising or digital publishing. A paid Avocado page or a product-promotional creator video therefore should not treat the fan policy as blanket clearance. Get specific rights or use independently cleared footage/assets; preserve a simple route to the official Rockstar page instead of mirroring its audio/media.

Platform rules are another layer: the current [PlayStation Software EULA](https://www.playstation.com/en-us/legal/software-eula/) grants SIE a broad license when UGC is shared through an enabled feature, requires the poster to control rights, and treats game virtual content as licensed within a closed virtual universe. Microsoft's current [Xbox GDK UGC certification guidance](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/gaming/gdk/docs/store/policies/xr/xr018?view=gdk-2604) requires in-product report/removal processes and disclosure when integrated third-party mod-platform content is not developer-sourced; it separately excludes platform-managed capture clips from the title UGC definition. These are platform/title compliance contexts, not evidence that GTA VI itself has a creator-sharing feature, not an Avocado license, and not a substitute for third-party music rights.

Avocado RQ14/IP Studio candidate: track asset/clip source, game/platform, capture-vs-upload method, embedded music/third-party rights, scope of creator permission, allowed commercial use, attribution, remix/model-training grant, platform-specific UGC grant and takedown/report state separately. Prefer external official links for music discovery, and test that a creator can tell which material they may monetize before publish. The cross-platform comparison does not set Avocado legal terms or authorize GTA VI integration. No GTA VI media was copied, no creator content was published, and no rights or product policy changed.


## 86. 追加調査：Asset StoreのAI開示・consent・quality reviewを独立したlisting fieldsにする（2026-10-17 / RQ14、IP Studio、AI governance）

Unity's current [Asset Store Terms](https://unity.com/legal/as-terms) prohibit use of the store or assets to train AI/ML without express provider and/or Unity consent. Its [Submission Guidelines](https://assetstore.unity.com/publishing/submission-guidelines), updated May 20, 2026, require plain-language AI-description disclosure of the tools and generated/assisted content and what human changes add value; they can reject AI assets that resemble third-party work or lack usability/value and reserve rejection for mass-produced assets lacking differentiation. They also require third-party notices and disclosure of external API key handling, terms/costs, with analytics opt-in and opt-out. These obligations are specific to Unity's catalog and do not establish Rockstar or Avocado rules.

RQ14/IP Studio candidate: separate creator provenance (human/AI/tool/version), which asset portions were generated, post-generation edits, rights source/third-party notices, permission to display/sell/remix, explicit training consent and model/data scope, external API and cost disclosure, analytics choice, moderation/review outcome, and compatibility/support evidence. A creator may allow sale while withholding AI-training consent; publishing an item is not consent to train on it. Review should evaluate safety, distinctiveness and declared function as different checks. No Avocado listing fields or AI policy were changed.


## 87. 追加調査：creator payoutのestimate・actual・return・eligibilityは別状態（2026-10-17 / RQ14、RQ15、Wallet）

Epic's current [Fortnite Account Level Monetization guide](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/fortnite/account-level-monetization-in-fortnite) separates engagement and in-island transaction earnings in different dashboard tabs. It labels daily values as estimates or actuals; recent estimates can lag/be inaccurate, are not guaranteed, and may appear when the creator is ineligible. Monthly payouts post about 30 days after period end, returns/cancellations can be deducted from later payouts, and the documented USD threshold is $100 within a 12-month window. In-island purchases use a V-Bucks-to-USD conversion affected by source channel fees, bundle mix, earned/discounted currency, FX and regional pricing. The currently documented creator transaction share is temporarily 100% from Jan-Dec 2026 versus the ordinary 50%; these rates and qualifying conditions are mutable and provider-specific. Engagement Pool payments remain a separate program from Support-A-Creator affiliate commissions and direct island transactions.

Avocado RQ14/RQ15/Wallet candidate: make estimates visibly non-payable, attach eligibility/version and calculation period, show currency conversion inputs and data latency, record return/dispute reserve adjustments as separate ledger events, and distinguish accrual, confirmed payable, minimum-threshold carryover, withdrawal request, provider settlement, and confirmed receipt. Never use view/engagement estimates as Wallet funds or apply a temporary/platform-specific split as Avocado's standard rate. This is comparison only; no payout rule, Wallet balance or fee changed.


## 88. 追加調査：Fabはlicense tier・source format・project redistributionを分けて提示（2026-10-17 / RQ14、catalog/SDK、IP）

Epic's [Fab documentation](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/fab/fab-documentation?lang=en-US) describes an asset marketplace spanning Unreal, Unity and multiple 3D formats. The displayed [Standard License summary](https://www.fab.com/eula?lang=en) allows commercial use, modification into a project, commercial project distribution and sharing with collaborators on that project, while barring standalone asset resale/redistribution; it says Personal and Professional tiers have the same scope, whereas Reference-Only exposes a referenced asset rather than source format. The page expressly labels that summary non-binding and points to the full EULA as controlling, so it is a comparison signal rather than a definitive interpretation of a specific purchase. Fab's publisher guide separately advertises an 88% share, ~$100 payout threshold/monthly timing and refund deductions; those are dynamic provider terms, not rates for Avocado.

Avocado RQ14 candidate: list what the user receives (source vs reference/runtime), compatible tools, permitted project modification, collaborator access, redistribution inside a compiled project vs standalone package, attribution/third-party notices, warranty/support, and exact license text/version as independent fields. Avoid a single “commercial use allowed” badge that hides redistribution and source-format limits; link the controlling grant before acquisition. No Fab asset, license, seller account, fee, payout or Avocado catalog policy changed.


## 89. 追加調査：bundle metadataは権利制限を集約し生成由来を引き継ぐ（2026-10-17 / RQ14、IP Studio、catalog composition）

Fab's current [bundle documentation](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/fab/bundles) says bundled listings roll up selected metadata: if any constituent does not allow generative-AI use, the bundle does not allow it; if any was created with AI, the bundle is labeled Created With AI; mature and promotional-content flags also inherit. Bundle-level AI settings are derived from constituents rather than manually editable. Changing Created-With-AI or AI-permission fields on a bundled product can automatically unlist the bundle until re-publication. Fab's [listing-update guide](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/fab/publishing-assets-for-sale-or-free-download-in-fab?lang=en-US) says AI-use selections can be changed without review, while an existing buyer's downloaded UEFN source stays at its prior version until they download the update. Unlisting/deleting a Fab listing blocks discovery/new acquisition but prior buyers can still download; a quarantine is a distinct state that removes it from buyers' libraries. These are specific catalog behaviors, not evidence that every platform or every AI use is permitted.

Avocado RQ14/IP Studio candidate: when composing a pack/bundle, calculate effective permissions conservatively (all included grants must allow the operation), aggregate provenance as “contains AI-assisted/generated content” if any component does, preserve per-component origin/source/version, and show which item restricts a requested use. A metadata change should update cached bundle cards/search indexes, show existing buyers what changed, and trigger the appropriate re-review/unlisting/sync state; downloaded historical copies remain identifiable. Do not let a bundle inherit a broader grant than any of its contents. No listing/bundle/AI policy or asset changed.


## 90. 追加調査：Rockstar公認のGTA RP協業はGTAVの対象partner/access条件つき（2026-10-17 / RQ14、integration claims）

Rockstar's official [Newswire announcement](https://www.rockstargames.com/newswire/article/17857581o753k1/introducing-nopixel-v?pubDate=20260901) says it supports NoPixel V, describes its changes to the GTAV map and GTA Roleplay community, and announced an invited-player closed beta through Rockstar Games Launcher, with broader access/improvements only as future updates. NoPixel Studios' [official site](https://www.nopixel.net/?post=development-update-1-gtav-map) calls the release a GTA RP experience developed in collaboration with Rockstar. This is evidence of a specific GTAV partner route with a named service, launcher, invite cohort and stage. It does not establish a general Rockstar creator program, a public Avocado connector, a GTA VI mode/API, or guaranteed broad access.

Avocado RQ14 candidate: record endorsement/authorized partner, covered title/build, integration/API rights, launcher/client route, access cohort, beta/production status, eligible region, and expansion date as separate claims with source and timestamp. A press announcement about one partner must not populate other titles' compatibility badges. No Rockstar credential, NoPixel account, GTA install, or game integration was used.


## 91. 追加調査：Creator Storeの商品名・素材・宣伝画像にも第三者ブランドの権利証拠が必要（2026-10-17 / RQ14、IP、go-to-market）

Fab's official [Publisher Get Started guide](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/fab/publisher-get-started-in-fab?lang=en-US) says products cannot contain or be marketed using copyrighted/trademark names, branding or content (including game titles/logos/characters) unless the publisher owns or adequately licenses those rights; Fab may notify prior buyers if an IP violation causes removal. This aligns with Rockstar's fan-content policy's distinction between occasional non-commercial fan posts and promotion of a product/service (§85), but applies under Fab's own marketplace rules. Neither text alone determines how a specific nominative compatibility reference is treated.

Avocado RQ14 candidate: make creator identify the rights basis for each game title/logo/character/footage/sound used in the downloadable asset, thumbnail, trailer, listing copy and ads; separate a factual target-runtime compatibility field from artwork/branding that suggests endorsement. Provide an appeal/report path and preserve purchaser notice/remedy evidence if a listing is removed. This is an IP intake/review candidate, not legal advice, new terms, or a GTA VI publishing license. No branded content was uploaded or changed.


## 92. 追加調査：Steamはpre-generatedとlive-generated AIを分け、継続費用の選択肢を明示（2026-10-17 / RQ14、AI06、usage pricing）

Valve's current [Steamworks Content Survey](https://partner.steamgames.com/doc/gettingstarted/contentsurvey) says its AI disclosure concerns content made with AI that ships and is consumed by players, rather than general behind-the-scenes efficiency tooling. It separates pre-generated shipped content from live-generated content; a developer must describe guardrails against illegal output for the latter, and prerelease review evaluates AI content under the same legal/marketing consistency promises as other content. Once a build/store page is released, some survey answers require contacting Steam Support to change. Valve's FAQ also says a live external AI service with ongoing per-input/output costs can be handled in base price, granular microtransactions, subscription or DLC, using Steam-supported payment methods. These are Steam-specific publishing and payment rules, not a general AI certification or Avocado monetization rule.

Avocado RQ14/AI06 candidate: record whether content was created before sale or generated at runtime, what users consume, generator/provider/version, runtime safeguards, moderation/reporting route, cost estimate and cap, and disclosures shown at acquisition/use. Keep behind-the-scenes AI assistance distinct from the content rights the creator grants; changing a disclosed AI feature after release should trigger affected listing/terms and customer-notice review. For Rockstar/Avocado creator content, do not assume a general usage fee/Steam choice clears game IP, model rights or platform checkout rules. No service cost, game build, listing, checkout or rights policy changed.


## 93. 追加調査：Workshop mod versionとgame branchを組にしてknown-good buildを選ぶ（2026-10-17 / RQ14、SDK、recovery）

Valve's [Steam Workshop Item Versioning guide](https://partner.steamgames.com/doc/features/workshop/itemversioning) says subscriptions normally receive the latest uploaded mod and subsequent updates propagate automatically. A game can enable explicit supported-game-branch ranges per mod version; the uploader or author supplies min/max compatible branches, and Steam can download the mod version matching the player's selected branch. Valve specifically warns that a game auto-update may break installed mods and corrupt saves. Its recommended pattern is to maintain ordered old game branches, beta-test a new public build ahead of release, let mod authors update and mark compatible branches, then offer a choice to remain on the last-known-good game/mod set. The Workshop integration also supports a private tester-only visibility state.

Avocado RQ14/SDK candidate: model artifact version ↔ exact host/game/runtime build range, publish candidate to a test cohort, verify dependency/saved-data migration, then stage rollout and preserve rollback pair; show the user when an update may invalidate installed tools or saved work. Author-declared compatibility and uploader-verified compatibility are distinct evidence. This is a Steam opt-in integration feature, not GTA VI support or a universal versioning API. No Steam Workshop item, branch, save or game was changed.


## 94. 追加調査：UGC公開は通報・制限設定・第三者由来の表示を一つの運用にする（2026-10-17 / RQ14、moderation、privacy）

The current [Xbox GDK XR-018](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/gaming/gdk/docs/store/policies/xr/xr018?view=gdk-2604) defines online-visible player-created maps, mods, images, sounds, videos and other assets as UGC and requires in-product report/removal or proactive detection, published guidelines, and respect for users whose UGC privilege is restricted. When a third-party mod platform is integrated, it calls for the platform complaint API where available and a clear signal that content is not developer-sourced. It also recommends a way to disable all UGC if combinations can make the game unresponsive. These are current Xbox requirements, not a GTA VI feature list or a universal law. The [PlayStation Software EULA](https://www.playstation.com/en-us/legal/software-eula/) separately says UGC may be a feature and, when shared, carries an SIE license and rights warranty; that platform agreement does not grant Avocado rights to reuse the same UGC elsewhere.

Avocado RQ14 candidate: publish the content rules and show source/platform/creator at consumption; handle report → temporary visibility control → review → restore/remove → creator notice/appeal as a trackable state sequence; propagate platform restriction settings and retain minimal evidence needed for review. Keep an independent kill switch for unsafe/incompatible extensions and make it reversible for the user. Platform capture video, game-generated content, and third-party uploaded media need separate source/rights paths. This would help make creator publishing operate responsibly if Avocado ever has a supported UGC destination; it is not evidence GTA VI has mods or creator API. No UGC was submitted, removed or inspected, and no moderation or privacy setting changed.


## 95. 追加調査：GTA Online 1.73は作例・目的単位テスト・保存gate・推薦までCreatorを更新（2026-10-17 / RQ14、authoring feedback loop）

Rockstar's [GTAV Title Update 1.73 notes](https://support.rockstargames.com/articles/4vRqEDvjUs9h7nqRUgc8YO/gtav-title-update-1-73-notes-ps5-ps4-xbox-series-x-or-s-xbox-one-pc-enhanced-legacy), dated October 1, 2026, list a new example mission demonstrating tools; testing from any chosen Objective; toggling Mission Flags during Test Mode; and preventing a save while the mission exceeds a memory limit, with one-entity overage allowed until corrected. The patch adds further signals of complexity management: a 60-object shared cap, cutscene and interaction controls, and nomination of up to 16 player-created Jobs for the Next Job vote. Rockstar's [Mission Creator introduction](https://www.rockstargames.com/newswire/article/39a3412379o79o/play-thrilling-new-gta-online-experiences-built-by-the-community-with?pubDate=20260430) explicitly recommends learning from fully playable Rockstar examples, sharing a working prototype for peer feedback before publishing, and tagging jobs for possible editorial featuring.

The same cumulative patch history includes dozens of Mission Creator reliability fixes, spanning task selection/order, assigned starting inventory, Actor navigation, checkpoint respawns, objective skipping and memory-bar display. Its July 30 entry specifically fixed a defect where Prop spawn options went missing when returning to the editor from Test Mode. This shows the test loop itself—entering gameplay, retaining the author's edits and restoring editing controls—is a product-quality surface. The changelog is cumulative across platforms and dates; not every fix applies to every SKU/build, so it should not be read as one day-one defect list.

Avocado RQ14 authoring candidate: give each workflow both runnable reference examples and a short editable starter, let creators test from the failing step instead of replaying the whole flow, preserve draft/edit state while entering and leaving a test run, surface resource-limit causes before save/publish, and collect small reversible votes/nominations separately from editorial approval. Add regressions for editor-test-editor round trips and edit-state integrity, not only successful task execution. When the system evolves, document patch behavior as user-visible changes, compatibility warnings and regression fixes—not just feature names. This is live creator-product evidence from GTA Online (not GTA VI), and nomination/featuring is not a guaranteed payout. No Creator was launched or edited.


## 96. 追加調査：ストアの「offline可」と一部機能の接続・account条件は併記する（2026-10-17 / RQ14、access states）

The official [PlayStation product listing](https://www.playstation.com/en-us/games/grand-theft-auto-vi/) currently labels GTA VI “Offline play enabled” and “1 player,” with Remote Play supported and optional in-game purchases. By contrast, the [Xbox listing](https://www.xbox.com/en-US/games/store/grand-theft-auto-vi/9NL3WWNZLZZN/) describes a single-player experience but states that some features/content/services/functions may require Internet, Rockstar Games account registration, a serial code, fees, or gameplay unlocks, and may change or terminate under the applicable terms. Xbox capability metadata also lists cloud saves. These statements are compatible if they describe different features: the store-level offline flag does not promise every ancillary service works offline, just as a cloud-save feature does not mean the core story requires a network session.

Avocado product/compatibility candidate: expose separate fields for base play/local access, named online feature, account/license requirement, external service dependency, cloud sync, and purchase entitlement, each with source and last-verified time. Show “not verified” rather than infer a complete offline mode from one store badge. For any Avocado integration, retry/queue actions safely when offline and make unsynced state, entitlement freshness and recovery path explicit. This is a platform listing comparison, not proof of all launch-day GTA VI behavior; no game or account was accessed.


## 97. 追加調査：新しいPC・Multiplayer報道は公式platform gateを越えない（2026-10-17 / RQ14、source confidence）

PC Gamer's [October 13 report](https://www.pcgamer.com/games/grand-theft-auto/report-claims-grand-theft-auto-6-will-launch-on-pc-sooner-than-anyone-expected/) summarizes a GamesBeat claim that PC launch is planned for Q1 2027, citing four unnamed people familiar with studio plans; it says Rockstar had not commented. PC Gamer's [September 17 report on a Bloomberg interview](https://www.pcgamer.com/games/grand-theft-auto/twitch-ceo-says-gta-6-multiplayer-will-launch-in-2027/) quotes Twitch CEO Dan Clancy predicting multiplayer next year after saying Twitch had discussed creator engagement with Rockstar; PC Gamer itself notes Rockstar has made no multiplayer/Online announcement and does not present Clancy as authorized to announce it. These may be signals to monitor, but they do not specify supported PC storefront/build, multiplayer dates, creator APIs, permitted platform, terms or rollout eligibility.

The current [official GTA VI page](https://www.rockstargames.com/VI) lists PlayStation 5 and Xbox Series X|S, with November 19, 2026 release date; Rockstar's [pre-order announcement](https://www.rockstargames.com/newswire/article/5171972o3ak5oa/pre-order-grand-theft-auto-vi-on-june-25) calls it a single-player experience. Avocado RQ14 candidate: keep claims at `official`, `reported_unconfirmed`, `community_speculation`, or `unknown`, with source, publisher/author, date, affected claim, next review trigger and expiry; do not turn confidence or a rumor into compatible platform, API, launch schedule, revenue, or authorization. A user-supplied trailer and a third party's roadmap story are media input, not integration credentials. No forecast, platform support or roadmap was changed.


## 100. 追加調査：UGCの公開範囲・対話・投稿資格・異議申立てを分離する（2026-10-17 / RQ14、trust & safety）

Rockstar Support's [privacy and UGC guidance](https://support.rockstargames.com/articles/6NKqPKffElhndfInHKOyeP/rockstar-games-privacy-settings-and-user-generated-content), updated February 24, 2026, says users can limit their profile, wall and some statistics, but Crew membership, comments made on another person's wall, and multiplayer data can remain visible. Blocking prevents direct communication but does not prevent the blocked player from viewing or commenting on UGC; a separate report action is available for offending comments. The [Mission Creator access guidance](https://support.rockstargames.com/articles/1lmcf3ZEFlNIYlOhwDpY6z/grand-theft-auto-online-mission-creator-unavailable) says prior terms/guideline violations may temporarily disable the Creator, Creator misuse may lead to suspensions or bans, and users may appeal enforcement for reevaluation. Rockstar also warns that reposting removed crew content may lead to account or content-creation restrictions in its [administrative changes guidance](https://support.rockstargames.com/articles/5iadkRru4fIbsjC1XGMxcY/administrative-changes-to-crew-names-emblem-or-details).

Avocado RQ14 candidate: model audience visibility, discovery, comments/DM eligibility, block, report, moderation status, creator/publishing eligibility, account access and appeal as independent capabilities. Explain exactly what a privacy or block control changes and what remains visible; let a user report content even if blocking it is a separate action. For sanctions, record scope, duration, reason category, evidence retention, user notice, review/appeal state, and whether repost attempts are restricted; avoid treating a report as a finding or removal as permission revocation everywhere. These are Rockstar service examples, not GTA VI-specific controls or mandatory Avocado policy. No user settings or enforcement state changed.


## 101. 追加調査：bundle特典・trial・renewal・refund・地域eligibilityは販売先ごとに追跡する（2026-10-17 / SIM/eSIM、service access、RQ15）

Rockstar's current [GTA VI edition and platform FAQ](https://support.rockstargames.com/articles/4QfG4FmZCf5W1gS8jy4UVT/grand-theft-auto-vi-platform-editions-and-versions-Platforms) says digital purchases before November 20 include a one-month GTA+ offer, while physical-box purchases contain a download code and not a disc; physical Standard buyers can later buy the Ultimate upgrade. Its [offer redemption FAQ](https://support.rockstargames.com/articles/3SZF8cy8dG7lHsLf38uWK1/redeeming-the-one-month-gta-membership-included-with-your-grand-theft-auto-vi-pre-order) says the free membership starts through a separate store redemption, with different deadlines by platform (March 31, 2027 on PlayStation; 180 days after purchase on Microsoft), then renews monthly at the normal rate unless canceled. Eligibility is once per platform account; refunding and repurchasing does not grant the free month again, while an existing member gets one month added to their next billing date. Rockstar's [purchase support FAQ](https://support.rockstargames.com/articles/40DHWEDBnq5jUuWJqKcH02/grand-theft-auto-vi-pre-order-purchase-support-faq) directs storefront purchases to the platform, Rockstar Store physical order support to Xsolla, and other-retailer purchases to that retailer. The edition FAQ also ties some PlayStation code redemptions to the country group on the platform account and advises buying in the account's country to avoid activation problems.

Rockstar's [pre-order announcement](https://www.rockstargames.com/newswire/article/5171972o3ak5oa/pre-order-grand-theft-auto-vi-on-june-25) describes GTA+ as membership for GTA Online and its games library; the GTA VI product itself is separately described as a single-player experience. A subscription bundled with a game can belong to another title/service in the publisher portfolio, so the bundle alone does not establish an online mode or a continuing feature for the purchased game.

Rockstar's [Vice City Collection listing](https://store.rockstargames.com/merchandise/gtavi-goodtime-state-vice-city-collection?drm=PlayStation+5) adds another explicit boundary: the $399.99 box details its physical items, shipping limits and adult collectible/small-parts warning, then states the GTA VI game is sold separately. A brand-level collection therefore connects discovery and cross-sell while keeping the physical collectible transaction distinct from the digital game entitlement.

Avocado SIM/eSIM and RQ15 candidate: represent the durable service entitlement, promotional trial eligibility, activation/redemption, renewal consent, billing account, cancellation date, refund/reversal and support owner as separate records. At purchase, show any trial's exact end date and renewal price and make the renewal action/consent auditable; make channel-specific deadlines, account/region checks, duplicate redemption and refund/chargeback effects visible before the user relies on access. Keep the carrier connectivity/SIM profile separate from Avocado cloud service access even when one checkout bundles them. These are retailer/platform mechanics, not an Avocado price, contract term or legal conclusion; no Avocado offer, billing or entitlement changed.


## 102. 追加調査：UGC safetyを報告件数だけでなく処理時間・人の関与・appeal reversalで測る（2026-10-17 / RQ14、safety operations）

Take-Two's [2025 DSA Transparency Report](https://ir.take2games.com/static-files/b7784109-bccb-48df-83f6-e76bdd37b2af) covers services where player-created content is exposed to other players, across Take-Two labels—not GTA VI alone. For 2025 it reports 7,994,904 player reports, 7,313,622 enforcement actions, and a 39.8-hour median action time. The report says every player report had human-moderator involvement at some stage. Its separate proactive moderation table records 528,506 enforcement decisions made solely by automated means and 186,474 not solely automated; this is a decision count, whereas Table 3's 907,485 total sums multiple actions that may stem from one decision. Where appeal was possible, 22,215 appeals were reported, with a 63.3-hour median decision time; the table shows 128 initial inaction decisions and 314 initial action decisions reversed. The report describes human review as a way to identify discrepancies and bias in machine-led detection/actions.

Avocado RQ14 candidate: instrument intake volume, triage/decision/action separately, median plus tail latency, escalation and human-review share, appeal eligibility, appeal latency, reversal rate, and repeat-offender outcomes. Keep one moderation decision linked to the multiple effects it triggers (hide content, suspend a feature, or suspend an account), and explain why a report did not result in a restriction without exposing another user's private data. Report rates and rates of enforcement are not identical quality measures; track false-positive/false-negative audits and abuse-report outcomes too. Take-Two's numbers cover a large, multi-label service ecosystem and its 2025 DSA reporting population; they are not GTA VI telemetry, Avocado staffing/SLAs, or recommended thresholds. No moderation workflow or target was changed.


## 103. 追加調査：サービスstatusを製品・platform・認証・store・community層ごとに分ける（2026-10-17 / SIM/eSIM、service access、reliability）

Rockstar's [live Service Status page](https://support.rockstargames.com/servicestatus?lang=en) lists current health by GTA Online platform (PS5, PS4, Xbox Series X|S, Xbox One, PC) and separately lists Online Services such as authentication, Launcher and Store, then a distinct Cfx group for authentication, FiveM, RedM, Community Servers and Marketplace. The page carries an “as of” timestamp, so “operational” is a time-stamped provider report, not proof that a particular user's console/network, login, entitlement or complete end-to-end flow is working. The GTA VI support hub separately routes pre-order questions by storefront and retailer, while the status table at the time of this review did not list a GTA VI multiplayer service.

Avocado reliability candidate: expose status at the layer that owns the failure—carrier/SIM profile, device client, identity/authentication, model provider, cloud execution, storage/sync, billing and marketplace—and timestamp each provider signal. Let diagnostics compare provider health to the user's actual client/network/account state before blaming a device or asking for repeated retries. Show known issue, last update, scope, current workaround and named support owner; mark the dependency unknown where no health signal exists. Do not infer a GTA VI online service from the game's release announcement or a Rockstar “all services operational” snapshot. This maps Rockstar's current service-status organization, not GTA VI service availability or an Avocado status requirement. No service was probed beyond reading the public status page, and no connection settings changed.


## 104. 追加調査：GTA VIのミッションは目標の必須条件と手順の選択肢を分ける設計（2026-10-17 / AI06、Zema、Agent execution）

In a full [IGN interview with Rockstar North co-studio lead Rob Nelson, quoted by GamesRadar+](https://www.gamesradar.com/games/grand-theft-auto/with-gta-6-missions-rockstar-has-made-a-very-conscious-effort-to-open-things-up/), Nelson says the team tries to open missions to multiple routes “where possible” while preserving story beats when they must guide the player. He gives two examples: more than one way out of a tower block, and a police chase whose suggested route is not mandatory; the mission reuses the same wanted-level/robbery systems as free roam so the systems do not feel inconsistent. This is a reported pre-release design interview, not a published technical specification or a claim about all missions.

Avocado AI06/Zema candidate (design inference only): Agent tasks can present a clear goal, fixed safety/approval/permission gates, a recommended tool path and acceptable alternatives. If the recommended path is unavailable or expensive, the Agent can propose another path that still meets the same user-approved outcome and limits, while asking again if an alternative changes the result, scope, data sharing or cost. Hard approvals remain gates; flexibility belongs in execution choice, not authority. Where one business workflow is available through Web, OS or a delegated Agent, keep the existing business state contract and fixture consistent across surfaces instead of granting a separate path extra authority. Evaluate task completion, user rework, unexpected side effects and whether agents respect invariants across alternate routes. A game mission's narrative constraints do not prove this is the right Avocado behavior; no task runtime, permission model or roadmap changed.


## 105. 追加調査：ゲーム内の音楽licenseと動画利用権は目的・媒体・地域で異なる（2026-10-17 / RQ14、IP/media provenance）

Rockstar Support's [Rockstar Editor music guidance](https://support.rockstargames.com/articles/3f8XH01Kodw4zKnwbF6yTB/information-about-being-safe-from-copyright-strikes-when-uploading-rockstar-editor-videos) says GTAV radio tracks included in the Rockstar Editor can be used in videos shared on YouTube without the use itself causing a copyright strike, but the track owners or labels may monetize the video or restrict it in some countries; instrumental score tracks are suggested to avoid those specific issues. This limited GTAV/Editor/YouTube guidance is separate from Rockstar's broader [game-content posting policy](https://support.rockstargames.com/articles/7bNaeoMFTV0iUDGhStTXvz/policy-on-posting-copyrighted-rockstar-games-material), which generally addresses occasional, non-commercial individual fan use and excludes some commercial streaming/film/publishing uses. It is not GTA VI music licensing or general permission for cross-platform commercial reuse.

Reopened Rockstar's game-content policy (last updated January 10, 2025): it defines non-commercial use as neither making money from posted gameplay footage nor using the material to promote a product/service, reserves takedown at any time, and says leaked pre-release in-game footage (including early unboxing) is subject to takedown regardless of how obtained. The policy allows some narrated playthrough context while separately identifying isolated cinematics and isolated in-game entertainment as takedown examples, and directs commercial licensing questions to case-by-case written requests with a detailed proposed use. This is a limited fan-policy statement, not a general copyright license or a GTA VI creator-program acceptance; the policy itself excludes broader commercial entertainment/publishing uses.

Avocado RQ14/media candidate: attach rights to the specific use—preview, in-product playback, social share, stream, commercial advertisement, creator submission—along with medium, territory, duration, monetization, rights-holder and effective date. For user uploads, capture whether the source is officially released, gameplay/cutscene/isolated cinematic, and the creator's proposed audience/commercial use; route unresolved combinations to a rights review rather than declaring them permitted from a general fan policy. A source asset may be playable in one authorized context while exported video receives a claim, revenue diversion or territorial block elsewhere. Offer rights-aware alternatives and let creators verify the target platform's current policy before publish; keep source links/catalog discovery separate from a reusable download/export license. These Rockstar-specific rules are examples, not legal conclusions or Avocado music policy. No media was exported or republished.


## 106. 追加調査：voice moderationの第三者処理・安全録音・限定保持を利用前に説明する（2026-10-17 / avocadoMini、privacy/security、MAT18）

Rockstar Support's [GTA Online voice moderation FAQ](https://support.rockstargames.com/articles/468kV5K1q7zWtaaviqNOV/voice-chat-moderation-in-gta-online-faq), updated February 4, 2026, says voice chat is monitored through Modulate for proactive moderation. It says some audio chat may be securely recorded and retained for a limited period when needed for player safety, that it is not sold or rented, and that moderation considers conversational context alongside Rockstar's Community Guidelines. The FAQ says “some” audio may be retained; it does not claim all voice chat is permanently recorded or that recording is a universal voice feature. This is GTA Online-specific, not an announced GTA VI feature or Avocado design.

AvocadoMini/MAT18 candidate: before voice use, distinguish live transmission/processing, optional transcription or summary, safety monitoring, stored evidence and user-created voice notes. For each, state whether audio leaves the device, which provider processes it, purpose, retention trigger and period, who can access/delete it, and how to use the feature without that processing when feasible. Detection should consider context and support review/appeal; do not let a probabilistic transcript/flag silently become identity, intent or permission evidence. No audio was recorded or sent and no Avocado privacy behavior changed.


## 107. 追加調査：作品内の音楽発見からアーティストの正規配信先へ直接つなぐ（2026-10-17 / RQ14、catalog discovery、IP）

Rockstar's October 15 [GTA VI Launch Trailer announcement](https://www.rockstargames.com/newswire/article/517572321a549a/watch-the-grand-theft-auto-vi-launch-trailer) identifies Justice as original-score composers, names the track “Flower in a Hurricane” and its featured artists, and links it to the official album, which is available for streaming-service pre-save and physical CD/vinyl preorder. This sits alongside the [GTA VI music hub](https://www.rockstargames.com/VI/music), which presents station previews and artist/track details. The example joins an in-game context, track metadata, artist attribution, standalone single, soundtrack and purchase/discovery endpoints without implying that a listener owns an export or reuse license.

The official [music hub](https://www.rockstargames.com/VI/music) now also groups curated radio station previews, singles from the 34-track *Grand Theft Auto VI: The Album*, an official-score single, links for digital pre-save and CD/vinyl preorder, and artist/host metadata. Rockstar's October 8 [radio-stations announcement](https://www.rockstargames.com/newswire/article/o3982oa93a23k4/the-music-of-grand-theft-auto-vi-in-game-radio-stations) describes radio and on-demand podcasts as playable both in vehicles and on foot through character smartphones/headphones. The game experience, web previews and album commerce are connected editorially but remain distinct listening and purchase contexts.

Avocado RQ14/catalog candidate: connect a moment or creator work to a canonical artist/title/source record and rights-holder-approved destinations; let a user preview, bookmark, follow, or open the authorized platform directly. A single hub can place editorial preview and creator/context information next to verified commercial destinations, but each button should explain whether it previews, opens an external service, saves, or purchases. Preserve credit and distinguish in-product preview/play permission from audio download, remix, sample, resale, and public/commercial video rights. This is a discovery and information-architecture analogy, not an Avocado music-market decision or GTA VI API. No link integration, catalog or purchase route changed.


## 108. 追加調査：社内identity侵害とthird-party breachを別incident pathとして扱う（2026-10-17 / Security、RQ14, AI06）

Take-Two's [2026 SEC annual report](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/946581/000130817926000378/ttwo015601ars.pdf) records its September 2022 Rockstar network intrusion: an unauthorized third party accessed and downloaded confidential information, including early development footage for the next Grand Theft Auto. Separately, after a reported April 2026 breach involving a third-party provider, Rockstar told outlets that “a limited amount of non-material company information was accessed” and said the incident had no impact on the organization or players; this second statement does not identify the attack path, exposed data categories, or specific integration. Bloomberg's 2026 development retrospective also describes the 2022 event as a phishing attack into internal Slack, based on interviews with Rockstar staff. Do not collapse these into one incident or claim the more recent event exposed GTA VI source/players' data; Rockstar's published scope was narrower.

Avocado Security/RQ14 candidate: threat-model both human account takeover and provider/integration compromise. Keep service credentials scoped, expiring and revocable; isolate build/source, user, billing and analytics data by need; inventory vendor data flows and owners; and keep audit evidence sufficient to identify which provider, token, dataset and time window were involved. Incident communications should state confirmed scope, affected users/data, operational status, what remains unknown and the next update time without treating an attacker claim as verified impact. These incidents reinforce existing least-privilege and integration-isolation work; they do not establish Rockstar's undisclosed controls or a confirmed Avocado vulnerability. No account, key or vendor connection was inspected or changed.


## 109. 追加調査：物理コレクター商品とdigital game entitlementを明示的に分ける（2026-10-17 / RQ14、SIM/eSIM product）

The [Rockstar Store Goodtime State – Vice City Collection page](https://store.rockstargames.com/merchandise/gtavi-goodtime-state-vice-city-collection?drm=PlayStation+5) lists a physical collector's box and its contents, provides adult collectible and small-parts safety notices plus shipping terms, and explicitly says “Grand Theft Auto VI game sold separately.” The [official Newswire announcement](https://www.rockstargames.com/newswire/article/9k2a49ook82o57/pre-order-the-goodtime-state-vice-city-collection-now-while-supplies-l) likewise describes the physical collection as separate from the game. This is a concrete catalog example where themed physical goods do not imply a software license.

Avocado SIM/eSIM product-page candidate: show SIM/hardware and fulfillment, service-access entitlement, supported native/client/browser path, and metered cloud usage as distinct line items and lifecycle states. A SIM order should not silently imply that an incompatible handset can install the native OS, nor imply unlimited cloud agent use. This is an information-design analogy only; it does not change pricing, bundles, or service terms. No order was placed.


## 110. 追加調査：platform account再リンク・profile migrationはprogressと価値を変える（2026-10-17 / RQ13、identity、RQ14）

Rockstar Support's [unlinking guidance](https://support.rockstargames.com/articles/tp2PvsKT8MKjraByTigca/unlinking-an-account-from-your-rockstar-games-account), updated June 23, 2026, says replacing a linked PSN/Xbox account can result in loss of Story Mode and Online Play progress associated with the platform identity; console-family platform accounts are one-per-family on a Rockstar account. Separately, the [GTA Online migration FAQ](https://support.rockstargames.com/articles/18gGCbIVmslXytEyNj6rCP/migrating-your-gta-online-profile-from-playstation-4-or-xbox-one-to-playstation-5-or-xbox-series-x-s), updated February 24, 2026, says migrated old- and new-generation profiles remain separate and progress made on each after migration does not sync. The [transfer policy](https://support.rockstargames.com/articles/7gFUOWEoLxs87G4Bqh08mu/platform-changes-and-gta-transfers-during-gta-online-profile-migration) distinguishes earned in-game currency from purchased GTA$; cross-family migration may exclude unspent purchased currency, and the support page gives additional exceptions. These are GTA V/GTA Online platform-specific rules, not GTA VI transfer terms.

Avocado identity/Wallet candidate: before unlinking, changing the primary identity or migrating a profile, preview exactly which records, entitlements, balances and history will follow, remain behind, become read-only or be lost; require explicit confirmation and provide a recovery/contact path. Preserve provenance for earned vs purchased/redeemed balance rather than treating a displayed total as fungible across destinations. Use an idempotent migration receipt and reconcile both old and new ledgers, while respecting existing Wallet reservation/ledger contracts. This is a candidate safeguard derived from Rockstar's documented consequences, not a claim about Avocado's current implementation or authorization to move value. No account was linked and no balance or profile was transferred.


## 111. 追加調査：2段階認証の復旧経路そのものがsecurity dependencyになる（2026-10-17 / Security、AI06）

Rockstar's [2-Step Verification setup guide](https://support.rockstargames.com/articles/1c5O1fKIOo0iG9W7y7WtM4/setting-up-2-step-verification-with-rockstar-games), updated August 18, 2026, requires verification of the account email and supports authenticator apps on a device or in a browser. Its [lost-authenticator guidance](https://support.rockstargames.com/articles/4VUQ2Su7dsuNXlW1cSFTJD/no-longer-have-access-to-phone-or-authenticator-to-complete-2-step-verification) routes recovery to the authenticator provider, while the [account-protection advice](https://support.rockstargames.com/articles/77PLWpN7ZfeLpLVLQ4l753/best-practices-for-protecting-your-rockstar-games-account) warns about phishing, support impersonation and credential-sharing, and recommends monitoring unauthorized linked accounts and devices. The documented recovery chain therefore depends on both a verified email and the chosen authenticator's recovery route; the cited help pages do not describe a universal Rockstar backup-code flow.

Avocado security candidate: map the primary credential, second factor, recovery provider, support identity check and account-owner contact method as separate dependencies. During setup, make recovery readiness visible before a factor is lost; account recovery should be auditable and should not silently weaken high-risk Wallet, signing-key or device-management permissions. Offer a safe, documented recovery path without asking the user to disclose a seed, password or time-limited code to an agent. This is an account-security design inference, not a Rockstar-to-Avocado integration. No credentials or recovery factors were accessed or changed.


## 112. 追加調査：Mission Creatorの試作・peer feedback・選定・enforcementを別段階にする（2026-10-17 / RQ14、trust/safety、creator rewards）

Rockstar's April 28, 2026 [Mission Creator announcement](https://www.rockstargames.com/newswire/article/39a3412379o79o/play-thrilling-new-gta-online-experiences-built-by-the-community-with?pubDate=20260430) describes a GTA Online-only, up-to-eight-player mission tool and five Rockstar-created missions as interactive tutorials that creators can also inspect in the editor. It recommends sharing a working prototype in Discord's #job-sharing for peer feedback, using #creator-tools to learn, then publishing with `#communityseries` as a tag for *potential* Community Series consideration. Rockstar also invites product feedback through its feedback portal. Selection is explicitly separate from self-publishing: featured Community Series creators get a one-time GTA$10,000,000 in-game award plus named items; this is not a cash payout or a guaranteed creator income program. Rockstar's December 10, 2025 [support article](https://support.rockstargames.com/articles/1lmcf3ZEFlNIYlOhwDpY6z/grand-theft-auto-online-mission-creator-unavailable) says a prior Community Guidelines/Terms violation can temporarily block Mission Creator access, new Mission Creator violations can lead to suspensions/bans, and affected players may appeal for re-evaluation. It does not specify a fixed restriction duration or guarantee appeal reversal. This is not evidence of GTA VI creator tooling.

Avocado RQ14 candidate: stage the author journey as playable tutorial/examples → private draft/prototype → peer feedback → public publish → curator/editorial consideration, with each transition and its visibility explicit. A discovery tag should express eligibility for consideration, not acceptance or guaranteed reach. Keep a non-cash in-product recognition award separate from Wallet revenue or settlement, and provide a clear, reviewable enforcement state plus appeal route when creation access is restricted. Measure where authors abandon, prototype-to-publish completion, feedback usefulness, time to first valid publish and appeals/reversals; do not assume Rockstar's reward size or access policy suits Avocado. No creator content, moderation action, reward or integration was performed.


## 113. 追加調査：PS5のGTA VIは触覚feedbackを使うが、機能はoff可能で安全文書に明記（2026-10-17 / MAT18、device capability）

Sony's September 3, 2026 [official PlayStation Blog announcement](https://blog.playstation.com/2026/09/03/first-look-at-the-grand-theft-auto-vi-limited-edition-dualsense-wireless-controllers/) says GTA VI on PS5 uses DualSense haptic feedback and adaptive triggers to respond to action and provide dynamic resistance. This is a game/platform-specific feature statement from Sony; it does not establish the same feature on Xbox, other devices, or general Avocado support. The [DualSense instruction manual](https://www.playstation.com/content/dam/global_pdc/en/corporate/support/manuals/accessories/ps5-accessories/dualsense-wireless-controller-cfi-zct1w/CFI-ZCT1W_PS5_Wireless%20Controller_Instruction%20Manual%24en.pdf) says not to use vibration/trigger effects with certain hand/arm bone, joint, or muscle conditions/injuries unless set to Off and gives the PS5 settings path to disable them. It separately advises stopping if discomfort or pain occurs. The [GTAV PS5 upgrade feature note](https://blog.ja.playstation.com/?p=200860) provides earlier concrete examples—road texture, weather, directional damage and explosions—but describes GTAV, not GTA VI.

AvocadoMini/MAT18 candidate: treat haptics as an optional per-device output channel, confirm device capability, allow intensity and immediate off, and ensure essential instructions/approvals remain available visually or audibly. Provide plain operating guidance and a stop/off route for discomfort rather than assuming more sensation is always better. These are accessibility and physical-interaction design candidates; Sony's controller warning is not medical advice for Avocado or evidence that any proposed wearable has equivalent characteristics. No device was tested or configured.


## 114. 追加調査：第三者roleplayへの公式支援と外部視聴特典は限定条件つきで橋渡しする（2026-10-17 / RQ14、community partnership、RQ15）

Rockstar's September 1, 2026 [nopixel V announcement](https://www.rockstargames.com/newswire/article/17857581o753k1/introducing-nopixel-v?pubDate=20260901) says the studio supports the community-run GTAV roleplay experience, with an invited closed beta accessed through the Rockstar Games Launcher; future expanded access was described as a later update, not open access at announcement. Separately, Rockstar offered time-limited Twitch Drops for watching nopixel V streamers to earn GTA Online rewards. The [Twitch linking instructions](https://support.rockstargames.com/articles/2LEP2gpqxyUXzh4BrSxhky/linking-your-twitch-account-to-your-rockstar-games-account), updated June 23, 2026, let users choose the Twitch account visibility during linking and say a GTA Online promotion requires a linked character that has completed the online tutorial. A third-party roleplay experience, Rockstar-launcher access, livestream eligibility, account linking, and GTA Online reward delivery are therefore distinct layers; none establishes a GTA VI multiplayer or creator integration.

Avocado RQ14/RQ15 candidate: describe integrations by an explicit status (community-made, supported/partnered, curated, first-party) with proof and scope, instead of letting endorsement appear by proximity. A cross-service campaign should state which identity connects, what profile data becomes visible, the eligible destination account/content prerequisite, campaign dates, claim step, expected delivery window and support owner. Track watch/participation, eligible, claimed, delivered and reconciled states separately; do not conflate creator discovery with a payment or entitlement. Rockstar's closed beta and in-game currency promotions are examples, not Avocado partner rules or cash-reward commitments. No account was connected and no campaign or reward was run.


## 115. 追加調査：offline accessとentitlement refreshを製品別・状態別に表示する（2026-10-17 / SIM/eSIM service access、Security）

Rockstar Support's general [Launcher offline-play guidance](https://support.rockstargames.com/articles/13xEcY8dWBDY4D7lC1Vsew/playing-games-offline-from-the-rockstar-games-launcher) says story mode generally does not require a connection, while some titles or modes may require one for activation/gameplay, cloud sync or updates. The separate [GTAV on PC entitlement FAQ](https://support.rockstargames.com/articles/d2zq9DgyTloMtsdVrZC9d/periodic-entitlement-verification-for-gtav-on-pc) explicitly says this specific PC title periodically verifies entitlement online; after successful verification, offline play is available again. Rockstar Error 133 guidance further states that offline play is available only if a Rockstar title was recently launched online on that computer, but does not publish a fixed grace-period duration. These are Launcher/GTAV PC support statements, not a verified GTA VI offline policy; they also distinguish basic story access from cloud saves, online mode and update checks.

Avocado SIM/eSIM service-access candidate: represent service entitlement freshness as an explicit state with a last successful check and next refresh condition, independent from physical SIM/network connection, OS/client installation and cloud-agent execution availability. Define which local functions keep working offline, whether queued work can run later, what cloud calls/costs pause, how usage reconciles after reconnect, and the recovery path when a license cannot refresh. Avoid showing a broad “offline supported” badge as if every feature and data sync works offline, and never invent a grace-period timer without a tested policy. Rockstar's Windows launcher behavior is an analogy, not an Avocado entitlement policy or GTA VI promise. No game, license or network was accessed.


## 116. 追加調査：Rockstarの2026 Mod Guidelinesは収益化・通貨交換・承認先に明確な境界を引く（2026-10-17 / RQ13、RQ14、IP/marketplace）

Rockstar's [Mod Guidelines](https://www.rockstargames.com/community-resources/mod-guidelines?pubDate=20260921), updated September 2026, say the Rockstar Creator Platform is the only licensed environment for the community to monetize mods and that, unless permitted through this official platform, mods must be strictly non-commercial. Where monetization is permitted, mods may be monetized only through digital storefront providers approved by Rockstar and subject to applicable rules. The guide says no selling in-game virtual currency for real money, real-money gambling/loot boxes/gacha, or crypto/cryptoasset promotion; it also prohibits third-party advertising/sponsorship in mods and monetizing Rockstar-created game content/missions/DLC. It expressly says the guidelines are a summary, not a license. The current [Rockstar Terms of Service](https://www.rockstargames.com/legal?country=us) separately state that Virtual Items have no monetary value outside Rockstar Services and prohibit unauthorized markets that rent, sell, share, exchange or transfer Virtual Items/accounts, except under separate express written terms from Rockstar.

Avocado RQ13/RQ14 boundary: do not design a GTA VI mod storefront, payment route or GTA-currency exchange on the assumption that general-purpose creator SDK or Wallet capabilities imply permission. Only present such a route if the exact Rockstar authorization, eligible title, approved storefront, content scope and transaction model are documented; otherwise keep Avocado creator billing for its own distinct, permitted services and prevent game-currency cash-out. This is a material opportunity *boundary*: a standalone, platform-agnostic creator workflow could still support ordinary software/service listings when rights allow, but Rockstar game mods need the specific permission. Neither the Guidelines nor Rockstar's current FiveM/RedM Creator PLA announces that GTA VI is supported or that Avocado is approved. No marketplace, virtual currency exchange, purchase or mod was created.


## 117. 追加調査：公式launch trailerはcinematic media assetとして提示し、playable proofと区別する（2026-10-18 / RQ14、media discovery、accessibility）

Replayed the user-provided [Rockstar Games Japan official launch trailer](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=MNPmV5nWvC4) in-browser: it is a 1:30 cinematic montage, opening with a nighttime aerial city view, then moving through sunlit/coastal and character/action cuts before returning to the GTA VI logo/title card. The player exposed no transcript or caption track for this Japanese-channel copy at review time; the [official launch-trailer page](https://www.rockstargames.com/VI/launch-trailer) identifies the October 15 trailer and its debut use of Justice's “Flower in a Hurricane.” By contrast, Rockstar's [26:48 Extended Look](https://www.rockstargames.com/VI/an-extended-look) is explicitly described as captured entirely from in-game footage on PlayStation 5. This is stronger capture evidence than the cinematic launch montage, but still doesn't establish every scene is interactive, final runtime performance, or production acceptance on other platforms.

Avocado media/catalog candidate: label source, owner/channel, runtime, release date, region/language, caption/transcript availability and media type (trailer, capture, tutorial, interactive demo) before recommending a resource. Offer an accessible summary when captions/transcript are missing and route users to the canonical official source; require an interactive artifact or reproducible interaction evidence before calling anything a playable demo. This video observation does not change the GTA VI feature inventory or Avocado media policy. No clip was downloaded, copied, or reused.

Reopened Rockstar's [official music hub](https://www.rockstargames.com/VI/music): it distinguishes an official Justice score/single feature, hand-picked “Preview Station” cards for six named radio stations, and a separate 34-track album section with nine singles and pre-save/physical pre-order links. “Flower in a Hurricane” appears both in the launch-trailer debut/score description and the album singles list; that relationship does not say it is on an in-game radio station, and hand-picked station previews are not a complete station roster. Avocado media candidate: represent canonical asset, promotional appearance, product placement, curated preview, full collection, and commercial reuse permission as distinct linked facts with rights owner, scope and freshness. A title, genre, artist or teaser link must not imply an available full asset or reuse license. This is a catalog/provenance pattern only; no audio was downloaded, embedded or reused.


## 118. 追加調査：Cfx Portalのサーバー情報編集と5タグ制限は段階的な告知（2026-10-18 / RQ14、catalog/discovery、creator onboarding）

Cfx's [October 14 Portal update announcement](https://forum.cfx.re/t/portal-update-server-metadata-management/5431315) says FiveM/RedM server names, descriptions, tags, language and imagery are moving from local config files to Cfx Portal. It says missing metadata will display as “Missing server data (must be configured in Portal),” tags will become searchable and each server will have at most five unique tags, and a Discord URL can appear in the client details panel. The announcement says most changes take effect the following week, October 21, 2026; as of this research date (October 18), treat it as announced, not yet fully in effect. Existing FiveM documentation still describes the previous `sv_projectName`/`sv_projectDesc` settings, demonstrating why source freshness and rollout state matter.

Avocado RQ14 candidate: model a listing as structured metadata with owner, description, language, bounded searchable categories, imagery specs, public contact and external community link. Give authors a preview and validation before publish, and store where metadata is edited plus the rollout/version that currently controls public rendering. Don't let free-text stuffing masquerade as tags, and surface missing/invalid listing fields before review. This is a Cfx/FiveM/RedM service-management change, not a Rockstar GTA VI server-browser feature; it is an implementation pattern, not a direct integration claim.


## 119. 追加調査：Creator Platform License AgreementはGTA VI対応を認めず、AI素材利用も限定する（2026-10-18 / RQ13、RQ14、AI06、IP/provenance）

Rockstar's [Creator Platform License Agreement (PLA)](https://fivem.net/terms), last updated September 10, 2026, scopes Creator Services to authorized covered games and describes FiveM/RedM; the linked agreement does not name GTA VI as a covered game. It is a revocable, limited license, and its restrictions reserve ungranted rights. This text therefore cannot be used as evidence of a GTA VI mod API, multiplayer creator feature, Avocado approval, or permission to sell GTA VI-related services.

The PLA gives a useful concrete example of marketplace contract boundaries: it distinguishes the operator's responsibilities (marketplace operation, payment collection/processing, transaction conclusion and delivery) from the creator's customary technical support; it grants only the specified server-use license, restricts standalone redistribution, and says a subscription license ends when the subscription ends or the creator stops offering it. It states that Rockstar may designate, require or prohibit third-party services, and such lists may change. It also bars using Marketplace Content to develop, train, enhance, tune, provide source material for or promote generative-AI tools. Custom-server listings must identify/contact the operator and disclaim Rockstar approval/sponsorship/endorsement. These statements describe this particular agreement and its covered services, not general law or Avocado obligations.

Avocado RQ14/AI06 candidate: each catalog asset should carry source/provenance, creator and operator identity, permitted product/title/environment, exact license scope, one-time vs recurring term, redistribution/modification rights, support owner, external-service dependencies, revocation/end-of-subscription behavior, and whether use for AI inference, retrieval, training or evaluation is permitted. Keep that evidence attached at purchase/install/export boundaries and invalidate capabilities when a license expires. Display non-affiliation clearly where required by a partner contract. Do not assume every tool marketplace may ingest purchased assets into an AI model. This is a design inference from a FiveM/RedM-specific contract; no Rockstar agreement exists here with Avocado and no external transaction occurred.


## 120. 追加調査：creator economyは「稼ぐ手段」だけでなく、cash-out・広告・第三者販売の禁止領域を機械判定できる形にする（2026-10-18 / RQ13、RQ14、RQ15、Wallet）

Section 3 of the September 2026 [Creator PLA](https://fivem.net/terms) prohibits, absent separate express written Rockstar terms, a list of monetization patterns for its Creator Services: paying in and cashing out real-money value (including real-money gambling), selling random-chance mechanics for money or game currency, selling game virtual currency for real money, facilitating sale/access to Rockstar-created virtual items, running a branded/sponsored custom server, aggregating and selling other creators' server content for real money, inserting third-party ads/sponsorships in creator content, and facilitating cryptoassets. It says the list is non-exhaustive and can be changed. The PLA places compliance responsibility for lawful commercial activity on the operator/server admin. Its marketplace section also says the Rockstar/Operator role in transactions is limited, creators provide customary technical support, and buyer/creator disputes need not be resolved by Rockstar. Those exact limits are tied to this Creator PLA, not a universal statement about all Rockstar products or all creator platforms.

Avocado RQ13/RQ15 candidate: create a policy matrix by platform and product that distinguishes (a) Avocado subscription/service fees, (b) creator's own software/service sales, (c) game currency or virtual-item sale, (d) payout/cash-out, (e) chance-based rewards, (f) ads/sponsorships, and (g) referrals/in-game rewards. Each route needs separate allowed/blocked/pending status with the evidence and exact title/jurisdiction/transaction scope; unknown or missing authorization should fail closed for the affected route, not block unrelated allowed Avocado services. Keep creator-issued game points separate from Rock's real-money ledger and from third-party game balances. This is a product-safety design inference, not a legal conclusion, review of Avocado's financial products, or permission to implement any GTA currency exchange. No money moved and no commercial service launched.


## 121. 追加調査：Cfxのlisting privacy・anti-abuse knobsは「見える情報」と「接続時の安全性」を別々に扱う（2026-10-18 / Security、RQ14、catalog trust）

The current [Cfx server command reference](https://docs.fivem.net/docs/server-manual/server-commands/) exposes separate controls with different functions: `sv_endpointPrivacy` hides player IP addresses from public server reports; `sv_forceIndirectListing` avoids advertising the server's real IP; `sv_appearAllowlisted` shows a lock icon and `sv_allowlistInstructions` tells users how to request entry. The reference also warns against `sv_scriptHookAllowed` because it exposes security risk, describes a `sv_pureLevel` setting that rejects modified client files, and provides controls to filter certain network control events. This is server-operator configuration documentation, not a claim about Rockstar's internal protections or GTA VI capabilities.

Avocado catalog candidate: make public listing visibility, contact/location disclosure, admission policy, identity assurance and runtime permission controls independent fields. A public listing does not mean open access; an allowlist gate should tell the user how to request access, while the protected service should enforce the same gate at connection. Admins should see a safe default profile and explicit warnings for high-risk compatibility settings. Don't imply anonymity simply because one IP field is hidden; explain which fields remain visible and to whom. This is a security design analogy, not FiveM software integration or a claim that these exact controls fit Avocado's architecture. No external server was connected or modified.


## 122. 追加調査：GTA VI購入後のentitlement・特典・supportは販売経路とplatform accountに依存する（2026-10-18 / SIM/eSIM access、RQ14、support routing）

Rockstar's [platform/edition FAQ](https://support.rockstargames.com/articles/4QfG4FmZCf5W1gS8jy4UVT/grand-theft-auto-vi-platform-editions-and-versions) says the physical GTA VI package contains a download code and no disc; console/account region alignment matters for PlayStation code redemption, Xbox codes are not region-locked, and the Japan PlayStation code is stated to expire 170 days after issue (the listed issue/launch date is November 19, 2026). A Standard owner can later buy a digital Ultimate Edition Upgrade after redeeming the download code. Physical and digital editions have different promotion eligibility. The exact storefront support FAQ routes PlayStation/Xbox purchases to each platform, Rockstar Store physical order issues to Xsolla, and third-party retailer issues to that retailer. These are current published details, may change, and are not uniform across regions.

The [Japan PlayStation listing](https://store.playstation.com/ja-jp/product/JP0230-PPSA29660_00-GTAVISTANDARD001) displays a release timestamp of November 18 at 15:00 UTC; its [U.S. listing](https://store.playstation.com/en-us/product/EP1004-PPSA01547_00-GTAVISTANDARD001) shows November 19 at 05:00 UTC. Both correspond to midnight on November 19 in the respective storefront's local time zone. Keep canonical instants/time-zone labels alongside localized dates and cutoff inclusivity; a plain date string can appear to differ across regions even when the local release moment aligns. This observes the storefront metadata and does not establish a universal launch unlock schedule.
