Rockstar's [June 24 preorder announcement](https://www.rockstargames.com/newswire/article/5171972o3ak5oa/pre-order-grand-theft-auto-vi-on-june-25) says digital preloading begins November 12 so the game can be ready to play at launch November 19; it also says the physical box (which contains a download code, not a disc) is available starting November 12 to support preloading. This separates buying/redeeming, downloading, and launch availability into different states. A preload notice does not establish any offline entitlement or the exact launch unlock behavior on each platform.

Rockstar's [GTA+ preorder offer FAQ](https://support.rockstargames.com/articles/3SZF8cy8dG7lHsLf38uWK1/redeeming-the-one-month-gta-membership-included-with-your-grand-theft-auto-vi-pre-order) says the one-month trial is claimed separately in the platform store, then automatically renews monthly at regular price unless canceled. Redemption deadlines differ (PS Store by March 31, 2027; Microsoft within 180 days), it is one redemption per platform account, and refunding/reordering does not provide a second month. PlayStation's [offer detail](https://www.playstation.com/en-us/games/grand-theft-auto-vi/) additionally lists country exclusions and age eligibility. The [Xbox listing](https://www.xbox.com/en-US/games/store/grand-theft-auto-vi/9nl3wwnzlzzn) says the trial redemption is unavailable to child accounts restricted from age-rated content. Keep a promo's age/account eligibility separate from core title availability and the platform's broader age rating. This is a limited promotion and should not be generalized to every channel/country or to paid GTA+ terms beyond what the pages state.

Avocado SIM/eSIM and service-entitlement candidate: bind each benefit to its precise purchase source, eligible hardware/platform account, country/region, age/account type, redemption action/deadline, renewal price/date, cancellation path, one-time redemption key and support owner. Separate eligibility for an included bonus from the base product's entitlement. Distinguish hardware shipment/physical packaging from digital license, download code and service entitlement; a retailer may own purchase/refund support while Avocado owns its SIM/service access. Before claim, show future recurring charge and cancellation path. Do not promise regional compatibility from the SKU alone; verify the destination account/region and preserve an auditable claim/refund event. GTA VI's details are an example of channel fragmentation, not Avocado's contract or product requirements. No preorder, account, purchase or subscription was created.

Avocado lifecycle cross-check: preserve separate `ordered/shipped`, `code issued/redeemed`, `downloaded/installed`, `service entitled`, and `usable at` states with provider-specific dates; never translate preload/installation into an active subscription, accepted device, online entitlement or feature-complete release. This restates the current SIM/eSIM and OS/client/device separation as an operational example, not a new requirement.

The promotional [one-month GTA+ offer](https://support.rockstargames.com/articles/3SZF8cy8dG7lHsLf38uWK1/redeeming-the-one-month-gta-membership-included-with-your-grand-theft-auto-vi-pre-order) should not be read as GTA VI multiplayer or GTA VI included in GTA+. Rockstar's [membership benefits FAQ](https://support.rockstargames.com/articles/21l14aEXmnyjFrnsqQ6eXG/gta-membership-benefits) defines current GTA+ value as a selection of other Rockstar titles and benefits for GTA Online on specified platforms; the [cancellation FAQ](https://support.rockstargames.com/articles/5mD2vI7nNEYIPu4fonpu01/gta-membership-benefits-status-after-cancelling-gta-membership) separates claimed/retained items from ongoing features, an accessible games library and unclaimed event benefits that end or are forfeited on cancellation. This is a current service scope and can change; GTA VI remains described elsewhere as single player.

Rockstar's [GTA VI preorder information](https://support.rockstargames.com/articles/3iH5w2nKVFySqs7hrONlLS/grand-theft-auto-vi-pre-order-information) routes readers to the game site, Newswire and email newsletter; subscription is managed separately under account Communications. The separate [special-offers policy](https://support.rockstargames.com/articles/QXVJsLQyvLgKMuR8np1nR/receiving-special-offers-or-promotions) is specifically about GTA Online, says offers may be selected, platform-limited, one-time, nontransferable and without guaranteed availability/frequency, and requires the offer to have been received. Avocado candidate: distinguish required service/security notices, optional marketing consent, product-account identity, offer eligibility, offer redemption and channel/platform limits; an email opt-in is neither a service entitlement nor guaranteed promotion. Rockstar's mailing list and GTA Online offer rules are not Avocado marketing consent or promotion policy; no account or newsletter preference was accessed.


## 123. 追加調査：privacy setting名・block・UGC閲覧権限は同じ範囲を制御しない（2026-10-18 / Security、RQ14、UGC）

Rockstar's [Privacy Settings and User Generated Content FAQ](https://support.rockstargames.com/articles/6NKqPKffElhndfInHKOyeP/rockstar-games-privacy-settings-and-user-generated-content), updated February 24, 2026, says some information remains visible despite a “Me Only” setting, including Crew membership, wall comments and multiplayer data. Blocking a player prevents direct communication but does not prevent that player from viewing or commenting on UGC. The same FAQ points users to report an individual comment when it violates guidelines. This is the scope of the documented Rockstar profile/UGC controls, not a GTA VI privacy feature and not a claim that all privacy controls are ineffective.

AvocadoMini/RQ14 candidate: describe each privacy control by the exact object and action it controls—profile display, job metadata, file view, comments, direct messaging, group membership, exported copy—and show what remains visible. Keep `block`, `hide`, `mute`, `restrict interaction` and `report for review` as separate operations with their own reversible state and audit trail. A user-facing label should not imply that blocking erases prior comments, hides shared artifacts or revokes copied/exported data unless it actually does. No Rockstar profile or privacy setting was changed.


## 124. 追加調査：creator-hosted media機能の終了には保存・移行・link-healthの明示が必要（2026-10-18 / RQ14、media persistence）

Rockstar Support says that, as of April 15, 2025, uploading Rockstar Editor UGC videos to the Rockstar Games site is no longer supported, and videos uploaded earlier are no longer available from that site ([support notice](https://support.rockstargames.com/articles/5urCEPfihTyTgjWlWAkx3/end-of-support-for-user-generated-content-videos-on-the-rockstar-games)). This demonstrates a specific hosting surface and its historical media retirement; it does not say that creators lost original local files or that other sharing services stopped working.

Avocado RQ14 candidate: store artifact source and canonical home separately from an embed/cached preview; publish lifecycle with sunset date, export window, migration route, and what happens to comments, permissions and buyer access. Periodically check link/preview health and offer a portable export while a service still exists. Clearly distinguish “hidden,” “deleted from provider,” “provider service ended,” and “local/original retained”; do not promise permanent hosting unless backed by an explicit retention contract. Rockstar's retirement is an analogy, not a request to import GTA media or an Avocado retention policy change.


## 125. 追加調査：Rockstarの現地調査とmilestone評価は小さく学び、記録して次の焦点を決める例（2026-10-18 / Avocado pilot、Zema、AI06）

In an October 5 [LOVE Magazine interview with Rockstar leadership](https://lovemagazine.net/articles/gta-vi-the-american-dream), Rockstar North co-studio heads Aaron Garbut and Rob Nelson describe starting research trips with a small group, returning with more of the team to go deeper, and grounding fictional Leonida in places, people and stories observed across Florida. Nelson describes building in eight-week milestones around agreed sections, evaluating those milestones, taking notes to determine the next focus, and course-correcting repeatedly. He also says accumulated team experience supports a consistent creative vision across the expanded team. This is their account of a large game's creative process, not an independently audited delivery method or evidence that Avocado should use an eight-week cadence.

Avocado pilot candidate: start with a small representative task cohort, observe real setup and recovery friction, then widen only after documenting what the initial evidence answered and what remains uncertain. At each checkpoint, capture intended user outcome, observed workflow, time/errors/rework, permission/cost surprises, evidence source and the next chosen improvement; maintain one visible product intent across OS, Web and Agent surfaces. Use a cadence that matches task risk and real usage volume rather than copying Rockstar's eight weeks. This improves product learning, not a GTA feature or an approved schedule; no pilot cohort, acceptance metric or roadmap changed.


## 126. 追加調査：通報フォームに「問題カテゴリ」と「発生した機能面」の両方を持たせる（2026-10-18 / RQ14、trust/safety、UGC）

Rockstar's [GTA Online reporting guide](https://support.rockstargames.com/articles/3h4bpT9AgPUjudbEq2mZU2/reporting-content-and-behavior-in-grand-theft-auto-online), updated December 5, 2025, lists reports against player behavior, online IDs, Job cover images/descriptions/text/assets, cheating/modding, griefing and abusive communication. For some categories the report flow asks both which abuse category applies (e.g., harassment, identity-based hate, asking for personal information, regulated activity, sexual threat, violent content) and which feature surface was used (e.g., voice chat, phone chat, crew name/emblem/tag). This is a GTA Online support flow only, not GTA VI's reporting UI.

Avocado RQ14 candidate: capture the reported object and feature surface alongside a plain-language reason, severity and optional incident context, then show the reporter what information will be shared with reviewers. A single “report abuse” bucket makes triage difficult; an overly broad questionnaire may discourage legitimate reports or collect excess personal data. Default to a short required path with context-sensitive follow-up, allow report updates, and record review/appeal outcome separately from account enforcement. No report was filed, and no Avocado moderation workflow changed.


## 127. 追加調査：promoで会員trialを売る場合、対象サービス・残る特典・失効特典を切り分ける（2026-10-18 / SIM/eSIM、subscription lifecycle、RQ15）

The free month of GTA+ associated with eligible digital GTA VI purchases is a platform-store membership promotion. Rockstar Support describes GTA+ as benefits usable in GTA Online plus a rotating library of other Rockstar titles; its current materials do not describe GTA VI as the membership's included online game, and the PlayStation page explicitly labels GTA VI a single-player experience. After cancellation, support distinguishes claimed in-game items that remain from recurring/member-only services, catalog access and event benefits that stop or are lost; unclaimed event items may be forfeited. Exact platform and country conditions are documented in §122. Do not infer any future GTA VI online or GTA+ integration from this promotion.

Avocado SIM/eSIM candidate: if a SIM purchase includes a third-party promo, draw a clear boundary around which separate service it activates, what data/account binding is needed, who renews and bills it, what cancels automatically versus separately, which downloaded/claimed value persists, what requires an active membership and what expires unclaimed. Offer “claim,” “active,” “cancel at,” “renewing,” “expired” states without turning a marketing trial into proof of native OS installation or a game's feature. Rockstar's offer is an analogy and doesn't define an Avocado promotion. No subscription was claimed or changed.


## 128. 追加調査：age ratingは国・issuer・対象media/versionを結合し、古いstore copyと混同しない（2026-10-18 / RQ14、Security、regional catalog）

The official [ESRB GTA VI entry](https://www.esrb.org/ratings/41627/grand-theft-auto-vi/) lists Rockstar Games, PS5/Xbox Series, Mature 17+, content descriptors and “In-Game Purchases.” That descriptor alone does not specify what is sold or establish an online marketplace, currency exchange or multiplayer feature. The U.S. PlayStation product page still includes the contradictory sentence “age ratings ... are unconfirmed,” an example of a stale publisher/retailer copy after the rating body has published a decision. Australia's [R18+ GTA VI result](https://www.classification.gov.au/titles/grand-theft-auto-vi-12) is specifically categorized “Demonstration (Self Selected)” and lists Take 2 Interactive as applicant; it is not the whole game's final classification. The same search returns other similarly named IARC entries with unrelated applicants (e.g. “semstudio”) and generic low ratings; title text alone cannot authenticate a rating record. Ratings and descriptors are jurisdiction-specific, and the Australian demo result must not be substituted for the full game rating.

CERO's [2026 parent guide](https://www.cero.gr.jp/relays/download/1/83/100/325/?file=%2Ffiles%2Flibs%2F325%2F202605151504152017.pdf) separately explains that its age category describes expression in the submitted game materials and cannot indicate by itself whether in-game purchases or online communication exist; it advises checking the store/product feature descriptions. It also says a same-title game may receive different results when the platform/version or reviewed content differs, and materially changed DLC can lead to re-review. This is CERO process guidance, not a GTA VI-specific CERO classification or an Avocado rule.

Avocado candidate: store a rating/eligibility record with issuer, jurisdiction, exact product/version or media type, publisher/applicant, decision date, status and content descriptors; prefer the authoritative rating body's current record over stale retailer copy and reject unverified title-string matches. Keep age/content rating separate from purchase features, external network/chat, and subscription/payment metadata. If sources conflict, show the conflict and the responsible region rather than silently selecting a universal age threshold. This is a catalog data-quality pattern; it is not a legal rule or an Avocado age policy. No age gate or user profile changed.


## 129. 追加調査：評価スコアは購入・使用の実在性とmoderationを併記する（2026-10-18 / RQ14、catalog trust）

The [PlayStation GTA VI page](https://www.playstation.com/en-us/games/grand-theft-auto-vi/) states that only owners of the game can rate it and that every review comes from a verified owner and is evaluated by a moderator under Sony's Ratings and Reviews Policy. This is Sony's system for its product page; it does not establish Rockstar's UGC policy or an Avocado marketplace review feature.

Avocado RQ14 candidate: label raw star/review count separately from verified purchase, verified successful installation/use, task-specific result and human moderation. Let the reviewer identify the exact Tool/runtime version and optional execution context; keep support tickets, abuse reports and public reviews distinct. A high listing click count is discovery, not product quality; account ownership is not proof the Tool worked. Show “no verified usage feedback yet” when evidence is absent. No rating, review, purchase or install occurred.


## 130. 追加調査：GTAの比較はAvocadoの既存marketplace/report/entitlement契約へ足し合わせる（2026-10-18 / RQ14、SIM/eSIM、workflow reuse）

Local read-only inspection confirms that Avocado already has relevant foundations: [Sky Marketplace](../components/sky-marketplace.tsx) loads a public tool registry and presents only packages whose state is `verified` and `installable`; [Campus Store report handling](../lib/campus-store.ts) accepts a target profile/item, a reason from the current list, optional detail, and persists an `open` report; [the eSIM public catalog](../lib/esim-public-catalog.ts) projects country coverage/roaming, data allowance, validity period, service-start trigger and installation prerequisites, and hard-codes commerce disabled while provider contracts remain pending. These are current code facts, not proof of completed end-to-end production service.

The GTA examples therefore point to narrow extensions to evidence, not a duplicate marketplace: retain `verified`/`installable` as distinct from verified successful use or buyer review; add source/contract/build version and support ownership only where the existing registry needs those facts; map any additional report context onto the current report workflow; and preserve current eSIM purchase gate while keeping shipment, service entitlement, client install and partner promotion distinct. Do not create new rating, moderation, payment or entitlement stores before checking existing schemas and `lib/workflow.ts`. No code, schema, price, eligibility or service state changed.


## 131. 追加調査：realismはユーザーの目標に役立つところだけ再現し、選択の意味づけを奪わない（2026-10-18 / AI06、MAT18、agent UX）

In the October 5 [LOVE Magazine Rockstar interview](https://lovemagazine.net/articles/gta-vi-the-american-dream), development head Rob Nelson says realism can help immersion, but too much becomes tedious, so the team tries to balance it with convenience; some real-world elements are omitted when they do not serve the experience. Narrative lead Rupert Humphries says the game does not prescribe an interpretation of Jason's material circumstances and leaves it to the player to decide whether they necessitate his choices. This is a statement of game-design intent, not an empirical UX finding.

Avocado AI06/MAT18 candidate: preserve meaningful user choice while automating routine workflow friction. Surface the real details that change outcome, permissions, data sharing, price, risk or reversibility; collapse irrelevant operational steps behind a clear explanation and recovery path. Agents should state why a path is recommended and offer valid alternatives rather than infer what the person “really needs” from context and silently decide for them. Measure task completion, rework, surprise, user understanding and abandonment to decide whether detail is helping or burdensome. Rockstar's fiction-writing approach is an analogy only; no agent prompt, approval boundary or product UX changed.


## 132. 追加調査：公式メディアは素材種別・版・一括取得単位を分けている（2026-10-18 / RQ14、AI06、evidence provenance）

The [official GTA VI media page](https://www.rockstargames.com/VI/media) groups featured material, videos, screenshots, artwork and wallpapers; it separately exposes four videos, nine short clips, 99 screenshots, 51 Ultimate Edition benefit images, 12 Vintage Vice City pack images and 25 artwork/wallpapers, with individual entries and some bulk-download links. The [official launch-trailer page](https://www.rockstargames.com/VI/launch-trailer) labels the Oct 15 upload as 1:31, credits its debut track to Justice and collaborators, and links the separately titled 26:48 Extended Look and the earlier two trailers with dates. The [official landing page](https://www.rockstargames.com/VI) also links Music, People & Places, Media downloads and Shop. This is a publisher's pre-release media catalog, not a license to redistribute or use assets to train models. The launch trailer itself remains cinematic evidence rather than proof that every depicted scene is interactive; Rockstar's separate Extended Look page explicitly describes its footage as captured in-game on PS5.

Avocado candidate: make a single Tool/job evidence page distinguish source listing, version/build, verified install/use, execution output, help/tutorial, release notes and downloadable assets; preserve provider/source, capture date, hash where applicable, eligibility and reuse terms on each asset. Permit a compact preview plus a user-selected bulk export of eligible artifacts; explain which are public, private, generated, licensed or only link-outs. Avoid mixing a screenshot with a capability guarantee, a marketplace listing with verified use, or an asset download with a reuse right. This complements current Sky `verified`/`installable` filtering without creating a second catalog. No media was downloaded or copied; no code, rights, catalog or contract changed.


## 133. 追加調査：証拠・承認・成果の連鎖は既存Zema workflowに沿わせる（2026-10-18 / AI06、RQ14、workflow reuse）

Read-only inspection of [lib/workflow.ts](../lib/workflow.ts) shows the cloud-agent template has a brief/approval step and a result/record step; `WorkPlanGate` names provider quote + Wallet reservation + explicit cloud approval, then a terminal result captured with a usage receipt. Agent records require a saved delegation ID. Each command uses a job revision; duplicate operation IDs with different payloads are rejected, completion requires review and all steps passed, and a changed revision is rejected for reload. This is current local contract evidence, not proof of a production provider or end-to-end execution. The work schema does not currently provide a generic attachment/evidence manifest for arbitrary media.

Avocado candidate: if a catalog/tool card points to supporting files, keep source provenance and rights metadata in the current registry/job evidence boundary; when it invokes a cloud Agent, bind the selected tool/build, quote, reservation, approval, delegation ID, execution receipt and final result to the same revisioned job. Any new evidence manifest should extend the existing `lib/workflow.ts` contract and OS/fixture equivalence rules instead of adding a separate marketplace or report queue. No implementation or schema changed; this is a crosswalk and identifies an unimplemented attachment surface, not an approved requirement.


## 134. 追加調査：assist inputは共同操作として扱い、独立した本人承認へ昇格させない（2026-10-18 / MAT18、Security、RQ14）

Sony's [PS5 assist-controller instructions](https://www.playstation.com/en-ca/support/hardware/second-controller-assistance/) say two controllers can operate as one: the main and assist controller are both assigned to the same user, and the assist controller does not expose haptic feedback, adaptive triggers, mic functions or custom button assignments. Sony introduced this as an accessibility aid for collaborative play or helping someone through a difficult section, as distinct from a second user/player; its [2023 announcement](https://blog.playstation.com/2023/09/13/ps5-update-rolls-out-globally-with-new-accessibility-audio-and-social-feature-enhancements/) describes the one-account arrangement. This is a PS5 system-level function, not a GTA VI-specific feature or two-party approval mechanism.

AvocadoMini/security candidate: support helper input as a bounded accessibility mode while retaining an explicit indicator of whose authenticated identity, data scope and approval are active. Two input sources that are collapsed into one user session must not be counted as two-person approval, two independent credentials or separate authorship. For actions that share data, spend money, publish or change permissions, require the existing authenticated owner approval and provide a clear way to suspend helper input. Offer equivalent text/audio/touch paths where a hardware cue (including haptics) is unavailable or disabled. This maps to existing identity/approval boundaries and is a research analogy only; no controller, permission, workflow or hardware design changed.


## 135. 追加調査：accessibility featureは実際に対応する版単位のmetadataとして見せる（2026-10-18 / RQ14、catalog UX、MAT18）

Sony's [Accessibility Tags announcement](https://blog.playstation.com/2023/04/03/accessibility-tags-roll-out-this-week-on-playstation-store-on-the-ps5-console/) says developers may describe supported game features in six groups: visual, audio, subtitles/captions, controls, gameplay and online communication. Examples include clear/large text, visual alternatives, volume/mono controls, subtitle controls, remapping, no rapid-press or motion-control requirement, difficulty modifiers and chat transcription. If both PS4 and PS5 versions exist, their tags can be compared separately. Sony describes tags as developer-provided information; they are neither a clinical assessment nor a guarantee that every player will be accommodated. This generic storefront model does not prove GTA VI has submitted tags or that its specific options are known.

Read-only code inspection shows [the current `sky-tool-package/1` contract](../lib/sky-tool-package.ts) uses exact package fields, with `capabilities` limited to connectivity, execution targets, permissions and side effects; accessibility metadata is not present, and unknown keys are rejected. Avocado catalog candidate: consider a version-scoped accessibility/interaction profile—e.g., text scale/contrast, caption and non-audio alternatives, input methods, timed/precision actions, controller/haptic requirements, pause/undo, and shared/private output. Show who supplied and tested each claim, exact product/runtime/device version, evidence date, and unverified gaps. Let users filter based on their needs without assigning ability labels or inferring them from sensors. Keep provider self-report separate from Avocado's verified platform behavior. If pursued, this requires an explicit schema/design update and compatibility plan; no package schema, rating, requirement or accessibility claim changed. GTA VI's listing status remains unverified.


## 136. 追加調査：offline-playの商品表示は全feature・license checkのoffline保証ではない（2026-10-18 / AI06、SIM/eSIM、catalog trust）

The Japanese [PlayStation GTA VI listing](https://store.playstation.com/ja-jp/product/JP0230-PPSA29660_00-GTAVISTANDARD001) flags the PS5 title “offline play enabled” and “1 player,” while the same store page's legal text says certain features/services may require an internet connection, Rockstar account, serial code, fee or gameplay unlock, may not be available to everyone or at all times, and can be modified under the ToS. Xbox's [official listing](https://www.xbox.com/en-US/games/store/grand-theft-auto-vi/9nl3wwnzlzzn) labels it single-player and similarly warns some features may require internet/account registration and may change or be unavailable. Thus the platform-level offline flag indicates an offline play mode exists; it does not establish that every connected service, promotion redemption, account entitlement, cloud save or post-release feature works offline. These are pre-release, platform-specific listing statements, subject to change.

The same Japanese PlayStation page states that the account's designated primary PS5 can download/play the title at any time under the console-sharing/offline-play setting, whereas a different PS5 can do so after the same account signs in. This is a platform entitlement rule, not a Rockstar requirement for all devices or an Avocado policy; it shows why “installed” and “licensed for this device/session” may be separate states.

Avocado candidate: use the existing [`SkyToolPackage.capabilities.connectivity`](../lib/sky-tool-package.ts) enum (`offline`, `online`, `hybrid`) per Tool, but explain the dimensions separately: local execution, initial activation/license check, ongoing provider connection, cloud sync, remote Agent and upgrade/install access. Test and state the supported offline scenario, cached state and recovery path; never turn “offline-capable” into “all functionality available offline.” The current schema's connectivity enum alone may not encode all these conditions, so any schema extension requires explicit design review. No connectivity claims or package schemas changed.


## 137. 追加調査：platform account linkはsource identity・consent・unlink・active userを通して検証する（2026-10-18 / Security、AI06、identity lifecycle）

The [Xbox store listing](https://www.xbox.com/en-US/games/store/grand-theft-auto-vi/9nl3wwnzlzzn) displays a generic Microsoft notice that publishers of Xbox games receive profile information and associated data while players use the game; this is a platform relationship disclosure, not a GTA VI-specific statement about which fields Rockstar accesses. Microsoft's [Privacy Statement](https://www.microsoft.com/en-us/privacy/privacystatement) lists examples of publisher-accessible Xbox data such as Xbox user ID, Gamertag, country/age range, in-game communications, enforcement activity, gameplay sessions, network presence, play time, rankings/profiles/avatars, friends and official-club data, and user-created/submitted content. The statement says third-party developers have a separate data relationship/policy; stopping future platform sharing and handling data already collected by a publisher are different matters.

Microsoft's [Xbox console certification guidance](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/gaming/gdk/docs/store/policies/Console/console-certification-requirements-and-tests?view=gdk-2510) requires Xbox titles that use publisher-account linking to show applicable terms/privacy and explain the reason before linking, authenticate both accounts (not an unauthenticated Gamertag alone), notify users, support optional opt-out and unlinking, handle age/region/account-type restrictions, and respect current permissions. Its separate user-state checks require save/progress to follow the right signed-in user after profile changes, sleep/suspend or switching devices; cached profile data must refresh when connection returns. These are platform certification conditions for Xbox titles, not GTA VI integration evidence or a general legal rule for Avocado.

Avocado cross-check: the existing [RockstarOS device-link flow](../rockstar-device-link.md) requires account-holder approval, owner-scoped API resolution, protected session storage and list/revoke. The Xbox materials are a useful QA analogy for showing source identity, requested data categories and purpose before a link, avoiding display-name-only binding, offering visible unlink/revoke, and rechecking the active owner after resume/device changes rather than carrying a stale session. Distinguish unlinking/stopping future access from deleting provider-side data already held, and show which party owns each request path. Do not copy Xbox identifiers, child-account policy or console certification requirements into Avocado. No account, session, data permission or integration changed.


## 138. 追加調査：Remote Play・platform cloud save・cross-platform portabilityは別の能力（2026-10-18 / RQ12、SIM/eSIM、state continuity）

The Japanese [PS5 Store listing](https://store.playstation.com/ja-jp/product/JP0230-PPSA29660_00-GTAVISTANDARD001) labels GTA VI “Remote Play supported”; the [Xbox Store listing](https://www.xbox.com/en-US/games/store/grand-theft-auto-vi/9nl3wwnzlzzn) lists “Xbox cloud saves.” Those are different platform features: one provides remote interaction with the console, the other indicates save storage in the Xbox cloud. The current product pages do not establish that GTA VI save state moves between PlayStation and Xbox, can be exported to a user-owned backup, or continues across platforms through a Rockstar account. Treat cross-platform save portability as unconfirmed, not as supported or impossible.

Microsoft's [Xbox state/roaming guidance](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/gaming/gdk/docs/store/policies/Console/console-certification-requirements-and-tests?view=gdk-2510) separately maps state to the users who created it and describes save roaming within Xbox consoles/devices/generations for the same TitleID; cross-platform Xbox-PC roaming depends on product configuration, and roaming to non-Microsoft platforms is recommended rather than a universal promise. This is Xbox title guidance, not evidence that GTA VI implements any specific transfer path.

Avocado candidate: label remote access, same-platform cloud sync, account-linked cross-device state, portable export/backup, and cross-platform migration separately. Show the authoritative identity, data included, last-sync/backup timestamp, conflicts, restore path and platform scope; never infer durable data recovery from a remote-control label or a cloud-save badge. Reuse existing owner/revision/save contracts and keep OS-device acceptance distinct from product cross-platform claims. No Avocado backup, sync or migration contract changed.


## 139. 追加調査：対応言語の「数」とUI・音声・字幕の内訳は別の表示項目（2026-10-18 / RQ14、catalog metadata）

The [Xbox GTA VI product page](https://www.xbox.com/en-US/games/store/grand-theft-auto-vi/9nl3wwnzlzzn) currently reports 13 supported languages. The [Rockstar Store product page](https://store.rockstargames.com/game/buy-gta-vi?drm=PlayStation), freshly reopened for this continuation, names English, French, Italian, German, Russian, Japanese, Polish, Korean, Simplified Chinese, Traditional Chinese, Spanish (Mexico), Spanish (Spain) and Portuguese (Brazil). However, neither retrieved product-page rendering exposes a per-language split between interface, spoken audio and subtitles, nor a region/package caveat. The Xbox page shows the count in its accessible page rendering; Rockstar's list supplies language names but labels all of them only “Supported Languages.” Treat those exact count/names as published listing data; do not infer full voice localization or platform-region parity from inclusion in the list. The product pages can change before release.

Microsoft's current [Xbox publishing metadata guide](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/gaming/game-publishing/tutorial-xbox-managed/how-to-add-metadata) tells publishers to set Interface, Voice and Subtitles independently for each language; where packages vary by Store region and initial download, the product page can disclose that language support may vary. This is Xbox publisher guidance, not confirmation of GTA VI's localization matrix.

Avocado candidate: keep locale claims componentized—UI, spoken output, subtitles/transcripts and documentation—per exact Tool/runtime/package version and market; show whether the claim is provider supplied or verified, whether an additional pack/download is required, and any region/initial-install limitation. A generic language count is a discovery hint, not proof that the full workflow can be used in that language. The current `sky-tool-package/1` contract has no localization matrix and rejects unknown package keys, so this is a design candidate only. No language claim, package schema or marketplace behavior changed.


## 140. 追加調査：Xbox presence・実績・single-playerはそれぞれ別の事実（2026-10-18 / RQ14、catalog capability claims）

The current [Xbox GTA VI listing](https://www.xbox.com/en-US/games/store/grand-theft-auto-vi/9NL3WWNZLZZN) labels the product a “single-player experience” and separately shows “Xbox achievements,” “Xbox presence” and “Xbox cloud saves.” Microsoft defines Basic Presence as indicating which game a player is playing; Rich Presence can also show the player's activity/location in a game. Xbox guidance says achievements are platform features for player actions, while its Multiplayer Activity requirement is separately for titles offering joinable multiplayer or co-op experiences. These Xbox pages describe platform support semantics; they do not establish that GTA VI has multiplayer, co-op or a Rockstar-linked online mode. The game's listed cloud-save capability likewise says nothing about cross-platform migration (see §138).

Avocado candidate: catalog Tool capability tags by what the user can actually do. Keep “owner is currently using this Tool,” progress/achievement receipt, cloud persistence, invite/joinable collaboration and cross-account sharing as distinct claims, with source and target platform/version. A platform “presence” or achievement badge must not be read as multi-user execution; a job status shared with its owner is not an invitation or collaboration endpoint. This is metadata reasoning only; no Avocado presence, social, achievement or multiplayer behavior changed.


## 141. 追加調査：GTA VI公式ミュージックハブのpreviewは選定例であり、完全な曲目表ではない（2026-10-18 / RQ14、catalog completeness, provenance）

The current [Rockstar music hub](https://www.rockstargames.com/VI/music) expressly calls its six radio cards “hand-picked” previews. The same page separately describes an official 34-track album, shows nine selected singles with external full-track destinations, and features a Justice original-score single. The landing labels the game’s wider radio and podcast offering as the largest/widest-ranging in the series, but the page does not provide a full radio roster or playlists; the album page does not say every album track is a radio track playable in-game. Accordingly, previewed examples, named songs, complete game audio inventory, dynamic score, standalone album, and authorized streaming destinations remain different evidence/rights categories. The public page is mutable promotional material.

Avocado candidate: every sample or demo should carry a clear scope label: selected preview, partial sample, complete catalogue, tested end-to-end run, or marketing example. Link sample items to the exact package/version and independently identify the complete inventory, launch/support date, source, off-platform destination and license/usage permission. Do not let an impressive preview imply exhaustive capability, completeness or authorization to download/remix/reuse. This deepens the evidence/provenance candidate in §107 and §132; no audio was copied and no catalog, data schema or media right changed.


## 142. 追加調査：公開範囲・account unlink・personal-data request・retentionは別のprivacy action（2026-10-18 / Security、RQ14、account lifecycle）

Rockstar's [Xbox-account linking instructions](https://support.rockstargames.com/articles/3pfU39dbg9x6VZG9diluJ9/linking-your-xbox-account-to-your-rockstar-games-account) tell the player to review what information will be public before continuing; after linking, the account page shows the linked Gamertag, privacy settings and unlink option. A separate [Rockstar support article](https://support.rockstargames.com/articles/72etb4nCNDS00Iy15iPxEc/how-to-review-the-personal-information-associated-with-my-rockstar-games-account) routes users who want to review account-associated personal information to account Privacy settings. Rockstar's [Japan privacy policy](https://www.rockstargames.com/jp/privacy?country=jp), updated March 2026, describes service-wide categories and management choices; it says retention may continue as needed to provide/administer services or meet legal and rights-related needs, and some information may be retained for a reasonable period after account inactivity/deletion requests for disputes, policy enforcement, operations, or technical/legal constraints before steps to delete/archive. These are Rockstar group/service-wide public documents, not a GTA VI-specific data inventory or proof that any particular title collects every listed category.

The Japan policy also says its disclosure supplements the privacy notices of software/platform manufacturers, and that collection varies by how a person uses a service. Its general gameplay-data section says gameplay information may be received automatically, may include platform/game-service and device identifiers, performance and usage statistics, may be collected even without registering for the services, and may be combined with other information. That is the group's stated general practice, not evidence that the unreleased GTA VI collects all those fields or operates without a platform account.

Avocado candidate: expose separate actions and consequences for (1) changing public profile visibility, (2) unlinking an identity provider / stopping future access, (3) inspecting or requesting a copy of account data, (4) deleting the account, and (5) deleting provider-held copies where that request is available. Also disclose per-execution-mode what is local, what is sent to Avocado/provider/platform, whether usage telemetry exists before provider account registration, and which sharing or marketing choices are optional. Name the responsible party and route, explain which actions are immediate vs. requests, and explain residual retention only from the applicable Avocado contract/policy and evidence. Do not imply that unlinking deletes data, that no account means no telemetry, or that a request form guarantees immediate erasure. This follows the existing account-owner approval/revoke boundary; no Avocado identity, privacy setting or account was changed.


## 143. 追加調査：account unlinkの可逆性とデータ影響は接続先platformごとに確認する（2026-10-18 / Security、AI06、identity recovery）

Rockstar's [current unlinking instructions](https://support.rockstargames.com/articles/tp2PvsKT8MKjraByTigca/unlinking-an-account-from-your-rockstar-games-account), last updated June 23, 2026, distinguish platform flows. Xbox network and PlayStation Network links can be unlinked/relinked in account settings, one linked account per platform at a time. Steam, Epic and the Xbox app for PC require Rockstar Support to unlink; PC eligibility depends on the Rockstar account's status. The same support page specifically warns that *existing GTA V and Red Dead Redemption 2 console* Story/Online progress is tied to Xbox Gamertag/PSN ID and will be lost when the player links a new account. That warning does not establish GTA VI save, account-link or migration behavior.

Avocado candidate: before disconnect/reconnect, show which party can perform it, whether it is self-serve or requires support, account-state eligibility, cooldown/relink limit if documented, which local/remote state or active jobs may be affected, and a verified recovery path. Require owner confirmation over the exact connection and consequence; do not silently switch linked identity or imply data transfer. Reuse current `rockstar-device-link.md` owner scope, list/revoke and protected-session boundaries; no provider link or Avocado data was changed.


## 144. 追加調査：Remote Playはconsole stream、cloud saveはstate roamingで別の条件を持つ（2026-10-18 / RQ12、SIM/eSIM, continuity）

Sony's [Remote Play guide](https://www.playstation.com/en-us/remote-play/) defines it as streaming compatible games from a PS4/PS5 to a compatible screen. It requires a PS console; for PlayStation Portal, a PS5, the same PlayStation account, an enabled/paired console connected to broadband, and at least 5 Mbps are listed, with 15 Mbps recommended. The Japanese GTA VI listing's “Remote Play supported” badge therefore indicates a console remote-session route subject to the game's compatibility; it is not by itself evidence of cloud-hosted execution or transferable save data. Sony also says haptics and adaptive triggers may vary or be unavailable on some Remote Play clients.

The current [U.S. PS Store listing](https://store.playstation.com/en-us/product/EP1004-PPSA01547_00-GTAVISTANDARD001) marks offline play enabled and Remote Play supported, but its legal description also says particular features/content/services can require internet, Rockstar account registration, a code, extra fees or gameplay unlocks, and may not be available for everyone or always. These statements can coexist when their scope is feature-specific. “Offline play” is not proof that first install, activation, account-bound offers, online services, cloud sync, updates or all content work offline.

Microsoft's current [XR-052 user-state/save guidance](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/xbox/gdk/docs/store/policies/xr/xr052?view=gdk-2604), last updated October 13, 2026, requires title state to remain associated with the users who created/selected/earned it and not be written for signed-out users. It tests user changes across suspension and roaming, requires save data to roam within Xbox platforms/generations and same-TitleID Windows PCs, but says roaming between Xbox and Windows is not required for non-XPA games, and non-Microsoft platforms are not required to roam. Microsoft's separate [Game Saves synchronization guide](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/gaming/gdk/docs/features/common/game-save/game-saves-syncing?view=gdk-2604) describes a data-ownership check when the same user is active on another device; offline play keeps local changes but does not sync them; if both local and cloud copies changed, the system prompts the user to choose, and the other device's progress is not in that copy. The related [system dialog guidance](https://learn.microsoft.com/pl-pl/xbox/gdk/docs/features/common/game-save/game-saves-dialogues?view=gdk-2604) states the conflict choices in terms of “this console” overwriting cloud data or “Xbox network (cloud data)” overwriting local data; if a user takes over a save lock, the other device's sync is canceled and that session's data is lost. It identifies cross-device play, offline/online use and title/platform bugs as possible conflict triggers. These are general Xbox platform/developer materials and do not assert GTA VI's specific save behavior. They support the caution in §138 that a storefront cloud-save label has limited platform/title scope.

Avocado candidate: name the execution path (local/native client, remote stream from a named device, hosted cloud execution, or hybrid); show the required source device/account/network and which display/input/haptic features survive at the remote endpoint. Separately state save/job owner, sync target, runtime compatibility, conflict handling and restore route. On a different-device resume, confirm active owner/device and reload the current revision; show unsynced work separately and never silently choose a stale local copy over a newer server record or vice versa. If moving an active session to another device stops or invalidates work on the first one, explain that consequence before taking over. When a choice discards one side, preview which changes will be kept and lost, and preserve an inspectable prior revision/restore path. Do not use a “remote,” “cloud” or “offline” badge without specifying whether it describes execution, interaction, license/activation, sync, updates or online service access. No Avocado remote session, sync or feature claim changed.


## 145. 追加調査：年齢区分・購入表示・任意性・地域別表示を別々のclaimにする（2026-10-18 / RQ14、catalog trust、SIM/eSIM）

The [ESRB's current U.S. GTA VI entry](https://www.esrb.org/ratings/41627/grand-theft-auto-vi/) assigns Mature 17+, lists seven content descriptors and the separate Interactive Element “In-Game Purchases.” PlayStation's [PS5 product page](https://www.playstation.com/en-us/games/grand-theft-auto-vi/) separately says “In-game purchases optional” and that country store age ratings are unconfirmed at the time of its page copy; its product/legal description warns that some features may require internet, account, codes, fees or unlocks. This is a real difference in issuer, market, field and page freshness, not necessarily a contradiction: an age/content rating answers a different question than whether purchase offers exist or whether they are necessary. ESRB describes Interactive Elements as distinct from age/content descriptors and defines “In-Game Purchases” broadly; that badge alone does not enumerate the items, total possible spend, renewal terms, optionality, or whether an offer uses random outcomes. The Japanese PlayStation Store listing currently renders the separate generic claim “ゲーム内課金あり” (“in-game purchases available”) without the U.S. page's optionality sentence; that difference is page/market field coverage, not evidence that a purchase is mandatory in Japan or that U.S. optionality generalizes worldwide. PlayStation's optionality statement applies to its surfaced product claim and should not be silently generalized to all editions, storefronts, future offers or post-launch changes. The cited pages are mutable and pre-release.

Avocado candidate: version product claims with issuer/source, jurisdiction, platform, product/package and observed date. Store age classification, content descriptors, optional paid features, recurring service price, metered Agent usage, external costs, random-item disclosure, and connectivity should be separate fields with their own evidence and currentness. If regional sources disagree or one is stale, display each scope and route users to current terms rather than flattening the discrepancy into a universal “safe,” “free” or “optional” badge. This research does not establish a GTA VI-specific purchase catalogue, randomized mechanics, or Avocado age/payment policy; no purchase, age gate, price or catalog schema changed.

The issuer's criteria also evolve independently of a title's build. PEGI's [official March criteria notice](https://pegi.info/news/pegi-expands-age-rating-criteria-interactive-risk-categories) says newly submitted games from June 2026 are assessed for functionality as well as content, including limited-quantity/time offers, paid random items, return-play mechanics, and communications. Its announcement also says publishers of online games must submit a privacy policy, community standards that explicitly prohibit illegal/harmful content and behavior, and reporting mechanisms for removal; entirely unrestricted communication without disable/mute features receives PEGI 18. Its [October update](https://pegi.info/index.php/news/pegi-updates-age-ratings-existing-games-paid-random-items) says that since October PEGI is retroactively updating a selection of existing digital-only PEGI 3/7/12 games containing paid random items to PEGI 16 across PEGI storefronts, and publishers must update product listings and future marketing materials; the update will take time to propagate. Microsoft's current [publisher rating guidance](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/gaming/game-publishing/concepts/age-ratings-guidance) independently describes the June criteria and requires PEGI Provisional to be replaced by the final long-form rating. This is evidence about PEGI's evolving system, not GTA VI: its historical PEGI entry remains secondary-reported and unavailable to verify here, and the October retroactive scope is not a rating decision for GTA VI. Avocado candidate: classification metadata needs both product/version and criteria-version/effective-date; detect issuer changes, expose stale copies per storefront, and preserve provisional/final/withdrawn states with provenance. For any later approved community/UGC service, bind submitted privacy policy, standards, report route and actual moderation/removal action as distinct evidence and operational states. Do not infer that GTA VI contains paid random items or any particular feature from the rule change.

One further claim-lifecycle check: PC Gamer reported on October 4 that an apparent PEGI 18 GTA VI record and detailed descriptors had been removed from PEGI's site; its report attributed the earlier discovery to GamesRadar. A fresh search of PEGI's public index here did not surface an accessible GTA VI record, and the exact search endpoint could not be retrieved through this research client, so this pass cannot verify the removed page's original contents or PEGI's reason for removal. Treat the content descriptors as secondary-reported/transient, not a currently verifiable official full-game decision; the live Australian entry observed in §150 remains explicitly a “Demonstration (Self Selected),” not a final retail-game rating. Avocado candidate: preserve source/version history and allow a classification claim to move to `withdrawn/unavailable` without silently treating its former value as current eligibility evidence.


## 146. 追加調査：family permission・feature exception・支出上限は異なるprincipal/action（2026-10-18 / Security、RQ14、consent model）

PlayStation's [Family app overview](https://www.playstation.com/en-us/ps-family-app/) separates child playtime requests, age-restricted game exceptions, Store spending limits and restricted communication-feature exceptions. Its support page explains that a child requests a game/communication exception and the family manager/guardian allows or denies it; Store purchases debit the family manager's wallet under a monthly child spending cap. These are platform family controls, not GTA VI functionality or an Avocado consent specification. Microsoft's current [Xbox title requirements](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/gaming/gdk/docs/store/policies/Console/certification-requirements?view=gdk-2604) require age/region/account-appropriate sign-in alternatives and consent for use of Microsoft-account information during publisher-account creation/linking. Its [XR-045 privilege requirements](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/gaming/gdk/docs/store/policies/xr/xr045?view=gdk-2604) treat multiplayer, cross-network play, communication, shared sessions, UGC and social-network sharing as distinct privilege checks; some child users may request a specific exception through system UI, while title code must continue checking the live privilege.

Avocado candidate: if a future approved product scope serves managed or delegated users, model requester identity, adult/family-manager authority, task-owner authorization, per-feature permission, account-link/data consent, and financial budget approval as separately scoped actors and decisions. A guardian's ability to enable one feature does not imply acceptance of provider privacy terms, permission to publish/share, or authorization to spend on a task; a platform permission bit or prior exception is not durable authority after session/user/settings change. Prefer a narrow feature exception to granting blanket access, recheck live provider privilege before sensitive actions, explain a supported alternative when the linked account/type/region cannot proceed, and collect no extra personal data merely to retry a failed age check. Applicable age-assurance and child-safety laws and Avocado's user scope require separate product/legal review; no child mode, family relation, identity, permission, spending rule, or account-link flow changed.


## 147. 追加調査：日本ストアのrating badge・legal copy・決済条件の更新時刻を別に追う（2026-10-18 / SIM/eSIM、regional catalog trust）

Directly reopened the Japanese [PlayStation Store GTA VI page](https://store.playstation.com/ja-jp/product/JP0230-PPSA29660_00-GTAVISTANDARD001) and, on a later refresh, the exact Japanese [Xbox Store GTA VI page](https://www.xbox.com/ja-JP/games/store/grand-theft-auto-vi/9NL3WWNZLZZN/). The current Xbox retrieval now renders 18+/CERO Z, Violence/Crime, a card-only checkout notice and current localized preorder copy. The older Xbox observation below is therefore stale and superseded as of this fresh check; page state can change between retrievals. The PS page similarly displays Crime/Violence icons and legal text identifying CERO Z/18+, with a card-only rule that charges full price regardless of wallet balance, but still carries a generic "CERO review pending" checkout sentence. CERO's system guide defines the formal "審査予定" mark as applying to pre-review promotional material; generic platform help copy is not itself the current classification decision. I still did not retrieve CERO's own GTA VI record in this pass. Keep exact page observation separate from issuer record and preserve superseded snapshots rather than calling them contemporaneous disagreement.

Avocado candidate: for age/payment disclosures, capture exact URL, store/region, product SKU, observed timestamp, visible badge, legal body, checkout rule and source authority separately; mark a stale or mixed page as a source-quality issue and re-check before using it in an eligibility decision. Do not transfer Japan's platform-specific credit-card/wallet rule to Avocado, infer the full game's content edit from category icons, or claim the page proves any Avocado customer age/checkout policy. No purchase, payment, age gate or product catalog changed.

Further checked the platform-wide Japanese checkout rules against these current product pages. Xbox's [CERO-rating guidance](https://www.xbox.com/ja-JP/games/gameratings) says the Xbox Store uses credit-card-only payment for age verification on CERO Z products; Microsoft's [Japan shopping guide](https://www.microsoft.com/ja-jp/store/b/shopping-guide) separately says Microsoft-account prepaid balance cannot buy CERO Z games and foreign-issued credit cards cannot be used in the Japanese Microsoft Store. The current PlayStation listing says a CERO Z purchase charges the full price to a credit card regardless of wallet balance. This is not one universal “age check”: the published seller rules differ in the purpose of card-only settlement, prepaid-balance eligibility and issuer region. Avocado candidate: represent age/eligibility evidence, account/store region, payment instrument eligibility, wallet-balance application and final capture as distinct checks and explain a failed checkout with the relevant responsible party. Keep the policy bound to issuer, territory, product state and observation time; do not transfer CERO or platform checkout mechanisms into Avocado's legal age, payment or Wallet policy. No account, payment or purchase was used.

## 154. 追加調査：同じ予約特典でもXboxとPlayStationでGTA+ redemption期限・条件の表現が異なる（2026-10-18 / SIM/eSIM、RQ15、promotion state）

The current Japanese [Xbox product listing](https://www.xbox.com/ja-JP/games/store/grand-theft-auto-vi/9NL3WWNZLZZN/) describes the one-month GTA+ offer as redeemable within 180 days after preorder, once the offer is available; it is once per platform account and unavailable to child accounts whose content restrictions block age-rated material. It also presents a Vintage Vice City pack, says purchases before November 19 at 23:59:59 receive the bonus, and labels the game single-player. The current Japan [PlayStation listing](https://store.playstation.com/ja-jp/product/JP0230-PPSA29660_00-GTAVISTANDARD001) gives an explicit redemption deadline of March 31, 2027, one per account, requires cancellation to stop renewal after the free month, and lists a November 19 campaign cutoff. Both pages show the Standard Edition at ¥9,800 and Ultimate Edition at ¥12,280, while the promotion detail and activation phrasing differ by storefront. Product times also appear as local dates/times: PlayStation renders the release as Nov. 18 15:00 UTC, corresponding to Nov. 19 local Japan time; the Xbox copy uses local Japan dates.

Avocado candidate: model one shared promotion as per-platform offer instances with eligibility, account/age restrictions, entitlement source, redemption readiness, relative versus fixed expiry, local cutoff timezone, one-use key, trial-start date, renewal price and cancellation state all explicit. Never merge "included with pre-order," "available to redeem," "redeemed," and "subscription active"; never infer that a recurring subscription has ended when a free month expires. These are current mutable platform listings, not Avocado promotion terms or a GTA VI entitlement verification. No checkout, account, offer redemption or subscription action was performed.

## 155. 追加調査：Rockstar Storeのゲーム・物販・アルバムは同一landing pageでもavailability単位が違う（2026-10-18 / SIM/eSIM、RQ14 catalog、RQ15）

The current [Rockstar Store landing page](https://store.rockstargames.com/) labels the GTA VI apparel/collectibles collection “New,” shows the Goodtime State collector box as “Coming Soon” at €399.99, marks some GTA VI apparel/collectible SKUs “Out of stock,” and has a GTA VI tote labelled “New.” The dedicated [GTA VI collection page](https://store.rockstargames.com/grand-theft-auto-vi-collection) instead renders product prices in USD and shows apparel/accessories as “New,” while the [white Macca the Gator tee detail](https://store.rockstargames.com/merchandise/buy-white-grand-theft-auto-vi-macca-the-gator-tee) marks every size out of stock but still displays a Buy Now/Add to Cart control. This demonstrates why collection label, individual option availability and an interactive purchase control are different facts; it does not prove those actions would complete. In the same apparent English/EUR homepage snapshot, the game is “Coming Soon” while reservation copy appears, and the album has a separate “Pre-Order Now” entry; on the dedicated collection page the album appears as several physical CD/vinyl SKUs with no visible stock badge. The page does not expose its precise shipping market in the extracted locale line, so EUR/USD difference is an observed page context difference, not proven geo localization or a pricing error. A “New” tag is not an in-stock/checkout-success signal, and a landing-page game state is not the same seller's current console preorder state.

Rockstar Support's [regional-shipping list](https://support.rockstargames.com/articles/4x9aYiCmLgCpnNATxhenu8/rockstar-store-regional-shipping-information), last updated October 7, 2025, lists Japan among supported shipping regions; it says shipping cost appears at checkout, delivery time depends on the selected shipping option, and local duties/taxes may apply outside the U.S. and U.K. The live tee detail advertises free shipping above $74.99 in its current USD rendering, but that is not a universal country/currency threshold. The Rockstar Store footer also links its Terms, Refunds and a contract-withdrawal route through its merchant provider.

Avocado candidate: represent a catalog item as a seller+SKU+market/locale+currency+fulfillment object, with independently sourced `announced/preorder/coming soon/in stock/out of stock/discontinued` state at SKU/variant level, observed time, item class (digital entitlement, size/color-dependent physical good, recorded media), inventory promise, price/tax/shipping estimates, import duties, checkout control state and merchant-owned cancellation/return support. An Add to Cart affordance can remain visible for an out-of-stock option, so recheck the selected variant and confirm merchant receipt before showing order/fulfillment. Do not collapse the game, limited physical box, ongoing merch inventory and album rights/delivery into one “GTA VI available” boolean. This snapshot provides no purchase or license proof; no Rockstar Store action, order, catalog schema or Avocado checkout was changed.

## 156. 追加調査：同じPS公式商品ページでCERO Zの法的表示と「未定」FAQが併存する（2026-10-18 / RQ14、classification provenance）

On today's direct Japanese [PlayStation GTA VI page](https://www.playstation.com/ja-jp/games/grand-theft-auto-vi/), the edition card and footer legal information identify CERO Z / 18+ and state card-only settlement rules. Yet the same URL's FAQ answer still says the game's rating is “currently undecided.” The separate PlayStation Store SKU likewise has Crime/Violence icons and Z legal terms alongside a generic pending-review checkout sentence. Rockstar's October 15 launch-trailer press release distributed through PR Times also retains “CERO: rating pending” in its product data block, while today's direct Xbox Japan listing shows 18+/Z. CERO's [official searchable title database](https://www.cero.biz/search/) is a separate authority surface and explains its rating marks, but I could not retrieve a GTA VI result from it through this search interface. These observations may reflect different update pathways or copied FAQ/press-kit text; they do not establish the legal issuer record, content edits, or whether all promotional copies have propagated.

Avocado candidate: separate authority decision/record, platform storefront rating image, age-gated checkout behavior, publisher press-kit copy, generic support FAQ and observed legal terms as distinct claim sources with field-level freshness and conflict flags. Use the issuing authority's current record for any eligibility decision; never let one still-visible “pending” snippet cancel a fresh store display, or let a store display substitute for the authority's underlying classification. No age gate or catalog policy changed.


## 148. 追加調査：PS5 Pro対応badgeはゲーム別feature実証の代替ではない（2026-10-18 / RQ14、catalog metadata、device capability）

The current [GTA VI PlayStation page](https://www.playstation.com/en-us/games/grand-theft-auto-vi/) marks both PS5 and “PS5 Pro Enhanced,” and separately lists DualSense vibration/trigger support; it does not give an exact GTA VI Pro rendering mode, resolution, frame-rate target or feature delta. Sony's [PS5 Pro overview post](https://blog.playstation.com/?p=396552) says Pro features are limited to selected games and vary game by game, and that some features require a compatible display. The newer [upgraded PSSR announcement](https://blog.playstation.com/2026/03/16/upgraded-pssr-rolling-out-to-silent-hill-f-monster-hunter-wilds-final-fantasy-vii-rebirth-crimson-desert-and-more/) says image and stability outcomes vary by supported title and can be disabled if unexpected effects occur. Other games' developer posts illustrate how to state exact modes and tradeoffs, but those numbers cannot be transferred to GTA VI. This supports a limited interpretation of the store badge: Pro enhancement is indicated, specific improvement is not quantified on this page.

Avocado candidate: report a device-level capability as a tuple of exact hardware/OS/runtime version, feature enabled/disabled state, user prerequisites and observed result; separate a compatibility badge from measured performance or guaranteed outcome. Where useful, show mode-specific tradeoffs, test conditions and user override, and state when only general hardware compatibility is known. This is a metadata/evidence candidate only; no GTA VI performance claim or Avocado device-support matrix was created or changed.


## 149. 追加調査：preorder cancellation・charge timing・refund requestを状態別にする（2026-10-18 / SIM/eSIM、RQ15、checkout recovery）

Microsoft's current [Microsoft Store pre-order guide](https://support.microsoft.com/en-us/accounts-billing/pre-orders-for-microsoft-store) says digital pre-order paid from Microsoft account balance is charged immediately, while other payment methods may be charged up to ten days before release; the user can change the payment method until billing. If collection fails, Microsoft says it will retry twice before release, then cancel the preorder if still unsuccessful. Preorders can be canceled online; if already billed before release, the cancellation is processed as a refund. The separate [cancellation support page](https://support.microsoft.com/en-us/accounts-billing/cancel-an-order-or-pre-order-from-microsoft-store) notes that a missing cancel control on an Xbox order can mean it was already charged/shipped, in which case the user must request an Xbox refund. These are general Microsoft Store/Xbox support flows, not a promise about the exact GTA VI charge date or refund eligibility.

This differs by state from the [PlayStation preorder cancellation guidance](https://www.playstation.com/ja-jp/support/store/cancel-ps-store-pre-order/) already compared in §63: cancellation timing can depend on both the pre-release window and whether main content has started downloading/streaming, and a preorder bonus may be revoked on cancellation. Therefore “cancel anytime until release” is not an adequate cross-platform claim without checking current channel-specific conditions.

Avocado candidate: make purchase authorization, pending order, amount reserved/charged, service entitlement issued, carrier activation, promotion redeemed, cancellation request, refund requested, refund confirmed, and entitlement/usage recovery separate states with timestamps, responsible party and next action. A timeout or pending response must not be reported as canceled/refunded. If payment can retry or a change-of-payment deadline exists, show the exact date and fallback; if access/use has already started, state the applicable policy before the user confirms rather than assuming a full refund. This is an operational analogy, not an Avocado contract or approval to change billing; no purchase, charge, refund, Wallet or service setting occurred.


## 150. 追加調査：classification recordは同じtitle文字列でもmedia・category・applicant・versionを照合する（2026-10-18 / RQ14、catalog trust）

Australia's official National Classification Database currently exposes separate records with similar GTA VI names. The [Rockstar/T2 record](https://www.classification.gov.au/titles/grand-theft-auto-vi-12) identifies a multi-platform computer-game “Demonstration (Self Selected),” dated August 27, 2026, with Rockstar Games as developer, Rockstar North as publisher and Take 2 Interactive Software Pty Ltd as applicant; it is R18+ for high-impact violence, sex scenes and drug use. The [GTA VI: An Extended Look record](https://www.classification.gov.au/titles/grand-theft-auto-vi-extended-look) is a 27-minute Netflix-classified film, submitted by Netflix, also R18+ but with its own film advice. The New Zealand Classification Office describes the similarly titled 26-minute work as a Netflix self-rated promotional film using actual game footage, not the game itself. Australian search also returns IARC tool entries whose title contains “Grand Theft Auto VI” but whose record metadata names unrelated applicant/production-company values; an exact-title search alone therefore surfaces non-equivalent records. The Department explains that IARC decisions arise from a developer/publisher questionnaire for a particular online/mobile game and that applying a classification to another format requires the same game/version (or exempt modifications). An official listing’s existence is not by itself proof that title, medium, submitter and version refer to the same artifact.

Avocado candidate: classify a submitted artifact by stable artifact/version identifier plus media type, platform, package or release channel, submitting publisher/authority, market and date. Search results may propose candidates but should not silently merge two same-name records; verify primary record fields and scope before displaying a rating or using it in access gating. A promotional film rating, a self-selected gameplay demonstration and the final purchasable game are separate evidence objects even when they depict or share the same title. This is evidence-quality guidance only, not a GTA VI content-access policy or Avocado age-gating decision; no catalog, schema, rating or eligibility control changed.


## 151. 追加調査：physical code-in-boxは配送物・download code・account region・redeemed entitlementを分ける（2026-10-18 / SIM/eSIM、RQ15、activation support）

Rockstar's current [platform/edition FAQ](https://support.rockstargames.com/articles/4QfG4FmZCf5W1gS8jy4UVT/grand-theft-auto-vi-platform-editions-and-versions) says the physical GTA VI version contains a download code and no disc, is expected to ship/be available for pickup from November 12 to support preload, and that the Standard-to-Ultimate digital upgrade is available only after the base download code has been redeemed. It ties PlayStation redemption eligibility to platform-account country groups and says Japanese PlayStation product codes expire 170 days after issuance on November 19; Xbox codes are stated as not region-locked. Rockstar's separate [preorder purchase FAQ](https://support.rockstargames.com/articles/40DHWEDBnq5jUuWJqKcH02/grand-theft-auto-vi-pre-order-purchase-support-faq) sends digital-storefront questions to Sony or Microsoft, Rockstar Store physical purchase/refund issues to Xsolla, and other-retailer issues back to that retailer. This shows a concrete chain of physical fulfillment → code delivery → platform redemption → regional/account validation → download/entitlement → eligible upgrade, with different support owners along the way; the offer is not literally an install disc.

Avocado candidate: where a SIM/eSIM or starter package is sold with cloud/service access, show separately what arrives physically, the activation secret/profile, account/region binding, service entitlement, carrier provisioning, first successful connection, and optional plan/Agent usage. Record which vendor owns each failure and where the user must go next. Do not say “active” merely because a parcel shipped, a code was delivered, or an entitlement was issued; verify the relevant step and surface region/expiry restrictions before purchase. GTA VI's physical package and code rules are title/platform-specific examples, not Avocado packaging, carrier, refund or service guarantees; no Avocado bundle, activation, storefront or support routing was changed.

The [Japanese PlayStation listing](https://store.playstation.com/ja-jp/product/JP0230-PPSA29660_00-GTAVISTANDARD001) shows a seven-day preload; Rockstar's FAQ says preload begins November 12 at local midnight for a November 19 launch and that redeemed codes start downloading then. Sony's general [preorder/download guide](https://www.playstation.com/ja-jp/support/games/pre-order-games-automatic-download-ps-store/) describes a release countdown and lets the account owner opt into or out of automatic downloads for the included content. This is a preparation stage with an account and content checklist, separate from a statement that a running service is ready. Avocado candidate: allow app/config/profile preparation to complete before an entitlement's service start when its approved contract supports staging, but show “installed/ready to activate” separately from “service active” and retain the owner, release/activation time and recovery route. This is a sequencing analogy only; no Avocado preload, activation schedule or entitlement behavior changed.


## 152. 追加調査：ゲーム予約の詐欺はtransaction intentと永続permissionを混同させる（2026-10-18 / Security、RQ15、Wallet consent）

Kaspersky's October 6 [GTA VI scam analysis](https://www.kaspersky.com/blog/gta-6-fake-leaks-malware-and-scams/56496/) reports several lures built around fake leaked builds, preorder keys, beta/early-access offers and a fake “human verification” which actually asks the user to install an unrelated AI chat application. The separate [Malwarebytes analysis of a fake leaked-copy site](https://www.malwarebytes.com/blog/scams/2026/09/fake-gta-6-leaked-copy-drains-your-crypto-wallet) says the page mixed accurate GTA VI facts with contradictory offer terms. In that specific observed site, simply connecting a wallet did not itself transfer funds, but the next approval could request a balance transfer or ongoing token/NFT permissions. These are vendor investigations of the sites they examined, not a claim that every fan or gaming site is dangerous.

The pattern is more than “check the logo”: a legitimate-looking context can route a user from a plausible claim into an unrelated binary install or a permission request far broader/longer-lived than the purchase they intended. Ethereum.org's [wallet-permission guide](https://ethereum.org/guides/how-to-revoke-token-access) and MetaMask's [approval guidance](https://support.metamask.io/more-web3/learn/how-to-revoke-smart-contract-allowances-token-approvals) distinguish disconnecting a site from revoking a token allowance; an already granted allowance can remain usable until revoked, and revocation itself is a new on-chain transaction rather than undoing transfers already made.

Avocado candidate: for any later-approved Wallet action, show the exact payee/provider, asset, amount, one-time transfer versus standing spend authority, applicable limit/duration and resulting state before approval; do not let “connect”, “verify account”, “start task” or a product price conceal a persistent authorization. Provide a separately named view for active authorities, and explain that revocation blocks future use but does not reverse an executed transfer. A requested install unrelated to the promised capability should be treated as an unexpected scope expansion. These are threat-informed review ideas only; no crypto transaction, wallet connection, allowlist, approval policy or game purchase was performed or authorized, and they do not establish any GTA VI/Avocado integration.


## 153. 追加調査：Take-Two特許や「AI」報道はGTA VIへの搭載証拠と分離する（2026-10-18 / Rockstar source claims、LLM boundary）

Take-Two's [2019-filed, 2020-published virtual-navigation patent](https://patents.google.com/patent/US20200338450A1/en) describes a coarse graph over lower-level game-world nodes to reduce node loads during pathfinding; its inventor/assignee data links it to Take-Two, but the document does not name GTA VI or establish a shipped feature. Recent online summaries describing this as proof of GTA VI's AI NPCs or technical performance add a game-specific inference beyond the claim text. Separately, Take-Two CEO Strauss Zelnick's February 2026 [GamesIndustry.biz interview as reported by MeriStation](https://en.as.com/meristation/news/strauss-zelnick-ceo-of-take-two-on-gta-6-generative-ai-has-zero-part-in-what-rockstar-games-is-building-f202602-n/) distinguishes the company's long use of machine learning/AI from his statement that generative AI had no part in what Rockstar was building for GTA VI, and describes its world as handcrafted. This is a reported executive statement at that date, not a technical audit of every build, production tool, AI definition, or future service. The sources can coexist: ordinary pathfinding/behavioral AI, a patent disclosure, machine-learning tools, and generative AI are not synonyms.

Avocado candidate: label AI capabilities by exact system/type, task, model/tool version, evidence source and observed environment; keep patent, announcement, report, demo, implementation and accepted runtime as distinct evidence states. Avoid “AI-powered” as a catch-all that turns a patent into a shipping guarantee or treats an LLM planner as an authority. This aligns with [`docs/llm-evaluation-architecture.md`](llm-evaluation-architecture.md) and `data/llm-capabilities.json`, where untrusted plans stay separate from Broker authority. No Rockstar feature was accepted, no model/runtime or product claim changed, and this research provides no game integration or reuse rights.

## 158. 追加調査：公式creator platform支援・第三者RP運営・GTA VI対応は別のclaim（2026-10-18 / RQ14、creator ecosystem、trust/support）

Rockstar's [September 1 announcement](https://www.rockstargames.com/newswire/article/17857581o753k1/introducing-nopixel-v?pubDate=20260901) describes nopixel V as an evolution of the **GTAV Roleplay** experience and says Rockstar is supporting the NoPixel community; its invited closed beta launches through Rockstar Games Launcher. Rockstar's current [RP-server support article](https://support.rockstargames.com/articles/5I66kExWgligszgMCU3XC1/roleplay-rp-servers) points operators to the Creator Platform License Agreement and mod guidelines. The [Creator PLA, updated September 10, 2026](https://fivem.net/terms), grants a limited, revocable license only for games authorized as Covered Games; this document and the announcement do not establish that GTA VI is a covered game or that NoPixel V/FiveM has a GTA VI implementation. NoPixel's own live [rulebook](https://www.nopixel.net/rules) places player reports and bug reports in the NoPixel V Hub or in-game pause-menu commands, and imposes its own 18+ membership rule; those are operator rules, not a GTA VI classification or Rockstar-wide age rule.

The Creator PLA also draws concrete ecosystem boundaries: creator content and custom servers are not automatically “official” or Rockstar-endorsed; user-facing custom-server pages must identify the operator and carry a disclaimer. It restricts commercial methods such as cash-out, real-money or in-game-currency chance sales, sale of Rockstar-created virtual items, branded/sponsored servers and selling third-party creator content through an aggregated storefront, absent separate express written terms. Marketplace content is licensed for use inside a corresponding custom server, has creator-specific support versus platform transaction support, and can have different continuity depending on a one-time versus subscription license; Rockstar can alter/cease marketplace availability.
